On a benchmark of 50 motions spanning single-object and two-object interactions, FlashDexRetarget achieves a 90% success rate, approximately 2.5x that of the evaluated sampling-based baselines, while requiring up to 100x less training compute than the evaluated RL-based baselines. Evaluations on both XHand and Sharpa Wave Hand show consistent gains, and component-wise ablations examine the contributions of our design choices. Beyond the 50-motion benchmark, experiments with 200, 500, and 1,000 motions demonstrate that our method remains stable at larger scales and produces successful retargeted motions more efficiently as the training set grows. Qualitative replay results using real-world-captured demonstrations further illustrate the applicability of our framework to recorded human manipulation. Videos and code are available at https://davian-robotics.github.io/FlashDexRetarget/

</details>


### [70] [ChunkVLA-AM: Parallel Action Chunking for Vision-Language-Action Robot Control in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01856)
*Zhugang Liu,Kaichuang Zhang,Jinman Zhang,Pu Sun,Martha Asare,Jose Hernandez,Maxim Ermolinsky,Efren Saenz,Qi Lu,Jinghao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将 OpenVLA-OFT 部署于 FAIRINO FR3 机械臂增材制造工位的框架，采用云边架构实现真实世界演示数据到 TFDS/RLDS 的转换，A-to-B 物体转移任务成功率达 92.9%，并给出光照鲁棒性区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽为增材制造自动化带来机遇，但适配未见过的机器人本体成本高，且环境变化下性能易退化，实际部署仍困难。

Method: 构建数据流水线，将单目真实演示转换为 OpenVLA 兼容的 TFDS/RLDS 数据集以适配 FR3 本体；运行时每次推理输出 8 步 7 维动作块，FR3 开环执行该动作块后再采集新观测，实现块间闭环反馈；系统采用云边架构，FR3 客户端经 FastAPI 接口向远程推理服务器流式传输观测。

Result: 42 次 A-to-B 物移试验（红蓝目标各半）中成功 39 次，成功率 92.9%；3 次失败均发生于最终放置阶段，因释放高度控制不足导致物体翻倒。光照扫描显示 0-255 尺度下低误差亮度区间为 85-125，平均空间误差最低出现在 95。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了 OpenVLA-OFT 在固定增材制造工位上跨本体部署的可行性，块间闭环与云边推理可支撑高成功率操作；后续需改进释放高度控制并利用已识别的亮度窗口提升放置稳定性与光照鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models unify visual perception, language understanding, and action generation, offering new opportunities for automation in additive manufacturing (AM). However, deployment in AM remains challenging because adapting these models to unseen robot embodiments is costly, and performance can degrade under environment changes. In this work, we present a framework for deploying OpenVLA-OFT on a FAIRINO FR3 robot in a fixed AM workcell. A data pipeline converts monocular real-world demonstrations into OpenVLA-compatible TFDS/RLDS datasets to support adaptation to the FR3 embodiment. At runtime, each inference request predicts an eight-step chunk of 7-D actions. The FR3 executes each chunk open loop before capturing a new observation, providing closed-loop feedback between chunks. The system uses a cloud-edge architecture in which the FR3 client streams observations to a remote inference server through a FastAPI interface. In 42 physical A-to-B object-transfer trials, evenly split between red and blue targets, the system succeeded in 39 (92.9%). All three failures occurred during final placement, when insufficient release-height control caused the object to topple. An illumination sweep identified a low-error luminance range of 85-125 on a 0-255 scale, with the lowest mean spatial error at 95.

</details>


### [71] [Towards Physical Underwater Robotic Assistance for Scuba Diver Movement in Confined Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01906)
*Demetrious T. Kutzke,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个可穿戴推进器导向潜水面罩式机器人RADMCS，通过单目深度估计与推进器力反馈触觉提示，帮助潜水员在受限空间保持与海底结构的安全距离，并经8名潜水员的IRB水下实验验证低推力（10%最大值）即可实现人体运动导向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜水员被训练控制深度以避免快速升降引发气体栓塞与气压伤，但珊瑚礁、水下钻探仪器、未爆物等任务要求横向距离控制。现有推进助力的外骨骼方案解决的是推进辅助，而非方向引导，缺少面向受限空间横向距离保持的可穿戴机器人方案。

Method: 提出RADMCS（受限空间机器人辅助潜水员运动），一种可穿戴机器人：利用单目深度估计感知潜水员与海底结构的距离，并通过潜水推进器的力反馈产生触觉提示，实现推进器驱动的方向引导；采用小型紧凑外形，作为可扩展至更复杂控制与导航行为的基础平台。开展IRB批准的水下人体研究，8名潜水员参与。

Result: 实验涵盖闭水泳池与海洋环境的阈值敏感性测试、闭水环境中的距离保持实验，以及海洋中的形态、适配与功能测试。结果证明仅需较低推力（最大值的10%）即可通过机器人引导的物理触觉感知实现对人体运动方向的引导。

Conclusion: 可穿戴推进触觉引导机器人可在低推力下有效引导潜水员运动，验证了用于受限空间横向距离保持的可行性，其紧凑平台为后续更复杂的控制与导航功能扩展奠定基础。

Abstract: Scuba divers are taught to control their depth to avoid rapid ascents and descents, which could result in serious injuries such as gas embolisms and barotrauma. However, many underwater tasks necessitate lateral control, maintaining distance between subsea structures such as coral reefs, submerged drilling instrumentation, or unexploded ordnance. In this work, we discuss a first-of-its-kind wearable robotic solution providing thruster-actuated directional guidance to a diver, as distinct from prior propulsive-assistance exoskeletons. We introduce ``Robotic Assisted Diver Movement in Confined Spaces'' (RADMCS), a wearable robot that assists divers in maintaining a fixed distance from subsea structures by leveraging perception techniques in monocular depth estimation and force-feedback from submersible thrusters to provide haptic feedback. Its small and compact form factor creates a foundational platform that could be expanded to include more sophisticated control and navigation behaviors. We present results from Institutional Review Board (IRB) in-water studies with eight human scuba diver participants on threshold sensitivity tests in both a closed-water swimming facility and ocean environments; distance-maintaining experiments in a closed-water facility; and form, fit, and function testing in the ocean. We demonstrate that relatively low thrust values (10 percent of maximum) allow robotic direction of a human's movement using the physical sensation of the robot's guidance.

</details>


### [72] [Robot Learning on Discrete Surfaces: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01910)
*Matteo Dalle Vedove,Fares J. Abu-Dakka,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出统一的离散黎曼框架，使机器人学习可直接在多面体表面网格上进行，并在DMP、GP、RFM三种学习范式中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习与运动生成框架通常将表面视为约束，忽略其内在几何；对于CAD与3D重建标准输出的多面体网格，其离散几何结构未被充分利用。

Method: 基于离散微分几何，定义跨面、边、顶点均良定义的对数/指数映射、平行传输与环境空间投影；并将其实例化于三种范式：(i)DMP采用改进指数映射计算与固定切锥强迫项编码加平行传输；(ii)GP使用带实用性容许控制的测地核；(iii)RFM使用网格原生算子。

Result: DMP在跨表面泛化与稳定性上优于既有基于网格的方法；GP无需光滑性假设即可在任意网格位置回归；RFM生成质量优于谱基线且减少训练时间。仿真对比SOTA方法，并在两个真实机器人场景验证：跨表面泛化手绘轨迹与RGB-D重建表面的抛光运动规划。

Conclusion: 该离散黎曼框架将表面内在几何纳入机器人学习，统一支持多种学习范式，并在仿真与真实机器人任务中展现有效性与实用性。

Abstract: All the objects composing our world are enclosed within surfaces. Yet, most robot learning and motion generation frameworks treat surfaces as constraints ignoring their intrinsic geometry. This gap is acute for polyhedral meshes--the standard output of CAD and 3D reconstruction--whose discrete geometric structure remains unexploited. In this paper, we propose a unified discrete Riemannian framework that enables robot learning directly on polyhedral surface meshes. Using discrete differential geometry, we define logarithmic and exponential maps, parallel transport, and ambient-space projections that remain well-defined across faces, edges, and vertices. We instantiate the framework in three learning paradigms: (i) Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs), an improved exponential-map computation and a fixed-tangent-cone forcing-term encoding with parallel transport yield better cross-surface generalisation and stability over prior mesh-based approaches. (ii) Gaussian Process (GP), a geodesic-based kernel with practical admissibility control, enables regression at arbitrary mesh locations without smoothness assumptions. (iii) Riemannian Flow Matching (RFM), mesh-native operators improve generative quality over spectral baselines while reducing training time. The framework is validated in simulation against state-of-the-art methods and demonstrated on two real-robot scenarios: generalising user-drawn trajectories across different surfaces and planning polishing motions on RGB-D-reconstructed surfaces.

</details>


### [73] [TouchTherm: Building Multimodal Digital Twins of Objects for Tactile and Thermal Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01943)
*Yitao Zhang,Hong Ying,Haoran Guo,Xiaoying Zhou,Guanyu Chen,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchTherm：构建视觉-触觉-热学多模态仿真就绪物体资产的框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真与VR需要同时具备视觉几何、触觉与热学物理线索的物体资产，但现有数据集与重建方法主要描述物体尺度几何与外观，忽略微观表面结构（用于高保真触觉渲染）与瞬态温度动态（用于温度感知交互）。

Method: 视觉与触觉重建：结构光扫描获取几何 + 光度立体获取多视角法线图；法线图配准到扫描几何并转换到切空间，恢复局部微观高度场用于光学触觉渲染，粗网格用于碰撞检测。热学重建：对受控加热后的自然冷却过程采集同步多视角红外视频，重建物理正则化的动态温度场。

Result: 20个物体实验：重建的微观高度场保留主要表面结构并恢复超出粗几何的高频细节；温度场在30s与45s的留出表面温度MAE分别为0.465°C与0.592°C。所得触觉资产支持从触觉观测进行合成到真实的物体识别，基于手套的VR系统展示时空变化的温度反馈。

Conclusion: TouchTherm在多模态感知仿真与温度感知虚拟交互方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Robotic simulation and virtual reality increasingly require object assets that capture not only visual geometry but also the physical cues underlying tactile and thermal interaction. Existing 3D datasets and reconstruction methods primarily represent object-scale geometry and visual appearance, overlooking microscale surface structure for high-fidelity haptic rendering and transient temperature dynamics for temperature-aware interaction. We present TouchTherm, a framework for constructing simulation-ready visuo-tactile-thermal object assets from real-world objects. For visual and tactile reconstruction, we combine structured-light scanning with multiview normal maps obtained from photometric stereo. The normal maps are registered to the scanned geometry and transformed into tangent space to recover local micro-height fields for optical tactile rendering, while the coarse mesh handles collision detection. For thermal reconstruction, we capture synchronized multiview infrared videos of natural cooling following controlled heating and reconstruct a physics-regularized dynamic thermal field. Experiments on 20 objects show that the reconstructed micro-height fields preserve dominant surface structures and recover higher-frequency details beyond the coarse geometry, while the thermal fields achieve held-out surface-temperature MAEs of 0.465 degrees C and 0.592 degrees C at 30 s and 45 s, respectively. The resulting tactile assets support synthetic-to-real object recognition from tactile observations, while a glove-based VR system demonstrates spatially and temporally varying thermal feedback. These results highlight the potential of TouchTherm for multimodal sensory simulation and temperature-aware virtual interaction.

</details>


### [74] [Training-Free Diffusion Planning with Analytical Local Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01959)
*Michael Y. Fatemi,Jinhao Liang,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的扩散式运动规划器，用解析局部分数替代学习到的全局轨迹分数，在复杂环境与多智能体任务中实现快速、平滑、无碰撞的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的规划器需在大量可行轨迹上训练，导致其依赖特定地图、难以在缺乏高质量示范数据时部署。作者希望摆脱数据依赖，同时保留扩散模型的迭代去噪精修能力。

Method: 用解析局部分数代替学习到的全局轨迹分数，分数由障碍、平滑度、速度与智能体间可行性四项构成；核心观察是轨迹分数可仅通过相邻航点与邻近约束的局部交互重构。该结构利用带来分解式去噪过程，既保留经典轨迹方法的优化结构，又继承扩散模型的迭代精修特性，且无需训练。

Result: 在大量复杂环境与大规模多智能体规划任务中，解析分数能在有限算力下产生平滑且可行的轨迹；例如在含100+障碍物的环境中为300+智能体生成可行路径，GPU上耗时不足6秒，优于强学习类与优化类基线，且无需学习式扩散规划器的数据要求。

Conclusion: 局部解析分数可有效重构轨迹分数，使扩散式规划无需训练数据即可部署，在复杂与大规模多智能体场景下兼顾轨迹质量与计算效率，为免训练扩散规划提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Path finding and multi-robot motion planning require trajectories that are smooth, goal-directed, and collision-free in environments with complex geometric constraints. Recent diffusion-based planners have shown that trajectory generation can be cast as iterative denoising which has opened the doors to learning-based approaches that can handle multi-modal trajectory distributions and refine entire trajectories. However, a key limitation is that diffusion planners require training on large collections of feasible trajectories, rendering them map-specific, and difficult to deploy when high-quality demonstrations are unavailable. This paper introduces a training-free diffusion-based motion planner that replaces learned global trajectory scores with analytical local scores derived from obstacle, smoothness, velocity, and inter-agent feasibility terms. The proposed idea relies on a key observation: the score of a trajectory can be reconstructed by considering only local interactions between neighboring waypoints and nearby constraints. This structure exploitation yields a decomposed denoising procedure that retains the optimization structure of classical trajectory methods while inheriting the iterative refinement behavior of diffusion models. Experiments on a large collection of complex environments and large multi-agent planning tasks show that the proposed analytical score produces smooth and feasible trajectories within limited computational costs, for example in generating feasible paths for 300+ agents in environments containing 100+ obstacles in under 6 seconds on a GPU, outperforming strong learning-based and optimization baselines, while avoiding the data requirements of learned diffusion planners.

</details>


### [75] [BLT*: Informed Belief Localization Trees for Uncertainty-Aware Planning on Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01972)
*Elliot Preston-Krebs,Abhishek Goudar,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Informed BLT*，一种采样型信念空间规划（BSP）算法，适配 RRT* 与 Informed RRT*，基于 2-Wasserstein（W₂）度量，在点云观测下可扩展至大型室外数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型室外数字孪生中，基于点云观测的信念空间规划面临观测传播开销大、计算难以扩展的问题，需要在考虑不确定性（信念）与概率碰撞约束的同时高效复用已有的测量信息。

Method: 将 RRT* 与 Informed RRT* 扩展到信念空间，采用 W₂ 度量；假设各向同性高斯信念，使采样信念状态在兼顾已有信息与概率碰撞约束下可高效连接，支持转向（steering）与重连（rewiring）而无需重复传播观测，并复用先前计算的测量信息；同时提出为真实环境生成语义标注数字孪生的框架，用于基于点云的定位规划。

Result: 在仿真环境与数字孪生中的实验表明，多数地图上初始解发现速度更快，且代价收敛性具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Informed BLT* 通过 W₂ 度量与高斯信念假设实现观测信息的复用，提升了大规模点云数字孪生场景下信念空间规划的求解效率，同时保持有竞争力的代价收敛。

Abstract: We present Informed Belief Localization Trees* (Informed BLT*), a sampling-based belief space planning (BSP) algorithm that scales to large outdoor digital twins with point-cloud observations. We adapt RRT* and Informed RRT* to belief space using the $2$-Wasserstein ($W_2$) metric. Assuming isotropic Gaussian beliefs, sampled belief states can be connected efficiently while accounting for available information and probabilistic collision constraints. This enables steering and rewiring without repeatedly propagating observations, and allows previously computed measurement information to be reused. We present a framework to generate semantically labelled digital twins for planning in real-world environments with point-cloud-based localization. Experiments in simulated environments and digital twins show faster initial solution discovery in most maps with competitive cost convergence.

</details>


### [76] [H-SPAR: Hydrodynamic-aware Simulation for Particle Transport and Autonomous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01985)
*Navid Zarrabi,Nariman Yousefi,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-SPAR 是一个统一的海洋机器人采样仿真框架，将时空变化流场、拉格朗日粒子输运、概率采样与 ROS 2/Gazebo USV 自主性集成，实现规划-执行-采样的一致评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境机器人采样需同时考虑水流对机器人运动与粒子输运的双重影响，但现有海洋机器人仿真器通常将流场、自主性与采样目标分开建模，难以联合评估任务成本与采样性能。

Method: H-SPAR 集成时空变化速度场、拉格朗日粒子输运、概率采样以及基于 ROS 2/Gazebo 的无人水面艇（USV）自主系统；采用共享的预计算流场，同时驱动粒子平流与闭环车辆执行过程中的水流诱导力；并开展路径规划与覆盖采样实验，对比 SVF-RRT* 与传统 RRT*。

Result: 规划层面，水流感知规划器 SVF-RRT* 相比传统 RRT* 上游成本降低 69.4%；但在时变水流下的实际执行中，该降幅降至 41.7%，反映了规划阶段未考虑的时间流变化、车辆运动约束与路径偏差。覆盖实验中，在完整 H-SPAR 配置下，扫掠取向可使粒子采样率变化达 22.2%。

Conclusion: 研究强调应在一致的水动力条件下，联合评估规划、车辆执行、粒子输运与采样过程；仿真框架与开源代码已公开。

Abstract: Environmental robotic sampling requires considering the dual influence of water currents on robotic motion and particle transport. Existing marine robotics simulators generally model flow, autonomy, and sampling targets separately, limiting joint evaluation of mission cost and sampling performance. H-SPAR integrates spatially and temporally varying velocity fields, Lagrangian particle transport, probabilistic sampling, and ROS 2/Gazebo-based uncrewed surface vehicle (USV) autonomy. In this work, shared precomputed flow fields drive particle advection and current-induced forces during closed-loop vehicle execution. Path-planning experiments show that the existing current-aware planner SVF-RRT* achieves 69.4% lower upstream cost than conventional RRT* at the planning level, but this reduction falls to 41.7% during execution under time-varying currents, reflecting temporal flow variation, vehicle motion constraints, and path deviation omitted during planning. Coverage experiments show that sweep orientation changes the particle-sampling rate by up to 22.2% under the complete H-SPAR configuration. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating planning, vehicle execution, particle transport, and sampling together under consistent hydrodynamic conditions. The project webpage is available at https://sites.google.com/view/h-spar, and the open-source code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/naviiidz/h-spar-sim.

</details>


### [77] [UniWAM: Unified World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02054)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Jingbo Wang,Shuai Zhou,Xicheng Gong,Zehua Fan,Ziyang Zhou,Junwu E,Haodong Yan,Fuhao Li,Qize Yu,Xu Huang,Pengwei Wang,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniWAM 提出统一架构，融合物理推理器、世界生成器与动作预测器，联合学习语义理解、视觉生成与动作预测，在多项评测中达到 SOTA，并揭示人机协同训练的 log-linear 缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型虽受益于预训练视觉-语言模型的理解与推理能力，但仅靠动作监督难以充分建模世界动态；世界-动作模型虽继承视频生成模型的时空先验，却在语义理解与分布偏移下的推理方面受限。作者希望将两者优势统一起来。

Method: 构建统一架构 UniWAM，集成物理推理器、世界生成器与动作预测器；设计严格的数据清洗与标注流水线（涵盖人类第一视角数据与机器人数据）；用自然语言表示低级动作以适配具身任务同时保留语言能力；提出预训练配方，将 VQA 数据、人类第一视角数据与机器人演示的互补监督分配给相应组件；后训练阶段采用未来视觉噪声增强与历史条件流匹配。

Result: 在分布内性能、鲁棒性、泛化性、指令跟随与长时程任务执行等多项评测中达到 SOTA；相关设计在保持性能的同时显著减少去噪步数；发现统一人机协同训练的 log-linear 缩放律，证明大规模混合人机数据预训练的有效性。

Conclusion: UniWAM 通过统一建模语义理解、视觉生成与动作预测，结合精心设计的预训练与后训练策略，验证了人机协同大规模预训练的可扩展性，为具身智能统一模型提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models benefit from the understanding and reasoning capabilities of pretrained vision-language models, but action-only supervision provides limited grounding in world dynamics. Conversely, world-action models inherit spatiotemporal priors from video generation models, yet remain limited in semantic understanding and reasoning under distribution shifts. We introduce UniWAM, a unified architecture that integrates a physical reasoner, a world generator, and an action predictor to jointly learn semantic understanding of the physical world, visual generation, and action prediction. To ensure the quality of the training data, we developed a rigorous data cleaning and annotation pipeline for both human egocentric data and robot data. To adapt the vision-language component to embodied tasks while preserving its inherited language capabilities, we represent low-level actions in natural language and introduce a pre-training recipe that assigns complementary supervision from visual question answering (VQA) data, human egocentric data, and robot demonstrations to the appropriate model components. During post-training, future visual noise augmentation reduces reliance on precise future predictions, while history-conditioned flow matching uses encoded action history to initialize action generation. Together, these designs significantly reduce denoising steps while maintaining performance. UniWAM achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across multiple evaluations, including in-distribution performance, robustness, generalization, instruction following, and long-horizon task execution. Furthermore, we uncover a log-linear scaling law of unified human-robot co-training, demonstrating the effectiveness of large-scale pre-training on a mixture of human and robot data.

</details>


### [78] [HumanoidToolBench: Benchmarking Humanoid Tool Use from Selection to Mobile Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02089)
*Kyochul Jang,Seohyeon Park,Ohchul Kwon,Sangjun Park,Junhyeok Choi,Seungyeop Yi,Chaeyun Kim,Sangkyu Lee,Idan Szpektor,Avi Caciularu,Jongmin Park,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidToolBench：面向人形机器人工具使用的18任务基准，覆盖3场景、3执行层级和2种工具集模式，并配套ToolBook数据集（3.1k演示，仿真+真实Unitree G1）。评测7个仿真策略与3个真机策略，揭示"选对工具"与"完成任务"之间存在显著差距；GR00T N1.7探针实验显示对未见工具选择准确率下降，且在无关指令下仍继续执行任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人硬件与学习方法进步，人形机器人需要借助工具突破自身物理限制。成功使用工具要求选择合适工具，并协调操作与（必要时）移动来完成的任务。然而现有基准未能在人形机器人上联合评估这些能力。

Method: 构建HumanoidToolBench：包含18个任务，覆盖3种场景、3个执行层级（execution levels）与2种工具集模式（tool-set modes）；同时发布ToolBook数据集，包含3.1k条演示，分别在仿真环境中及真实Unitree G1上采集。基于该基准评测7个仿真策略与3个真机策略，并对GR00T N1.7进行针对性探针实验。

Result: 评测显示，策略在"选择合适的工具"与"完成任务"之间存在显著性能差距。GR00T N1.7探针实验表明：面对未见工具时工具选择准确率下降；在无关指令下仍会继续执行任务。

Conclusion: 现有策略在人形机器人工具使用上能力不足，尤其在未知工具泛化与指令遵循方面存在明显缺陷；HumanoidToolBench与ToolBook为联合评估工具选择、操作与移动协调能力提供了统一平台。

Abstract: As robotic hardware and learning methods advance, humanoids need tools to perform tasks beyond their inherent physical limits. Successful tool use requires selecting a suitable tool and coordinating manipulation and, when needed, locomotion to complete the task. Existing benchmarks do not jointly evaluate these capabilities on a humanoid. We introduce HumanoidToolBench, an 18-task benchmark spanning three scenarios, three execution levels, and two tool-set modes, together with ToolBook, a dataset of 3.1k demonstrations collected in simulation and on a real Unitree G1. Evaluation of seven policies in simulation and three on the real robot reveals substantial gaps between selecting a suitable tool and completing the task. Focused GR00T N1.7 probes show reduced selection accuracy on unseen tools and continued task execution under unrelated instructions. Code and data are available at https://snu-pi.github.io/HumanoidToolBench/.

</details>


### [79] [GlassGuard: Verified Glass Plane Mapping for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02110)
*Hanwen Guo,Zhengzhi Lin,Yusen Xie,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GlassGuard：融合视觉与 LiDAR 证据重建建筑平面玻璃的导航导向框架，兼顾玻璃覆盖率与自由空间污染两个指标，在九个楼宇级场景中实现 82%–85% 玻璃覆盖率，并将单帧虚假体素降低 5–17 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 激光可能穿透玻璃，导致碰撞边界在地图中缺失，威胁 SLAM 与导航安全。已有重建方法虽尝试补全缺失表面，但障碍物定位不准会污染可通行自由空间，造成反向失效，因此需要同时满足"覆盖玻璃"与"不污染自由空间"的双重要求。

Method: (1) 由基础视觉模型提供玻璃实例掩膜；(2) 利用结构性 3D 线索生成度量平面假设；(3) 采用无深度的 2D 投影几何校验平面朝向；(4) 通过提案验证后合并进全局地图。整体以玻璃覆盖率与自由空间污染率为准则贯穿提案验证与全局建图。

Result: 在九个楼宇级场景、涵盖多种玻璃结构、空间尺度与光照条件下评估，累计超过 1 小时、2.1 km 真实机器人行驶。全景版本玻璃总覆盖率达 85%；相同针孔输入下 GlassGuard 达 82%，而基线最高仅 61%，同时每帧虚假体素减少 5–17 倍。结合导航规划器的定性示例显示，重建平面能阻挡穿玻璃路径并保留可通行路线。

Conclusion: GlassGuard 表明，将玻璃覆盖率与自由空间污染作为联合优化目标，可有效重建建筑平面玻璃并提升 LiDAR 导航安全性；多源证据（视觉掩膜＋3D 结构线索＋2D 投影几何）协同验证是关键，且方法在多样场景与真实机器人平台上具备实用性。

Abstract: Transparent and specular surfaces pose a serious challenge to LiDAR-based SLAM and navigation because laser returns may pass through glass, leaving collision boundaries absent from the map. Prior work attempts to reconstruct the missing surfaces, but inaccurate obstacle placement can create the opposite failure: contamination of traversable free space. Recognizing this dual requirement, we present GlassGuard, a navigation-oriented framework for reconstructing planar architectural glass from complementary visual and LiDAR evidence. We formulate success in terms of both glass coverage and free-space contamination and apply this principle throughout proposal verification and global map construction. A foundation vision model provides glass-instance masks, structural 3D cues generate metric plane hypotheses, and depth-free 2D projective geometry checks their orientations before they enter a consolidated global map. We evaluate GlassGuard in nine building-scale scenes spanning diverse glass structures, spatial scales, and lighting conditions, with more than one hour and 2.1 km of real-world robot traversal. GlassGuard achieves 85% of total glass coverage for its panoramic version. Under identical pinhole inputs, GlassGuard achieves 82% total coverage, compared with at most 61% for the evaluated baselines, while producing 5-17x fewer false voxels per frame. Qualitative examples with a navigation planner illustrate the reconstructed planes blocking paths through glass while leaving traversable routes open. The project page is available at https://glassguardproject.github.io/.

</details>


### [80] [SkeleWAM: Skeleton World-Action Modeling for Efficient Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02120)
*Juyi Sheng,Hua Wang,Mengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkeleWAM 用稀疏3D骨架（机器人关节、物体中心、交互点）表示操作场景，统一动作生成与未来骨架预测，在 LIBERO-Plus 上以 57.1M 参数取得 85.9% 成功率，超过 Cosmos-Policy 3.7 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）多预测视频或学习到的视觉隐变量，对交互几何的表示是隐式的，并可能保留与控制无关的外观信息，导致状态空间冗余、效率低且鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出 SkeleWAM，将操作场景表示为由机器人关节、物体中心和交互点构成的稀疏3D骨架，并在线由当前 RGB-D 观测与机器人本体感知构建。该骨架作为统一几何状态，同时驱动动作生成与未来骨架预测；未来骨架预测为动作学习提供额外几何监督，且无需视觉重建。推理时，模型直接由当前骨架与语言指令生成动作，并引入 Medoid Action Consensus（MAC）作为随机动作采样的辅助共识策略。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus 基准上，SkeleWAM 以 57.1M 参数量达到 85.9% 的总体成功率，较 Cosmos-Policy 高出 3.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，稀疏3D机器人—物体结构可作为有效的状态空间，支撑鲁棒且参数高效的世界动作学习。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) combine robot action generation with future state prediction. Existing WAMs typically predict videos or learned visual latents, which represent interaction geometry only implicitly and may retain appearance information unrelated to control. We introduce SkeleWAM, a compact WAM that represents a manipulation scene as a sparse 3D skeleton composed of robot joints, object centers, and interaction points. Constructed online from current RGB-D observations and robot proprioception, the skeleton provides a unified geometric state for action generation and future skeleton prediction. Future skeleton prediction provides additional geometric supervision for action learning without requiring visual reconstruction. At inference, SkeleWAM generates actions directly from the current skeleton and language instruction, while Medoid Action Consensus (MAC) serves as an auxiliary consensus strategy for stochastic action samples. On LIBERO-Plus, SkeleWAM achieves an overall success rate of 85.9% with 57.1M parameters, outperforming Cosmos-Policy by 3.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate that sparse 3D robot--object structure provides an effective state space for robust and parameter-efficient world action learning. The project is available at https://skelewam-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [81] [DuoMind: Enabling Distributed Multi-Robot Coordination with Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02161)
*Hanchu Zhou,Dechen Gao,Hang Wang,Brendan Lynch,Boqi Zhao,Qiyao Ma,Raman Goyal,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式分层多机器人协调框架DuoMind，结合VLM编排器与VLA执行器，通过语义通信实现长时程协作，并构建RoboPoly基准验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）与视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的进展主要集中在单机器人场景。多机器人系统需在分布式控制下协调长时程行为，同时保持可靠、精细的执行，现有方法难以兼顾，且缺乏相应基准。

Method: DuoMind为分布式分层框架：每个机器人配备基于VLA的低层动作模型与基于VLM的高层编排器。每步规划时，编排器基于任务指令、局部观测及来自其他机器人的语义消息进行推理，输出给动作模型的低层指令与面向同伴的语义消息，从而融合VLM的语义推理能力与VLA的精确动作生成能力。同时构建RoboPoly基准，包含分布式控制下的长时程协调闭环操作任务。

Result: 在RoboPoly与RoboTwin上的实验表明，DuoMind提升了多机器人任务性能；消融实验验证了分层编排与语义通信各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过分层编排与语义通信，DuoMind有效将VLM/VLA能力扩展至多机器人协调场景，RoboPoly为该方向提供了长时程协作评测基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs) have recently driven rapid progress in general-purpose robots, yet most progress has focused on single-robot settings. Extending these capabilities to multi-robot systems remains challenging because robots must coordinate long-horizon behaviors while maintaining reliable, fine-grained execution. We introduce DuoMind, a distributed hierarchical framework for multi-robot coordination through semantic communication. Each robot uses a VLA-based action model for low-level execution and a VLM-based orchestrator for high-level reasoning and inter-agent coordination. At each planning step, the orchestrator at each robot reasons over the task instruction, local observations, and messages received from other robots. It then generates low-level instructions for the action model and semantic messages for peer robots. This architecture exploits the complementary strengths of pretrained models by combining the semantic reasoning capabilities of VLMs with the precise action-generation capabilities of VLAs. To address the scarcity of benchmarks for multi-robot coordination, we further develop RoboPoly, a benchmark comprising long-horizon manipulation tasks that require coordinated, closed-loop execution under distributed control. Experiments on RoboPoly and RoboTwin demonstrate that DuoMind improves multi-robot task performance, while ablation studies confirm the contributions of hierarchical orchestration and semantic communication. More details are available on our project page.

</details>


### [82] [Watch, Infer, Coordinate: Inferring Robot Partner Constraints for Zero-Shot Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02170)
*Suyu Ye,Zheyuan Zhang,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Tianmin Shu,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Nakul Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WIC基准（三种物理耦合操作场景）与基于联合行为评分的约束推断方法，使助手机器人能从团队协作中推断伙伴的物理约束，并在新任务中实现零样本协调，接近先知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作（尤其搬运大型/重型物体）日益普遍，但硬件退化或执行器故障会限制机器人可靠执行的动作，而伙伴往往不知情。关键难点在于：示范只显示受限机器人"做了什么"，而非"本可以做什么"；在物理耦合任务中，另一机器人还会补偿其局限，使约束更难从受限机器人单独行为中识别。

Method: 核心洞见：约束会塑造团队联合行为，因此双方动作都对受限伙伴的能力具有信息量。据此提出Watch, Infer, Coordinate（WIC）基准，覆盖三种物理耦合操作场景，并配套一种推断方法，通过观测到的联合行为对候选约束进行打分。

Result: 在三种场景中，该方法显著提升了约束推断与零样本协调性能，接近拥有真实约束信息的oracle水平。

Conclusion: 助手机器人可通过观察伙伴与另一机器人的协作来推断其物理约束，并将推断能力迁移至新任务；从联合行为中推断能力是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Robots operating in the physical world will increasingly need to coordinate with other robots, particularly in manipulation tasks where an object may be too large or heavy for a single robot to carry alone. Physical limitations caused by hardware degradation or actuator faults can restrict the actions a robot can reliably execute, yet these limitations may be unknown to its partner. We study whether a helper can infer a robot partner's physical constraints from observing it coordinate with another robot, then use the inferred capability to coordinate with the same partner on a new task. This is difficult because a demonstration shows what the constrained robot did, but not what it could have done. In physically coupled tasks, the other robot may also compensate for its limitations, making those limitations difficult to identify from the constrained robot's behavior alone. Our key insight is that these constraints shape the joint behavior of the team, making the actions of both robots informative about the constrained partner's capability. We introduce Watch, Infer, Coordinate, a benchmark spanning three physically coupled manipulation settings, together with an inference approach that scores candidate constraints using observed joint behavior. Across all three settings, our method substantially improves constraint inference and zero-shot coordination, approaching an oracle with access to the true constraints.

</details>


### [83] [InterEvolve: Test-Time Evolution of Reward Programs for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02196)
*Zhuo Lin,Sirui Xu,Liuyu Bian,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出InterEvolve框架，通过LLM智能体与数值优化器协同进化奖励程序，在测试时复用冻结控制器（基于物体感知的前向-后向行为基础模型）的既有技能，无需重新训练即可完成人形机器人全身移动操作任务，并成功迁移至真实Unitree G1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需为每个新任务重新训练控制器，成本高。作者的核心洞察是：一个泛化的控制器已具备新任务所需的大部分能力，关键在于构建一个既能表达接触丰富、多阶段交互、又能被执行与度量、从而用执行反馈指导规划的"规划-控制接口"。现有接口难以同时满足表达性与可执行性，导致控制器潜在能力未被充分释放。

Method: InterEvolve由两部分构成：(1) 物体感知的前向-后向（FB）行为基础模型，在冻结的身体先验上叠加物体残差，可在测试时将关于身体或物体的新奖励即时转化为移动操作行为；(2) 将任务规范为"奖励程序"——带完成条件的阶段式奖励与可调常数。LLM智能体基于执行反馈和已验证程序构成的技能库，在上下文中修订程序结构；数值优化器则调节常数。每个候选方案在并行仿真场景中验证，迭代探索诱导、复用与组合控制器既有运动技能的新方式。

Result: 实验表明，人工设计的奖励未能充分挖掘FB模型的移动操作能力，而InterEvolve进化出的程序释放了这些潜力，部分通过新颖策略实现。该方法在仿真中为多样任务、复杂场景与长时程组合生成了行为，进化出的技能还能基于第一视角机载感知在真实Unitree G1上自主运行。

Conclusion: 通过测试时的奖励程序进化，可在不重新训练的前提下复用并重组控制器的既有技能，释放被人工奖励掩盖的潜力，并具备跨任务、跨场景、长时程及真机部署的泛化能力，为人形机器人测试时技能进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study test-time evolution for humanoid loco-manipulation: solving tasks that a controller was never trained for by repurposing its existing skills, improving from its own attempts, and retaining what it learns, without retraining. Our key insight is that a broad controller already holds much of the competence a new task needs, and that this competence becomes accessible through an interface between planning and control that is expressive enough to specify contact-rich, multi-stage interactions, yet executable and measurable enough that execution feedback can guide planning from experience. InterEvolve realizes this interface with two components. First, we develop an object-aware forward-backward (FB) behavioral foundation model, whose object residuals on a frozen body prior turn a new reward about the body or objects into loco-manipulation behavior at test time. Second, we specify tasks as reward programs: staged rewards with completion conditions and tunable constants. A large language model (LLM) agent revises the program structure in context, drawing on execution feedback and a skill library of verified programs, while a numerical optimizer tunes its constants. With every candidate verified across parallel simulation scenarios, the program explores new ways to induce, repurpose, and compose the controller's existing motor competence for the task at hand, and thus improves over iterations. Experiments show that human-designed rewards leave much of the FB model's loco-manipulation competence untapped, whereas the programs InterEvolve evolves release it, sometimes through novel strategies. It further produces behaviors for diverse tasks, complex scenes, and long-horizon compositions in simulation, and evolved skills run autonomously on a physical Unitree G1 from egocentric onboard perception.

</details>


### [84] [Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real: Guided Self-Improvement for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02204)
*Yen-Jen Wang,Haozhe Jiang,Shuying Deng,Haoru Xue,Weirui Ye,Rocky Duan,Nika Haghtalab,S. Shankar Sastry,Pieter Abbeel,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RPG 是一个无需更新模型权重、通过离线数据构建仿真练习任务并迭代修订符号技能与系统提示的机器人自主改进框架，将22项任务成功率从28.6%提升至95.0%，并在30次真机试验中全部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建跨多样任务的可靠机器人能力通常需要大量人工投入，包括技能开发与维护、奖励设计以及感知与控制的集成。作者希望减少这种人工依赖，实现机器人执行系统的自主改进。

Method: RPG（Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real）在不更新模型权重的前提下工作：先从离线数据集中识别可操作能力，并在仿真中构建相关练习任务；练习阶段利用执行反馈、仿真器特权状态和数据集视频诊断失败，据此开发可复用的新符号技能、精炼已有技能并修订系统提示；通过跨任务评估逐个检验候选改动及合并后的修订，再决定是否保留复用。测试时，由多模态大语言模型依据最终系统提示与技能库协调感知与机器人控制。

Result: 在22项操作任务的留出初始化上，成功率从首轮练习后的28.6%提升到15轮后的95.0%，优于所有评估基线，包括ASPIRE（75.5%）和由GPT-6 Astra Pro驱动的CaP-Agent0（60.0%）。经过统一的标定与硬件适配流程后，冻结系统在全部30次物理试验中成功（三项任务各10次）。

Conclusion: RPG 表明，机器人执行系统可以在不更新模型权重的情况下，通过"重建—练习—实机迁移"的闭环自主积累可复用技能并持续改进，并具备向真实硬件迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Building reliable robot capabilities across diverse tasks requires substantial human effort to develop and maintain skills, design rewards, and integrate perception with control. We present Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real (RPG), a framework for autonomous improvement of robot execution systems without updating model weights. RPG identifies manipulation capabilities in an offline dataset and constructs related practice tasks in simulation. During practice, RPG uses execution feedback, privileged simulator state, and available dataset videos to diagnose failures. It develops new reusable symbolic skills, refines existing skills, and revises the system prompt based on these diagnoses. Cross-task evaluation tests individual candidate changes and merged revisions before they are retained for reuse. At test time, a multimodal LLM uses the resulting system prompt and skill library to coordinate perception and robot control. On held-out initializations of 22 manipulation tasks, RPG improves task success from 28.6% after the first practice round to 95.0% after 15 rounds, outperforming all evaluated baselines, including ASPIRE (75.5%) and CaP-Agent0 powered by GPT-6 Astra Pro (60.0%). After a common calibration and hardware-adaptation procedure, the frozen system succeeds in all 30 physical trials, with ten trials on each of three tasks. Project Website: https://rpg-robot.github.io/

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Sectional category versus module sectional category for retractive maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00089)
*Paul-Eugene Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 构造了一个满足同伦收缩的映射 p，使得 msecat(p)=2<secat(p)=3，从而否证了 Carrasquel–Vera 关于收缩映射的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carrasquel–Vera 提出猜想，认为对于具有同伦收缩的映射，msecat 与 secat 应满足某种相等或不等式关系。本文旨在构造反例以检验该猜想的正确性。

Method: 在有限有理型单连通有理空间之间构造映射 p，使其满足同伦收缩条件，并以一个单连通十二维 CW 复形（十一个胞腔）的有理化空间为目标空间，计算其 msecat 与 secat。

Result: 所构造映射满足 msecat(p)=2<secat(p)=3，且目标空间为有理双曲空间，与猜想预期不符。

Conclusion: 该反例否证了 Carrasquel–Vera 关于收缩映射的猜想。

Abstract: We construct a map between simply connected rational spaces of finite rational type which admits a homotopy retraction and satisfies $msecat(p)=2<secat(p)=3$. The target is the rationalization of a simply connected twelve-dimensional CW complex with eleven cells, and is rationally hyperbolic. This disproves Carrasquel--Vera's conjecture for retractive maps.

</details>


### [86] [A homotopical enhancement of Neisendorfer's algebraic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01350)
*Bruno Stonek*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文对Neisendorfer关于有理单纯集、交换dg代数、余交换dg余代数与dg李代数同伦范畴之间等价关系的结论，给出了一个$\infty$-范畴提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neisendorfer证明的相关同伦范畴等价关系（在连通性、幂零性和有限型假设下）此前缺乏$\infty$-范畴层面的刻画，本文旨在将其提升到更精细的$\infty$-范畴框架。

Method: 证明线性对偶是弱有限型连通余交换dg余代数与交换dg代数之间的部分Quillen等价；并证明Quillen的Chevalley--Eilenberg伴随是余交换dg余代数与dg李代数之间的部分crossed derivable等价，后者为本文新引入的概念，用于精确公理化该伴随函子的行为。

Result: 建立了线性对偶的部分Quillen等价性，以及Chevalley--Eilenberg伴随的部分crossed derivable等价性，从而给出Neisendorfer等价关系的$\infty$-范畴增强。

Conclusion: 在相应假设（连通性、幂零性、有限型）下，理性同伦论中几类代数模型之间的等价可在$\infty$-范畴层面得到统一刻画，所引入的"部分crossed derivable等价"为分析此类伴随函子提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We give an $\infty$-categorical enhancement of Neisendorfer's equivalences between the homotopy categories of rational simplicial sets, commutative dg algebras (cdgas), cocommutative dg coalgebras (cdgcs) and dg Lie algebras (dgls), where these objects have respective hypotheses of connectedness, nilpotency and finite type. We do so by proving that linear duality is a partial Quillen equivalence between weak finite type connected cdgcs and cdgas, and that Quillen's Chevalley--Eilenberg adjunction is a partial crossed derivable equivalence between cdgcs and dgls, a concept we introduce in this paper to axiomatize precisely the behavior of this adjunction.

</details>


### [87] [Mumford--Morita--Miller classes in generalised cohomology theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01888)
*Oscar Randal-Williams*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在任意复定向上同调理论中定义黎曼曲面模空间上的Mumford–Morita–Miller类，并开启对其研究，重点揭示新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Mumford–Morita–Miller类通常在普通上同调中研究，其在更一般的复定向上同调理论中的行为尚不清楚，可能带来新的代数拓扑与模空间现象。

Method: 将Mumford–Morita–Miller类的定义推广到任意复定向上同调理论，并对其展开初步研究，着重分析新出现的现象。

Result: 在广义复定向上同调理论框架下成功定义了该类，并指出相较经典情形会呈现新的现象（摘要未给出具体计算结果）。

Conclusion: Mumford–Morita–Miller类可在任意复定向上同调理论中定义，其研究开启了新的方向，值得进一步深入探索。

Abstract: The Mumford--Morita--Miller classes on the moduli space of Riemann surfaces may be defined not only in ordinary cohomology but in any complex-oriented cohomology theory. We begin their investigation, emphasising the new phenomena that arise.

</details>


### [88] [Stable Complex Structures On Real Vector Bundles Over Connected Sums Of Quaternionic Projective Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02147)
*Souvik Mandal*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 通过分析复化映射的像，刻画了$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$上具有稳定复结构的实向量丛，并证明$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$稳定几乎复当且仅当$n=2$且$\ell-m$为偶数，从而对所有$n\ge3$否定了几乎复结构的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato与Suzuki在1974年仅对$3\le n\le10$断言了$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$上几乎复结构的不存在性，且遗留了$n=3,\ell=3m+1$的例外情形；本文旨在对所有$n\ge3$彻底解决该问题，并给出更强的稳定几乎复结构结论。

Method: 设$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}=\ell\,\mathbb{HP}^{n}\#\,m\,\overline{\mathbb{HP}^{n}}$，分析复化映射$c\colon\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$的像，用Pontryagin类刻画其上允许稳定复结构的实向量丛。

Result: 给出了用Pontryagin类描述的实向量丛允许稳定复结构的充要条件；证明$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$稳定几乎复当且仅当$n=2$且$\ell-m$为偶数；因此对任意$n\ge3$，$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$不允许几乎复结构。

Conclusion: 对所有$n\ge3$建立了$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$几乎复结构的不存在性，消除了Sato–Suzuki的$n=3,\ell=3m+1$例外情形，并将结论强化到稳定几乎复结构层面。

Abstract: For integers $n\ge 2$ and integers $\ell,m\ge 0$, not both zero, let $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}=\ell\,\mathbb{HP}^{n}\,\#\,m\,\overline{\mathbb{HP}^{n}}$ denote the connected sum of $\ell$ copies of the quaternionic projective space $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ and $m$ copies of $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ endowed with the opposite orientation. By analysing the image of the complexification map $c\,\colon\,\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$, we characterise, in terms of Pontryagin classes, the real vector bundles over $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ admitting a stable complex structure, and deduce that $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ is stably almost complex if and only if $n=2$ and $\ell-m$ is even. Consequently no $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ with $n\ge 3$ admits an almost complex structure. Sato and Suzuki asserted the non-existence of almost complex structures on $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ in 1974 for $3\le n\le 10$, except possibly when $n=3$ and $\ell=3m+1$; we establish it for all $n\ge 3$, and in the stronger form of stable almost complex structures.

</details>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Topologically Relevant Landmark Selection on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00019)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出基于Hodge Laplacian的landmark选择方法，通过为每个点赋予基于调和参与度的拓扑相关性得分，选取高分点作为landmarks，从而更准确地恢复原始点云的持久同调（PH）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PH在大规模点云上计算代价过高；常见的近似方法是选取代表点子集（landmarks）计算PH，但现有landmark选择方法主要应对离群点污染等问题，而非以恢复全数据集的拓扑特征为目标。

Method: 提出基于Hodge Laplacian的landmark选择方法：为每个点分配基于其调和参与度（harmonic participation）的拓扑相关性得分，并选择高分点作为landmarks。该方法量化每个点对全局同调结构的参与程度。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上，该方法恢复原始点云PH的准确性优于几何基线方法，以及一种基于局部PH的方法。

Conclusion: 基于Hodge Laplacian的拓扑相关性评分能有效选取拓扑相关的landmarks，从而更准确地近似原始点云的持久同调，为大规模点云的PH近似提供更优方案。

Abstract: Persistent Homology (PH) is an important tool in Topological Data Analysis for point clouds, but can be prohibitively expensive for large datasets. A practical approximation is to compute PH on a smaller subset of representative points, known as landmarks. Existing landmark selection methods mainly address challenges such as outlier contamination rather than restoring the topological features of the full dataset. To select topologically relevant landmarks, we propose a landmark selection method based on Hodge Laplacian. It assigns each point a topological relevance score based on its harmonic participation and selects points with high scores as landmarks. By quantifying each point's participation in global homological structures, our method restores the PH of the original point cloud more accurately than geometric baselines and a method built upon local PH on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [90] [Minimum Spanning Trees for Square Crop Plots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00807)
*Mingyang Gong,Adiesha Liyanage,Braeden Sopp,Muzhou Chen,Binhai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究单位正方形集合在“crop plot 距离”下的最小生成树问题：该距离不满足三角不等式，证明其 MST 计算为 NP-hard，并给出因子 4 的近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题是受农田中访问作物地块的场景启发：每个地块只能由给定的入口/出口点对访问（有 3 种类型，每种包含 4 种情形）。作者关注这类轴对齐单位正方形构成的平面点集在对应距离下的最小生成树（MST）。由于该 crop plot 距离即使来自几何设定也不满足三角不等式，常规几何 MST 方法不能直接套用，需要额外处理。

Method: 将问题形式化为轴对齐单位正方形集合 $P$ 上的 MST，其中距离为 crop plot 距离；针对入口/出口点对的三类、每类四种情形进行分析。由于距离非度量性，作者需要专门处理 MST 的结构与近似，而不依赖三角不等式。

Result: (1) 证明了在 crop plot 距离下计算集合 $P$ 的 MST 是 NP-hard；(2) 给出因子为 4 的近似算法。

Conclusion: 在受作物地块访问约束的单位正方形集合上，MST 问题具有本质困难性（NP-hard），但可通过常数因子近似有效求解，近似比为 4。

Abstract: Motivated by accessing crop plots in a field, where each crop plot can only be visited by a given pair of entry/exit points (we have three different types, each having four cases), we study the corresponding Minimum Spanning Tree (MST) problem of such a planar set of axis-aligned unit squares. It turns out that this crop plot distance does not satisfy triangle inequality, even though the whole setup of the problem is geometric. Hence additional care must be taken. The main results of this paper are as follows: (1) we prove that computing the MST of a set $P$ of unit squares under the crop plot distance is NP-hard, (2) the MST of $P$ can be approximated with a factor-4 approximation.

</details>


### [91] [Optimal Coresets for Hyperbolic Farthest-Point Queries via Ideal-Boundary Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01130)
*Eunku Park*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲空间中farthest-point查询的核心集（coreset）问题，证明对任意固定维度D≥2，最优最坏情形核心集大小为Θ(ε^{-(D-1)/2})，并通过将双曲查询精确归约为理想边界上的上包络来建立匹配的上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲空间中的最远点查询在核心集理论中缺乏紧确的复杂度刻画；作者希望在加性误差ε与保留1-ε比例的双重保证下，确定最优的核心集规模，并给出几何上的结构性刻画。

Method: 核心几何工具是将双曲查询精确归约为理想边界上的上包络：在双曲面模型中，每个输入点诱导一个正的边界得分函数，其对数给出沿测地射线的渐近距离偏移；定义理想边界包络为这些函数的逐点最大值，并证明所有查询上的加性损失上确界等于输入包络与核心集包络之间的最大对数间隙。上界方面，将最小包围球中心移至原点并归一化空间坐标，得到边界包络有正下界的欧氏点集，用标准欧氏核同时逼近所有方向得分最大值。下界方面，在固定半径双曲球面上做球面填充，结合对跖查询与双曲余弦定理，使每个输入点都不可替代。

Result: 对每个固定D≥2，最优最坏情形核心集规模被证明为Θ(ε^{-(D-1)/2})；下界即使在仅要求加性误差或仅要求1-ε比例保证之一时也成立，与上界匹配。

Conclusion: 双曲最远点查询的核心集复杂度由维度指数(D-1)/2决定，理想边界上包络的归约为上下界提供了统一的几何框架，且该复杂度在两种保证分别放宽时仍是紧确的。

Abstract: We study coresets for farthest-point queries in hyperbolic space. Given a nonempty finite set $P \subset \mathbb{H}^D$ and $0<\varepsilon \le 1$, we seek a coreset $P_{\varepsilon} \subseteq P$ whose farthest distance from every query point underestimates that of $P$ by at most an additive $\varepsilon$ and retains at least a $1-\varepsilon$ fraction of it. For every fixed $D \ge 2$, we prove that the optimal worst-case coreset size is $Θ\bigl(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2}\bigr)$.
  Our main geometric ingredient is an exact reduction from hyperbolic queries to an upper envelope on the ideal boundary. In the hyperboloid model, each input point induces a positive boundary-score function whose logarithm gives its asymptotic distance offset along geodesic rays. We define the \emph{ideal-boundary envelope} as the pointwise maximum of these functions and prove that the supremum additive loss over all queries equals the maximum logarithmic gap between the input and coreset envelopes.
  For the upper bound, we move the minimum-enclosing-ball center to the origin and normalize the spatial coordinates, obtaining a bounded Euclidean point set whose boundary envelope is bounded away from zero. A standard Euclidean kernel then approximates all directional score maxima simultaneously, and the structure theorem yields both guarantees. For the lower bound, a spherical packing on a fixed-radius hyperbolic sphere, together with antipodal queries and the hyperbolic cosine law, makes every input point indispensable, matching the upper bound even for either guarantee separately.

</details>


### [92] [Hyperbolic Sphericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01336)
*Thomas Bläsius,Lennart Großkreutz,Jean-Pierre von der Heydt*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出双曲球面性概念，研究其与欧氏球面性在半径可变、半径固定及半径变化三种情形下的关系，并给出半径变化导致球面性振荡的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧氏球面性已被广泛研究，但双曲空间中的球面性尚未被系统研究；双曲球面性可能显著小于欧氏球面性，且强烈依赖球半径，这一特性值得深入刻画。

Method: 定义图的（d维等半径球）双曲球面性为最小维数d；分别在半径可依赖图、半径固定、半径变化三种设定下证明球面性的上下界与关系，并给出使球面性随半径振荡的显式图构造。

Result: 半径可依赖图时，双曲球面性不超过欧氏球面性（将2维uniform disk graph的结果推广到任意维度，且证明更简洁）；半径固定时，双曲球面性可比欧氏球面性大最多1；增大半径可使球面性显著下降，但最多上升1；存在图使球面性随半径在不同取值间振荡。

Conclusion: 双曲球面性与欧氏球面性密切相关但不完全一致，其对球半径的依赖性带来新的理论现象；该结果对机器学习中图的低维数值表示与图嵌入具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The sphericity of a graph is the minimum dimension d such that the graph has an intersection representation of d-dimensional balls of equal radius. While sphericity has been studied in Euclidean space, we initiate the study of hyperbolic sphericity. The hyperbolic sphericity of a graph can be significantly smaller than its Euclidean counterpart, but, contrary to the Euclidean setting, depends strongly on the radius of the balls.
  We show that, if the radius of the balls can be chosen depending on the graph, the hyperbolic sphericity is upper bounded by the Euclidean sphericity. This extends a previous result for 2-dimensional hyperbolic space, i.e., uniform disk graphs, to arbitrary dimensions. Moreover, our proof is significantly simpler. If we fix the radius, i.e., do not make it dependent on the graph, we show that hyperbolic sphericity can be larger than Euclidean sphericity, but by at most 1. Additionally, we study how hyperbolic sphericity changes with the ball radius. We show that choosing a larger radius can substantially decrease the sphericity while increasing it by at most 1. We also provide a construction of a graph where the sphericity oscillates between different values as the radius increases.
  Besides being theoretically interesting, we note that these results are relevant for graph embeddings in machine learning, where one is interested in low-dimensional numeric representations of symbolic data like graphs.

</details>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Closed-form evaluation of the cyclic four-electron atomic correlation integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00036)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了Bonham 1965分类中情形V的环状四电子Slater积分在正指数下的显式有限表达式,权重不超过3,由18个仅含对数、二重对数与三重对数的块组成,并给出等指数特例、数值验证、极限律及对称性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环状四电子Slater积分是量子化学多中心电子排斥积分的基础对象,但此前该类积分缺乏有限闭式表达,往往需要广义多重对数、根式、围道规定或未求值极限。作者希望给出无需这些复杂工具的显式有限表达式,并厘清不同拓扑与对称性下积分的精确结构。

Method: 作者针对Bonham分类中的情形V(四环拓扑),对正指数α1,…,α4求值该Slater积分,将其表示为18个涉及指数有理函数之对数、二重对数、三重对数的块之和,并保证所有对数参数为正、非常数多重对数参数可置于(-1,1)。同时对等指数情况化简为特殊值;采用三种独立数值途径与精确二进制定值进行验证;推导小/大指数极限律与系数、二面体对称性以及三个不等价环状配置的严格重排律。

Result: 一般答案为18个块的显式和,仅含对数、二重对数与三重对数,权重至多3,无需根式、广义多重对数、围道规定或未求值极限。等指数时取值收缩为I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[Li_3(1/4)/2-7ζ(3)/24+π^2log2/12-2log^3 2/3],是仅在2和3处分歧的\Q上的权重3周期。三种独立数值途径在四组指数元组上至少吻合32位小数,经典公式的精确二进制定值给出8个100位证书,三个已发表四电子校准值逐位复现。同时证明了带闭式系数的小/大指数极限律、二面体对称性以及三个不等价环状配置的严格重排律。

Conclusion: 该公式适用于四环拓扑,不适用于两个带弦拓扑。结果为环状四电子Slater积分提供了有限、无需特殊函数的显式表达,并给出了等指数周期、极限行为与对称/重排性质。

Abstract: For positive exponents $α_1,\ldots,α_4$ we evaluate the cyclic four-electron Slater integral, the topology recorded as case V in Bonham's 1965 classification, in a finite real expression of weight at most three. The general answer is an explicit sum of eighteen blocks involving only logarithms, dilogarithms and trilogarithms of rational functions of the exponents; all logarithm arguments are positive and all nonconstant polylogarithm arguments may be placed in $(-1,1)$, so no radical, generalized polylogarithm, contour prescription or unevaluated limit is required. At equal exponents the value collapses to $I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[\mathrm{Li}_3(1/4)/2-7ζ(3)/24+π^2\log 2/12-2\log^3 2/3]$, a weight-three period over $\mathbb{Q}$ ramified only at 2 and 3. Three independently implemented numerical routes agree at four exponent tuples to at least 32 decimal places, an exact-dyadic evaluation of the classical formula gives eight 100-place certificates, and three published four-electron calibration values are reproduced to every printed digit. We also prove the small- and large-exponent limit laws with closed-form coefficients, dihedral symmetry, and a strict rearrangement law for the three inequivalent cyclic placements of four exponents. The formula applies to the four-cycle and does not evaluate the two chorded topologies.

</details>

Conclusion: 本原KMS对称量子Markov半群在 (0,1) 上不具有一致正的反向超压缩速率，但可在受限区间或经去相位扰动后获得该性质；图基半群在连通图条件下亦具备类似的反向超压缩行为，这深化了对量子Markov半群超压缩/反向超压缩机制的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant vanishes at $p=1/2$ and a correlated graph-based example for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant tends to $-\infty$ as $p\to 0^+$. These examples obstruct a positive uniform reverse-hypercontractivity rate on $(0,1)$. By contrast, we show that every primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup satisfies quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $1/2< p\leq q < 1$ with explicit sufficient times. For each fixed $δ>0$, a sufficiently strong dephasing perturbation satisfies a uniform positive logarithmic Sobolev inequality on $[δ, 1]$ and hence quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $δ< p\leq q<1$ with fixed $0<δ<1/2$. Furthermore, we establish reverse hypercontractivity for graph-based KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for $δ< p\leq q< 1$ with fixed $0<δ<1$ for connected graphs when $n \geq 3$.

### [95] [Complete Factorial Asymptotics and Integrable Structure of Generalized Charlier Recurrence Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00396)

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究广义Charlier多项式单项递推系数β_n、γ_n，证明其偏差呈超代数阶，主阶由μ^{n+1}/n!(n+1)!等给出，并通过Bessel比较与Hankel行列式分解两种途径给出严格渐近与精确联系公式c_n∼J_n(2√μ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对离散权ρ_μ(k)=μ^k/(k!)^2对应的广义Charlier多项式，作者希望严格刻画其单项递推系数相对极限的偏差，并揭示其与离散Painlevé II方程、Bessel函数及Hankel行列式/离散对数气体的深层可积结构联系。

Method: 通过参数化β_n-n=√μ c_n c_{n+1}、γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)，将问题化为满足离散Painlevé II的正序列(c_n)。在c_n=0附近线性化得到离散Bessel方程，其衰退解为J_n(2√μ)；证明比值c_n/J_n(2√μ)收敛，并结合Bessel比较与基于Hankel行列式扇区分解的全局分析确定极限L(μ)=1。另用Hankel行列式Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ)及其扇区分解T_n(μ)=1+Σ_{q=1}^n S_q(μ)进行独立推导。

Result: 得到精确连接公式c_n∼J_n(2√μ)及偏差渐近：β_n-n∼μ^{n+1}/[n!(n+1)!]，μ-γ_n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!)^2；证明偏差超越所有代数阶，出现在尺度μ^n/(n!)^2。并证明扇区贡献S_q(μ)∼e^μ μ^q/(q!)^2，独立给出阶乘修正。

Conclusion: Bessel比较与扇区分解共同揭示了离散Painlevé动力学、Toda形变与Hankel-分拆表示之间的丰富可积结构，为递推系数的超代数阶小偏差提供了统一而精确的描述。

Abstract: We study the monic recurrence coefficients of the generalized Charlier polynomials associated with the discrete weight
  \[
  ρ_μ(k)
  =
  \frac{μ^k}{(k!)^2},
  \qquad
  k\in\mathbb N_0,
  \qquad
  μ>0.
  \]
  We establish complete factorial asymptotics for the deviations $β_n-n$ and $μ-γ_n$, proving that they are beyond all algebraic orders and appear at the scale $μ^{n}/(n!)^2$. The key step is a parametrization $β_n-n=\sqrtμ\,c_nc_{n+1}$, $γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)$, which reduces the problem to a positive sequence $(c_n)$ satisfying a discrete Painlevé II equation.
  Linearization around $c_n=0$ yields a discrete Bessel equation whose recessive solution is $J_n(2\sqrtμ)$. We prove that the ratio $c_n/J_n(2\sqrtμ)$ converges to a finite limit $L(μ)$, which we identify exactly as $L(μ)=1$ by combining Bessel comparison with a global analysis based on the sector decomposition of the associated Hankel determinant. This yields the exact connection formula
  \[
  c_n \sim J_n(2\sqrtμ), \qquad n\to\infty,
  \]
  and consequently the precise asymptotics
  \[
  β_n-n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{n!(n+1)!}, \qquad
  μ-γ_n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{(n!)^2}.
  \]
  We further develop a complementary approach based on Hankel determinants interpreted as a discrete logarithmic gas. We show that the Hankel determinant factorizes as $Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ)$ and admits a sector decomposition $T_n(μ)=1+\sum_{q=1}^{n}S_q(μ)$ with sector contributions independent of $n$. We establish $S_q(μ)\sim e^μμ^q/(q!)^2$, providing an independent derivation of the factorial corrections. Together, the Bessel comparison and sector decomposition reveal a rich integrable structure linking discrete Painlevé dynamics, Toda deformations, and Hankel--partition representations.

</details>

Abstract: We study the spectral properties of the harmonic modification of the quantum mechanical Kronig-Penney model motivated by the theory of the dressing chain. In particular, we show that in the special cases the corresponding ground states can be given explicitly and all spectral gaps have the same length with the ends expressed as zeros of the corresponding Weber-Hermite functions.

### [97] [The Buchholz Algebra from the Universal Resolvent Algebra--Particle Structure, Gibbs States, and Closed-Path Expansions without Fock Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01622)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在不依赖Fock表示的前提下构造Buchholz代数：以规范固定预解代数的内禀粒子截断商的有限逆极限为框架，并具等距粒子数坐标；考察其有限矩阵角上的迹、KMS态、迹范数乘积公式与闭路展开，从而为路径积分表示奠定基础，并给出已知Fock空间描述与准局部子代数的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有Buchholz代数（通常）依赖Fock表示，限制了代数结构与物理实现的普适性；作者希望给出一个不依赖Fock表示的内禀构造，为KMS态、迹范数结构与路径积分表示提供更一般的代数基础。

Method: 取规范固定预解代数（gauge-fixed resolvent algebra）的内禀粒子截断商（intrinsic particle-cutoff quotients），构造其有限逆极限（bounded inverse limit），并引入等距粒子数坐标（isometric particle-degree coordinates）；在有限矩阵角（finite matrix corners）上定义并研究标准迹、KMS态与迹范数乘积公式，以及闭路展开（closed-path expansions）。

Result: 在该代数框架内得到有限矩阵角上的标准迹、KMS态和迹范数乘积公式，并建立闭路展开；这些结构为路径积分表示提供了出发点。

Conclusion: 所构造的Buchholz代数可作为自洽框架：既实现已知Fock空间描述，又产生一个合适的准局部子代数，从而在不依赖Fock表示的条件下统一代数与路径积分视角。

Abstract: We construct the Buchholz algebra without a Fock representation, as the bounded inverse limit of intrinsic particle-cutoff quotients of the gauge-fixed resolvent algebra, with isometric particle-degree coordinates. Its finite matrix corners carry canonical traces, KMS states, and trace-norm product formulas, together with closed-path expansions that lay the groundwork for path-integral representations. The known Fock-space description and a proper quasilocal subalgebra arise as realizations.

</details>

Abstract: In an earlier paper of P. Rossi and the first author, a construction of a wide class of quantum hierarchies was presented, with the input data given by an arbitrary cohomological field theory. A crucial role in the construction is played by the double ramification (DR) cycles on the moduli spaces of stable curves. As a special case, one gets a quantization of the hierarchy of the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation. The ILW equation has two famous limits: the Korteweg--de Vries equation and the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, and for the latter one a quantum hierarchy was constructed by Nazarov and Sklyanin. In our paper, we propose a conjecture describing a relation between a limit of the quantum ILW hierarchy and the Nazarov--Sklyanin hierarchy and present various checks to support it.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解多重可分离超可积系统在不同处理阶段会呈现哪些隐藏代数结构，以及精确或准精确可解性如何随分离与规范变换而变化；同时解释为何两个在原分类中不同的磁场哈密顿量会共享同一sl(3)结构。

Conclusion: 多重可分离超可积系统在不同阶段可涌现不同的隐藏代数结构与精确/准精确可解性；共同sl(3)结构源于固定轴向积分后的同一正规形式，而非原系统的等价性。

Abstract: We study two three-dimensional quantum superintegrable systems in nonvanishing axially symmetric magnetic fields. For each model the integrals generate a quadratic algebra with a Casimir, and finite-dimensional deformed-oscillator representations yield algebraic spectra. The same spectra are recovered by separation of variables. In circular parabolic coordinates the separated equations are sextic biconfluent-Heun equations with finite polynomial sectors. We formulate them as Sturm eigenvalue problems with the physical energy as a parameter and the separation constant diagonalized. After gauge transformation, each equation admits an $sl(2)$ algebraization and reduces to a finite tridiagonal matrix. We also construct a pre-separation Sturm representation of each complete Hamiltonian, taking the inverse-square coupling as the Sturm eigenvalue. The transformed integrals remain commuting and, after fixing the axial integral, changing variables and gauge rotating, the two-variable Sturm Hamiltonian and its non-central integrals belong to the universal enveloping algebra $U(sl(3))$. We thus obtain three complementary algebraic descriptions: the quadratic-algebra/deformed-oscillator representation of the original Hamiltonian, the $sl(2)$ algebraization after parabolic separation, and the $sl(3)$ algebraization of the complete Sturm problem before separation. Although the two magnetic Hamiltonians are distinct in the original classification, fixing the axial integral reduces them to the same singular 2:1 oscillator normal form with different effective parameter embeddings. This explains the common $sl(3)$ structure without implying equivalence of the original magnetic systems. These results show how distinct hidden algebraic structures and exact or quasi-exact solvability can emerge at different stages of a multiseparable superintegrable system.

</details>


### [100] [Superintegrability of a resonant ghostly tri-Hamiltonian model: polynomial first integrals and transcendental completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01901)
*Andreas Fring,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共振三维哈密顿系统的超可积性在不定动能与多重Poisson结构下的完整刻画尚不清楚，尤其是多项式、有理与超越首积分类的功能秩差异以及量子化后对称性的存在性需要系统澄清。

Result: 多项式不变环由一个八次关系约束，超越次数为四；所有有理首积分均为H1,H2,H3,I4^o的有理函数，有理不变域泛型功能秩为四；无分支超越积分在不变开集Δ>0上将泛型光滑功能秩提升至五。Weyl量子化在三种Poisson实现中均给出精确高阶微分对称，第五经典不变量的无分支形式存在稠定非局域量子对应。

Conclusion: 该系统在多项式与有理类中为最小超可积，在允许超越积分时于不变域上为最大超可积；量子层面通过Weyl量子化实现了超越完备化的具体对应。

Abstract: We study classical and quantum superintegrability of a resonant three-dimensional Hamiltonian with indefinite kinetic energy and three Poisson descriptions of the same flow. Besides three commuting quadratic Hamiltonians, the polynomial first-integral ring contains two primitive degree-four generators, $I_4^{\rm o}$ and $I_4^{\rm e}$, of odd and even momentum parity. A single octic relation leaves the polynomial invariant ring with transcendence degree four. Solving the complete first-integral equation shows that every rational first integral is a rational function of $H_1,H_2,H_3,I_4^{\rm o}$, so that the rational invariant field likewise has generic functional rank four. A branch-free transcendental invariant raises the generic smooth functional rank to five on the invariant open set $Δ>0$. The system is therefore minimally superintegrable in the polynomial and rational classes and maximally superintegrable on this invariant domain when transcendental integrals are admitted. Weyl quantisation yields exact higher-order differential symmetries in all three Poisson realisations. The branch-free fifth classical invariant also admits a densely defined nonlocal quantum counterpart, providing a concrete quantum realisation of the transcendental completion.

</details>
