### [117] [Emergence of electronic modes and triplet pairing from spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators in the Kanamori-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00110)
*Masanori Kohno*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，揭示了自旋-1反铁磁绝缘体在掺杂和温度变化下能带结构改变的机制，阐明了轨道简并系统中涌现电子模式的特征与起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规能带绝缘体的电子能带结构在掺杂（化学势移动）或升温下基本不变，但在Hubbard和Kondo晶格模型描述的Mott与Kondo绝缘体中，带边会向带隙涌现动量移动的磁色散模式。对于含轨道自由度的强关联系统，其能带随掺杂和温度如何变化及原因尚不清晰，本文旨在阐明这一问题。

Method: 研究采用双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，结合弱 hopping 有效理论、选择定则分析以及数值计算来刻画涌现电子模式的性质。

Result: 在典型参数区，每个轨道中掺杂诱导的模式反映不同自旋模式，而温度诱导的模式在两个轨道中主要反映低能自旋模式，两者均呈现动量移动的自旋模式色散关系；若hopping参数符号不同，涌现模式的动量区间可在轨道间不同。小轨道间排斥下，掺杂后可形成格点自旋三重态对，产生对破缺能隙；而温度诱导电子模式仍反映低能自旋模式且无对破缺能隙。此外还阐明了自旋或电荷扰动下非平衡态电子模式的涌现及特征。

Conclusion: 研究全面揭示了强关联大自旋反铁磁绝缘体中自旋、电荷和轨道自由度相互作用导致涌现电子模式的机理，为探索强关联系统中的非常规量子现象开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The electronic band structures of conventional band insulators generally remain unchanged under doping via chemical-potential shifts or increasing temperature. However, in Mott and Kondo insulators described by models such as the Hubbard and Kondo lattice models, distinct electronic modes with momentum-shifted magnetic dispersion relations have been shown to emerge from band edges into the gap. Here, by investigating the two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model, this study elucidates how and why the band structures of spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators change with doping and temperature, revealing emergent spectral features characteristic of orbitally degenerate systems. The nature of the emergent electronic modes is clarified using weak-hopping effective theory, selection-rule analysis, and numerical calculations. In a typical parameter regime, the doping-induced modes in each orbital reflect distinct types of spin modes, while the temperature-induced modes primarily reflect the low-energy spin mode in both orbitals; in both cases, the emergent modes exhibit momentum-shifted spin-mode dispersion relations. The momentum regimes of these emergent modes can differ between orbitals if signs of the hopping parameters differ. For small inter-orbital repulsion, onsite spin-triplet pairs can form after doping, leading to a pair-breaking gap, while the temperature-induced electronic modes continue to reflect the low-energy spin mode without a pair-breaking gap. Moreover, the emergence and characteristics of electronic modes in nonequilibrium states by spin or charge perturbations are elucidated. These findings provide a comprehensive understanding of how the interplay among the spin, charge, and orbital degrees of freedom in large-spin antiferromagnetic insulators leads to emergent electronic modes, opening avenues for exploring unconventional quantum phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [118] [Causality violations in cluster GW+DMFT: Exact Lehmann moments and the necessity of non-local vertex corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00324)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过在最小Hubbard团簇上精确对角化,严格检验了$GW$与$GW$+DMFT自能的解析结构,证明二者在阻挫团簇上均违反Matsubara因果性,并指出局域自能修正不足以恢复因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $GW$近似已知违反因果性,会在复频率平面上半部产生非物理极点;$GW$+DMFT通过注入精确局域自能试图修复这一问题。但目前缺乏在可控模型上对两者解析结构的严格基准检验,尤其是局域修正能否真正保证因果性尚不明确。

Method: 对最小Hubbard团簇进行精确对角化。先在二格点二聚体上分析,再转到非二分的三格点Hubbard环(具有阻挫)。通过解析计算精确Lehmann矩,推导自能高频尾的闭式表达式$C = U^2\,\mathrm{Var}(d)\,I$;以无插值的实轴精确自能计算作为主要因果性判据,有理延拓作为辅助证据,并分析完全不可约顶点$\Lambda$的高频渐近行为。

Result: 发现"二聚体反常":二格点二聚体上反演对称性迫使非局域自能为纯实数,掩盖了因果性破坏。在三格点阻挫环上,跳跃阻挫使非局域自能具有有限虚部,证明$GW$与$GW$+DMFT均违反Matsubara因果性条件。标准方法无法捕获强制该高频尾所需的精确等时双粒子关联函数。$GW$+DMFT中$\Lambda$的纯局域截断未能修正非局域RPA共振。两个诊断逻辑独立:$GW$+DMFT满足必要条件$C\succeq0$,但在有限频率仍违反Matsubara符号条件。

Conclusion: 局域自能修正不足以在阻挫团簇上保证因果的解析结构。等时关联函数编码于完全不可约顶点$\Lambda$的高频渐近中,而$GW$+DMFT的局域截断对其修正不完整,因此需要超越局域近似才能获得因果自能。

Abstract: The $GW$ approximation is known to violate causality, yielding self-energies with unphysical poles in the upper half of the complex frequency plane. The combined $GW$+DMFT approach attempts to cure this by injecting the exact local self-energy. Using exact diagonalization on minimal Hubbard clusters, we rigorously benchmark the analytic structure of the $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT self-energies. We demonstrate a ``dimer anomaly'': on the two-site dimer, inversion symmetry forces the non-local self-energy to be purely real, masking the causality violation. Moving to the non-bipartite three-site Hubbard ring, where hopping frustration yields a finite imaginary part in the non-local self-energy, we prove that both $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT violate the Matsubara causality condition. By analytically evaluating the exact Lehmann moments, we derive a closed-form expression for the high-frequency tail of the self-energy, $C = U^2\,\mathrm{Var}(d)\,I$, and show that standard methods fail to capture the exact equal-time two-particle correlators required to enforce it. The equal-time correlators that govern this tail are precisely those encoded in the high-frequency asymptotics of the fully irreducible vertex $Λ$; nevertheless, our intermediate-frequency analysis demonstrates that the local-only truncation of $Λ$ in $GW$+DMFT leaves non-local RPA resonances uncorrected. The two diagnostics are logically independent: $GW$+DMFT satisfies the necessary high-frequency condition $C\succeq0$ yet still violates the Matsubara sign condition at finite frequencies. Taking the interpolation-free real-axis evaluation of the exact self-energy as the primary causality certificate, with rational continuation as supporting evidence, we establish a rigorous benchmark demonstrating that local self-energy corrections are insufficient to guarantee a causal analytic structure on frustrated clusters.

</details>


### [119] [Anyon Proliferation and Anyon Superconductivity in Higgsing Transitions via Conformal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00452)
*Diego García-Sepúlveda,Da-Chuan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于共形嵌入讨论(2+1)维Chern-Simons Higgsing相变及其与任意子增殖的关系，刻画了拓扑序保持或扩大的转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Chern-Simons Higgsing相变如何在约化规范群的同时保持或扩大内禀拓扑序，并厘清其对偶相中任意子的增殖与凝聚机制。

Method: 采用共形嵌入方法研究(2+1)维Chern-Simons Higgsing相变；分析标量表示相对UV Chern-Simons水平的非可积性；利用启发式伴随筛选(adjoint screening)提议未Higgs相中的候选任意子，并通过在Higgs子群下的分支(branching)揭示Higgs相的可凝聚任意子通道；构造Spin(16)₁→Spin(9)₂转变及其可凝聚代数。

Result: 在SO(N)₂↪SU(N)₁族(N≥3)中，两个半经典区域具有等价的内禀拓扑序但全局U(1)对称性的实现不同；对奇数N，对应于从费米子1/N Laughlin态到电荷2e任意子超导（伴以相同手征Z_N拓扑序）的转变的非阿贝尔实现。标量表示对UV Chern-Simons水平非可积，因而不能直接标记未Higgs相的任意子。构造的Spin(16)₁→Spin(9)₂转变其可凝聚代数包含一个非阿贝尔任意子，但自融合中并不包含全部允许的可规范通道。

Conclusion: Chern-Simons Higgsing相变可保持或扩大拓扑序；当拓扑序扩大时两相由任意子凝聚区分，实现阿贝尔向非阿贝尔拓扑序的转变；未Higgs相任意子的标记需借助伴随筛选而非直接由标量表示给出。

Abstract: We discuss (2+1)d Chern-Simons Higgsing transitions based on conformal embeddings and their relation to the proliferation of anyons. These transitions preserve or enlarge the intrinsic topological order despite reducing the gauge group. When the topological order is enlarged, the two phases differ by anyon condensation, and the transition takes an Abelian topological order to a non-Abelian one. In the $SO(N)_{2} \hookrightarrow SU(N)_{1}$ family with $N \geq 3$, the two semiclassical regimes have equivalent intrinsic topological orders but distinct realizations of a global $U(1)$ symmetry. For odd $N$, these are non-Abelian realizations of transitions from fermionic $1/N$ Laughlin states to charge-$2e$ anyon superconductivity coexisting with the same chiral $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ topological order. The scalar representations are always non-integrable with respect to the UV Chern-Simons level and therefore do not directly label anyons of the un-Higgsed phase, despite defining Wilson lines in the UV. Using heuristic adjoint screening, we propose candidate anyons in the un-Higgsed phase, while branching under the Higgs subgroup reveals channels corresponding to condensable anyons of the Higgs phase. We further construct a $\mathrm{Spin}(16)_{1} \to \mathrm{Spin}(9)_{2}$ transition admitting a condensable algebra that contains a non-Abelian anyon but not all of the allowed gaugable channels in its self-fusion.

</details>


### [120] [Near-infrared magnetic linear and non-reciprocal directional dichroism in the chiral antiferromagnet SmFe3(BO3)4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00534)
*B. Beke,B. Szász,I. A. Gudim,D. Szaller,S. Bordács*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过偏振分辨磁光光谱研究了SmFe3(BO3)4中反铁磁序对近红外光学响应的影响，观察到磁线性二色性和非互易方向二色性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究反铁磁序对稀土铁硼酸盐近红外光学响应的影响，并寻找探测反铁磁序参量面内取向的光学手段。

Method: 采用偏振分辨的磁光光谱，研究SmFe3(BO3)4在奈尔温度以下的4f-4f跃迁；通过磁场旋转易平面反铁磁基态的序参量，并测量非互易吸收。

Result: 在Sm3+的4f-4f跃迁处观测到显著的磁线性二色性；利用磁场实现了线性二色性的调控并推导出偏振选择定则；在面内磁场垂直于二重旋转轴时，于对称性允许的环形几何中发现多个4f-4f跃迁的非互易方向二色性。

Conclusion: 非互易吸收表明电偶极与磁偶极激发之间存在强干涉；所观测的光学各向异性为探测反铁磁序的面内取向提供了途径，可用于稀土铁硼酸盐中反铁磁序参量的成像与时间分辨研究。

Abstract: We investigated the influence of antiferromagnetic (AFM) order on the near-infrared optical re- sponse of SmFe3(BO3)4 by polarization-resolved, magneto-optical spectroscopy. Below the Néel temperature, we observed pronounced magnetic linear dichroism at the 4f -4f transitions of Sm3+. By rotating the order parameter of the easy-plane AFM ground state using magnetic fields, we demonstrated the magnetic field control of linear dichroism, and deduced polarization selection rules. In addition, we detected non-reciprocal directional dichroism for several 4f -4f transitions. The non-reciprocal absorption appears in the symmetry-allowed toroidal geometry when the in- plane magnetic field is normal to one of the two-fold rotation axes. These results indicate a strong interference between electric- and magnetic-dipole excitations for transitions showing non-reciprocal absorption. The observed optical anisotropies provide a promising path to detect the in-plane orien- tation of the AFM order, enabling imaging and time-resolved studies of the AFM order parameter in rare-earth ferroborates.

</details>


### [121] [Mechanism of Incommensurate Magnetic Order in $\mathrm{BaCo_2(AsO_4)_2}$: Interplay of Frustrated Further-Neighbor Exchanges and Bond-Directional Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00626)
*Mohammad-Hossein Zare,Mehdi Biderang,Hamid Mosadeq*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展的 \(J_1\text{--}K\text{--}\Gamma\text{--}\Gamma'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3\) 模型研究 \(\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2\) 的零场非公度磁序起源，表明该磁序源于交换阻挫与键间各向异性的协同作用，而非异常强的 Kitaev 耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2\) 零场非公度磁序的微观机制尚不明确，尤其是键方向 Kitaev 型相互作用与交换阻挫的相对贡献存在争议，需澄清其微观起源。

Method: 构建包含 XXZ 型交换各向异性的扩展 \(J_1\text{--}K\text{--}\Gamma\text{--}\Gamma'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3\) 模型；最近邻参数由从头算电子结构约束；结合解析 Luttinger-Tisza 方法与扭曲边界条件下的精确对角化，绘制 \((J_2, J_3)\) 参数空间的经典与量子相图。

Result: 沿 \(\Gamma\to M\) 方向传播的非公度螺旋相的稳定并不需要异常主导的 Kitaev 耦合，而由进一步近邻耦合 \(J_2, J_3\) 驱动的交换阻挫与中等偏对角键各向异性 \(\Gamma, \Gamma'\) 的协同作用自然产生；量子情形下该非公度流形与面外铁磁 \(\mathrm{FM}_z\) 相竞争，后者通过量子序由无序机制被选择性稳定。

Conclusion: 研究调和了关于 \(\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2\) 磁相互作用相互矛盾的解释，确立了其非公度基态的微观起源与稳定范围。

Abstract: The microscopic mechanism governing the zero-field incommensurate magnetic order in the honeycomb cobaltate $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ remains a significant unresolved problem, particularly concerning the relative contributions of bond-directional Kitaev-type interactions and exchange frustration. This study investigates an extended $J_1\text{--}K\text{--}Γ\text{--}Γ'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3$ model, which incorporates XXZ-type exchange anisotropies on the honeycomb lattice. Nearest-neighbor parameters are constrained by ab initio electronic structure calculations. By integrating the analytical Luttinger-Tisza approach with exact diagonalization calculations under twisted boundary conditions, we delineate the classical and quantum phase diagrams across the $(J_2, J_3)$ parameter space. We demonstrate that the stabilization of the experimentally observed incommensurate spiral phase, propagating along the $Γ\to M$ direction, does not necessitate an anomalously dominant Kitaev coupling. Instead, this phase arises naturally from the synergistic interplay between exchange frustration, driven by further-neighbor couplings ($J_2, J_3$), and intermediate off-diagonal bond anisotropies ($Γ, Γ'$). In the quantum regime, we elucidate the competition between this incommensurate manifold and an out-of-plane ferromagnetic ($\mathrm{FM}_z$) phase, which is selectively stabilized via a quantum order-by-disorder mechanism. Our findings reconcile conflicting interpretations of the magnetic interactions in $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ and establish the microscopic origin and stability range of its incommensurate ground state.

</details>


### [122] [The half-filled optical Su-Schrieffer-Heeger-Hubbard model with uniaxial strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00681)
*Jonah Huang,James Neuhaus,Benjamin Cohen-Stead,Steven Johnston,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用量子蒙特卡洛研究单轴应变下SSH-Hubbard模型,发现各向异性跃迁将键序波(BOW)简并度从四降到二,并给出半满相图、BOW临界温度及方向依赖的谱隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变可通过改变键长、跃迁振幅和晶格对称性直接调控电子相,使电子-声子耦合系统成为研究应变效应的理想平台。SSH模型描述电子与调制跃迁的晶格自由度之间的耦合,预期受应变场强烈影响,但其在应变下的半满相图和有限温度行为尚不清楚,需要系统刻画。

Method: 以各向异性跃迁建模单轴应变,打破90°旋转对称性;在SSH电子-声子耦合基础上加入在位Hubbard U以引入反铁磁关联。采用行列式量子蒙特卡洛(有限温度)计算半满时的相图,在半满(e-ph耦合与Hubbard耦合)平面上确定BOW形成的临界温度,并通过解析延拓(最大熵方法)计算谱函数。

Result: 单轴应变使BOW相简并度由四种模式降为两种;半满时SSH相互作用在低温驱动主导的BOW关联,短键(高动能)与长键(低动能)交替;Hubbard U促进反铁磁关联,SSH耦合本身也弱驱动反铁磁。给出了e-ph与Hubbard耦合平面上的半满相图、BOW临界温度,并在BOW相中观察到方向依赖的谱隙形成。

Conclusion: 应变是调控SSH型电子-声子系统中BOW有序的有效手段:各向异性跃迁既降低基态简并又诱导方向依赖的能隙,且BOW与反铁磁关联在Hubbard耦合下相互竞争/共存,为通过应变工程调控关联电子相提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Strain offers a direct route to control electronic phases by altering bond lengths, hopping amplitudes, and lattice symmetries. These aspects make electron-phonon (e-ph) coupled systems a natural setting to study strain-induced effects. The Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) Hamiltonian describes the coupling between itinerant electrons and lattice degrees of freedom that modulate hopping and is thus expected to be strongly affected by strain fields. At half-filling and on a bipartite lattice, the SSH interactions drive dominant bond-order-wave (BOW) correlations at low- temperature in which short bonds with high kinetic energy alternate with long bonds with low kinetic energy along four possible patterns. We model uniaxial strain through anisotropic hopping, which breaks the $90^\circ$ rotational symmetry and reduces the BOW phase degeneracy to two. We also include an on-site Hubbard U that promotes antiferromagnetic correlations that are also (weakly) driven by the SSH interaction itself. We map out the half-filled phase diagram in the plane of the e-ph and Hubbard couplings, and determine the critical temperature of BOW formation. Using analytic continuation, we also evaluate the spectral function and demonstrate directionally dependent gap formation in the BOW phase

</details>


### [123] [Local geometry of the Fermi surface and its effect on the electronic characteristics of normal metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00810)
*N. A. Zimbovskaya*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文考察费米面几何中的精细特征（近圆柱形、近抛物形条带及局部平坦化）对金属电子性质（主要是电流磁效应）的影响，指出这些特征在特定条件下会显著改变常规金属或层状导电结构的响应方式，且效应强烈依赖扰动传播方向与外加磁场方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究费米面几何中的细微结构特征（而非整体形貌）是否以及如何主导金属的电子输运行为，特别是在高频外场扰动下的响应，从而为理解常规金属与层状导电材料的异常电磁响应提供几何层面的解释。

Method: 方法上，以费米面的局部几何特征（近圆柱形条带、近抛物形条带、局部平坦化区域）为出发点，理论分析其对外部高频扰动下电子响应的影响，重点考察电流磁（galvanomagnetic）性质；并对效应相对于扰动传播方向和/或外加磁场方向的敏感性进行讨论，同时探讨实验观测的可行性。

Result: 结果表明，在特定条件下，这些费米面精细特征能够显著改变常规正常金属或具有金属导电性的层状结构对高频外部扰动的响应方式；所考察的全部效应均对扰动传播方向和/或外加磁场方向表现出很强的敏感性。

Conclusion: 结论是费米面的精细几何特征可作为调控金属高频电磁响应的关键因素，相关效应具有明显的方向选择性，文中还讨论了观测这些效应的实验可能性，为后续实验验证指明了方向。

Abstract: Fine features in the Fermi surface geometry, such as nearly cylindrical or nearly paraboloidal strips or local flattenings, are examined as regards their effect on the electronic, mainly galvanomagnetic, properties of metals. It is shown that under certain conditions, these features may significantly change the way a conventional normal metal or a layered structure with metallic conductivity responds to high- frequency external disturbances. All of the effects considered appear to be very sensitive to the disturbance propagation direction and/or to that of the external magnetic field. Experimental possibilities of observing the described effects are discussed.

</details>


### [124] [Robustness of the Verwey transition against remanent strain-induced defects in magnetite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01337)
*M. A. Gala,K. J. Grzywa,K. Komędera,R. Zalecki,A. Baczmański,Z. Kąkol,A. Pacanowska,C. M. N. Kumar,R. Rodriguez Lamas,C. Detlefs,C. Yildirim,C. Marin,J. Debray,G. Beutier,L. Ortega,A. Kozłowski,N. Barišić,J. E. Lorenzo,W. Tabiś*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合暗场X射线显微术（DFXM）与交流磁化率测量，研究单轴压缩及卸载在化学计量比单晶磁铁矿中产生的应变诱导缺陷对Verwey转变的影响，发现线状扩展缺陷与残余应变场并不改变转变温度与转变陡度，从而区分了点缺陷与扩展滑移缺陷的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁铁矿Verwey转变是对晶格不完美和外部扰动高度敏感的电子驱动相变。掺杂、氧非化学计量和辐照点缺陷均会降低转变温度$T_{\rm V}$，但单轴应力产生的残余应变场与扩展缺陷结构的作用此前基本未被探索，因此需要明确扩展缺陷是否也像点缺陷一样抑制Verwey转变。

Method: 对化学计量比单晶磁铁矿施加单轴压缩后卸载，沿同一$[011]$方向加载两个样品：加载至$200~\mathrm{MPa}$产生稳定线状缺陷与残余应变场，超过断裂阈值加载则产生更致密缺陷网络和机械断裂；另测沿$[001]$压缩断裂样品的交流磁化率。采用暗场X射线显微术（DFXM）进行实空间成像、晶格取向映射和残余应变映射，并结合交流磁化率测定$T_{\rm V}$及转变陡度。

Result: DFXM实空间成像、晶格取向映射和残余应变映射显示压缩后存在显著残余结构无序；但所有压缩或断裂样品的Verwey转变温度$T_{\rm V}$及转变陡度均无可测量变化。该行为与点状无序对$T_{\rm V}$的强烈抑制形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 塑性变形产生的扩展线状缺陷和残余应变场不会可测量地扰动驱动Verwey转变的宏观电子有序，说明磁铁矿中点缺陷与扩展滑移相关缺陷对Verwey转变的影响存在本质区别。

Abstract: The Verwey transition in magnetite is a benchmark electronically driven phase transition that is highly sensitive to lattice imperfections and external perturbations. Doping, deviations from oxygen stoichiometry, and irradiation-induced point defects are known to lower the Verwey transition temperature, $T_{\rm V}$. By contrast, the role of remanent strain fields and extended defect structures generated by uniaxial stress has remained largely unexplored. Here we combine dark-field x-ray microscopy (DFXM) and ac magnetic susceptibility to determine how strain-induced defects affect the Verwey transition in stoichiometric single-crystalline magnetite after uniaxial compression and unloading. The DFXM measurements were performed on two samples compressed along the same $[011]$ direction: loading to $200~\mathrm{MPa}$ generated stable line-like defects and remanent strain fields, whereas loading beyond the fracture threshold produced denser defect networks and mechanical fracture. To test the effect of a different loading geometry, we also measured the ac susceptibility of a sample fractured by compression along $[001]$. Real-space DFXM imaging, lattice-orientation mapping, and residual strain mapping show substantial remanent structural disorder after compression. Nevertheless, neither the transition temperature $T_{\rm V}$ nor the sharpness of the transition changes measurably in any of the compressed or fractured samples. This behavior contrasts with the strong suppression of $T_{\rm V}$ by point-like disorder. Our results show that extended line-like defects and remanent strain fields produced by plastic deformation do not measurably perturb the macroscopic electronic ordering responsible for the Verwey transition, thereby distinguishing the effects of point defects from those of extended slip-related defects in magnetite.

</details>


### [125] [Na$_2$Ir$^\text{IV}$Cl$_6$: The Missing Member of the Perfectly Cubic Vacancy Ordered $A_2$IrCl$_6$ Family, Another Potential $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ Ground State Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01371)
*Siddhartha Sankar Soren,Aditya Patra,Rounak Das,Ritwik Das,Tanmay Chanda,Jens Buck,Indra Dasgupta,Sugata Ray*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次成功合成并全面表征了具有fcc结构、奈尔温度1.91 K的无水Na2IrCl6单晶，纠正了以往认为Na基VODP铱酸盐无法稳定立方相反铁磁性的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空位有序双钙钛矿（VODP）铱酸盐是研究在位库仑斥力、晶体场、自旋轨道耦合以及Heisenberg与Kitaev竞争相互作用的理想平台。然而Na基VODP铱酸盐存在水合依赖的结构与磁性不确定性，立方Na2IrCl6的fcc反铁磁态一直未被稳定实现，其确切结构与磁基态长期存在争议。

Method: 采用合成手段制备无水Na2IrCl6单晶，并进行综合表征：利用结构表征确认立方fcc相，通过磁性测量确定反铁磁转变温度，借助热重分析（TGA）验证晶格水的缺失及热稳定性。

Result: 成功获得无水Na2IrCl6单晶，呈立方fcc结构，反铁磁转变温度为1.91 K。TGA证实无晶格水，晶体热稳定至453 K。该立方相与先前报道的"无水"单斜Na2IrCl6形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了Na基VODP铱酸盐中结构与物理性质的强关联，为探索潜在完美Jeff=1/2八面体及最近邻Ir离子对间强Kitaev相互作用提供了新的研究平台。

Abstract: Vacancy ordered double perovskites (VODPs) have been known for more than 100 years, but their exact crystal structures and magnetic ground state remained controversial till date. Anhydrous VODP iridates ($A_2$Ir$X_6$; $A$ = K$^+$, (NH$_4$)$^+$, Cs$^+$, Rb$^+$, and $X$ = F$^-$, Cl$^-$, Br$^-$), having $face-centered~cubic$ (fcc) antiferromagnetic ground state, offer a chemically well-defined platform to investigate the influence of on-site Coulomb repulsion, crystal field, spin-orbit coupling, and competing Heisenberg and Kitaev interactions. Interestingly, among these, the Na-based VODP iridates seem to possess an unusual hydration dependent structural and magnetic uncertainty. As a result, a cubic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ system having fcc antiferromagnetism could not be stabilized ever. Here, for the first time, we report the synthesis and comprehensive characterization of anhydrous Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ single crystals having fcc structure with antiferromagnetic transition temperature of 1.91 K. Thermogravimetric analysis establishes the absence of lattice water and also demonstrates thermal stability of the crystals up to 453 K. The cubic phase reported here contrasts with previously described 'anhydrous' monoclinic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ and highlights the strong structural and physical property relationship in Na-based VODP iridates. This new material offers an exciting playing ground for exploring another potential perfect $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ octahedra and the consequent strong Kitaev interaction within the nearest-neighbor (nn) pairs of Ir ions.

</details>


### [126] [Room-temperature Magnetoelastic Coupling in UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01551)
*Mingyu Xu,Tomasz Klimczuk,M. Brian Maple,Weiwei Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在铀基金属间化合物 UIr4Al15 中观察到略高于室温的巨磁弹耦合，晶格参数与键长在磁转变区出现显著异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子与结构自由度的相互作用是量子材料中众多涌现现象的基础，但金属体系中的磁弹耦合通常较弱，且局限于低温或微观尺度，亟需寻找具强耦合的新材料平台。

Method: 采用变温单晶 X 射线衍射与各向异性磁化率测量，直接分辨与磁取向耦合的细微结构畸变，并分析晶格参数与化学键长的变化。

Result: 在无晶体学对称破缺的情况下，磁转变区附近晶格参数和特定化学键长出现显著异常，显示晶体结构对磁取向具有异常敏感性。

Conclusion: UIr4Al15 是磁性与晶格畸变在近室温下强耦合的罕见平台，为磁响应量子材料与功能磁传感应用提供了新机遇。

Abstract: The interplay between electronic and structural degrees of freedom underpins many emergent phenomena in quantum materials, yet magnetoelastic coupling in metallic systems is typically weak and confined to low temperatures or microscopic length scales. Here, we report giant magnetoelastic coupling slightly above room temperature in the uranium-based intermetallic compound UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$. Using temperature-dependent single-crystal X-ray diffraction together with anisotropic magnetic susceptibility measurements, we directly resolve subtle but reproducible structural distortions coupled to magnetic alignment. Despite the absence of crystallographic symmetry breaking, pronounced anomalies emerge in lattice parameters and selected chemical bond distances near the magnetic transition region, revealing an unusual sensitivity of the crystal structure to magnetic orientation. The coupling enables direct probing of how atomic distances and local chemical bonding govern the electronic and magnetic states in a bulk intermetallic material. Our results establish UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ as a rare platform in which magnetism and lattice distortions are strongly intertwined near room temperature, opening new opportunities for magnetically responsive quantum materials and functional magnetic sensing applications.

</details>


### [127] [Mass-asymmetry-controlled exciton dressing and dissociation in a quantum lattice model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01594)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过Holstein-激子模型的精确对角化，揭示了极化材料中激子-声子干涉及激子极化子的非微扰相图：等质量时交换选择规则保护激子免于声子修饰，质量不对称则破坏该规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极化材料中，中性激子的电子与空穴形变势之和因电中性而抵消，使弹性激子-声子顶点被电子-空穴干涉正则化。作者希望确定这种干涉在非微扰情形下的最终命运，即激子是否以及如何被声子修饰。

Method: 采用Holstein-激子模型的精确对角化方法，通过参数化质量不对称性将其与小极化子原子极限解耦，构建包含内部修饰交叉（internal dressing crossover）和解离边界（dissociation boundary）的相图，并结合解析证明与数值验证。

Result: 证明并数值验证：等质量时，若声子源在电子-空穴交换下为奇（中性情形），对称激子基态因精确的交换选择规则而免受声子修饰；质量不对称增大时选择规则被破坏，激子获得强局域极化子云。解离边界由库仑束缚与极化子稳定化之间的全局能量平衡决定，几乎与内部修饰无关。

Conclusion: 保护激子免受修饰的对称性同时剥夺了其极化子稳定化，从而在强耦合下驱动其解离。该结果在晶格激子-极化子模型背景下讨论，并可解释质量对称与质量不对称极性半导体中激子谱线的锐线与宽线差异。

Abstract: In a polar material, a neutral exciton couples to phonons through the sum of the electron and hole deformation potentials. Because the total source vanishes by charge neutrality, the elastic exciton-phonon vertex is regularized by electron-hole interference. Here we determine the non-perturbative fate of this interference by exact diagonalization of a Holstein-exciton model. By parameterizing the mass asymmetry to decouple it from the small-polaron atomic limit, we map a regime map comprising an internal dressing crossover and a dissociation boundary. We prove analytically and verify numerically that for equal masses, the symmetric exciton ground state is protected from phonon dressing by an exact exchange selection rule, provided the phonon source is odd under electron-hole exchange (the neutral case). As mass asymmetry increases, this selection rule is broken and the exciton acquires a strong local polaronic cloud. We show that the dissociation boundary, conversely, is set by a global energy balance between Coulomb binding and polaronic stabilization, and is nearly independent of the internal dressing. Paradoxically, the very symmetry that protects the exciton from dressing denies it polaronic stabilization, driving it toward dissociation at strong coupling. We discuss these results in the context of lattice exciton-polaron models and their implications for sharp versus broad excitonic lines in mass-symmetric versus mass-asymmetric polar semiconductors.

</details>


### [128] [Why polar excitons stay sharp: parity protection of the center-of-mass recoil channel in exciton-phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01600)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文揭示Fröhlich激子-声子问题中被"冻结质心"处理忽略的质心反冲自由度，证明存在一个普适、无参数的1s→1s吸收通道，其线宽由电子-空穴相消干涉控制并随质量不对称度消失，从而否定了α作为唯一品质因子，并解释了钙钛矿异常窄线宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化物钙钛矿等极性半导体中Fröhlich相互作用是主导的电子-声子耦合，按理应导致显著展宽，但实验观测到的激子共振却异常尖锐（如27–40 meV线宽与理论预期不符）。标准"冻结质心"(frozen-center-of-mass)处理无法解释这一反常，作者认为其遗漏了决定性的运动学自由度——质心反冲。

Method: 恢复精确的激子质心(COM)反冲，在氢型Fröhlich模型框架内构建解析理论：推导1s→1s反冲吸收通道的动量q*=√(2M_ex ℏω_LO)/ℏ，计算其速率随N_LO(T)的标度；通过电子-空穴干涉分析反冲线宽γ_recoil_LO ∝ F_{1s,1s}(q*)²，并推导弹性修饰的精确抑制律S_X/S_ind=η²(6-η²)/5；随后对质量不对称材料(GaAs, η=-0.74)与质量对称材料(FAPbI₃, η=0; MAPbI₃, η=-0.11)进行定量对比，并考察1s→np等内部态改变通道。

Result: 反冲通道普遍开放且无参数、速率正比于N_LO(T)，但受相消干涉控制，线宽以γ_recoil_LO ∝ F_{1s,1s}(q*)²随质量不对称消失。质量不对称的GaAs中通道活跃(γ_recoil_LO=2.2 meV)；质量对称的FAPbI₃(η=0)和MAPbI₃(η=-0.11)中分别被压制至0.00和0.12 meV。因此实验中27–40 meV的钙钛矿线宽不能由COM反冲解释，须由内部态改变等非弹性通道承担，其中建设性、对η鲁棒的1s→np共振是当前模型下的首要候选。

Conclusion: Fröhlich常数α本身不足以作为品质因子；投影到关联激子后，真正控制激子-声子散射与展宽的参数是质量不对称度η、动量-半径比q*a_X以及Rydberg失谐(detuning)。该工作建立了散射区间层级（hierarchy of scattering regimes），并指出钙钛矿窄线宽源于反冲通道的干涉相消。

Abstract: In polar semiconductors the Fröhlich interaction is the dominant electron--phonon coupling, yet excitonic resonances in materials such as halide perovskites remain anomalously sharp. We show that standard frozen-center-of-mass treatments of the exciton--phonon problem miss the decisive kinematic degree of freedom: restoring the exact center-of-mass (COM) recoil reveals a universally open, parameter-free $1s\to1s$ absorption channel at recoil momentum $q_*=\sqrt{2M_{\rm ex}\hbarω_{\rm LO}}/\hbar$, whose rate scales as $N_{\rm LO}(T)$. We prove that this recoil channel is controlled by destructive electron--hole interference: the recoil linewidth vanishes with the mass asymmetry as $γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}\propto\mathcal{F}_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2$, and the elastic dressing obeys the exact suppression law $S_X/S_{\rm ind}=η^2(6-η^2)/5$ within the hydrogenic Fröhlich model. The theory establishes a hierarchy of scattering regimes. In mass-asymmetric materials (GaAs, $η=-0.74$) the recoil channel is active ($γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}=2.2$~meV); in mass-symmetric materials (FAPbI$_3$, $η=0$; MAPbI$_3$, $η=-0.11$) it is killed by interference (0.00 and 0.12 meV), showing that the observed 27--40 meV perovskite linewidths cannot be accounted for by COM recoil and therefore require internal-state-changing and other inelastic channels, of which the constructive, $η$-robust $1s\to np$ resonance is the leading candidate within the present model. The Fröhlich constant $α$ alone is therefore insufficient as a figure of merit: after projection onto the correlated exciton, the controlling parameters are $η$, $q_*a_X$, and the Rydberg detuning.

</details>


### [129] [Frustration-induced multiferroicity in hauerite MnS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01990)
*Vilmos Kocsis,Harish K. Singh,Kranthi K. Bestha,Yaqian Guo,Maxim Mostovoy,Jeroen van den Brink,Oleg Janson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: MnS₂在48 K以下形成共线反铁磁序，伴随晶胞倍增并打破反演对称性，产生最高360 μC/m²的铁电极化，从而成为第二类多铁材料；微观上极化源于S₂二聚体在相邻Mn位受挫磁序诱导下产生的偶极矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黄铁矿型矿物hauerite MnS₂这一磁性绝缘体在低温反铁磁序下的对称性破缺是否能够诱导铁电极化，从而确立其第二类多铁性并阐明其微观机制。

Method: 基于第一性原理/电子结构分析（结合对称性分析与微观机制建模），考察反铁磁序导致的晶胞倍增与反演对称性破缺，并计算电子极化及其微观来源。

Result: 反铁磁序打破反演对称性，产生最高达360 μC/m²的铁电极化；极化的微观来源是S₂二聚体在相邻Mn位受挫磁序诱导下获得偶极矩；时间反演对称性不破坏铁电畴，且畴翻转需克服由磁交换能标度决定的高能垒。

Conclusion: MnS₂被确认为第二类多铁材料，其铁电极化由受挫磁序通过S₂二聚体诱导产生；时间反演对称性保持铁电畴完整，而畴翻转受高能垒限制，体现了磁-电耦合的强关联特征。

Abstract: Pyrite-type mineral hauerite MnS$_2$ is a magnetic insulator with localized $S=\frac52$ moments. Below 48 K, it develops a collinear antiferromagnetic order, accompanied by unit-cell doubling and breaking the inversion symmetry. We demonstrate that the latter gives rise to a ferroelectric polarization of up to 360 $μ$C/m$^2$ rendering MnS$_2$ a type-II multiferroic. Microscopically, the electronic polarization originates from S$_2$ dimers that acquire a dipole moment induced by the frustrated magnetic ordering of neighboring Mn sites. We show that time-reversal symmetry leaves ferroelectric domains intact, and that domain switching necessitates overcoming a high energy barrier set by the magnetic exchange energy scale.

</details>


### [130] [Magnetic Phase Diagrams and Spin Hamiltonian of Monoclinic $α$-RuCl$_3$ from Angle-Dependent Torque Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02006)
*Daniel Antoniou,Danrui Ni,John S. Pearce,Robert J. Cava,Amalia I. Coldea,Radu Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过高灵敏压电悬臂力矩测量，系统研究了低温下保持单斜结构的亚100微米α-RuCl₃单晶的磁性相图，发现其与菱方样品存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多集中于较大单晶，其在150 K以下发生单斜到菱方的结构相变并于约7 K进入zigzag磁序。而高质量微小单晶在低温下仍保持单斜结构，磁转变温度为14 K，其磁性行为尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对亚100微米直径的高质量单晶，在最高16 T磁场下进行高灵敏压电悬臂力矩测量，使磁场在三个正交晶面内旋转，通过原始力矩数据及其对角度和磁场的高阶导数的异常来探测相变，并与考虑单斜对称性的平均场计算进行对比。

Result: 获得了三个正交晶面内的完整磁相图，显示出与菱方样品相比更低的旋转对称性、抑制自发磁序所需的高得多的磁场，以及不同的场诱导相。

Conclusion: 该工作为所有探索的相图区域提出了最小磁结构模型，并表明单斜对称性对α-RuCl₃的磁性有重要影响。

Abstract: The layered honeycomb $α$-RuCl$_3$ has been much explored as a candidate to display unconventional cooperative magnetism between spin-orbit entangled $j_{\rm eff}=1/2$ Ru$^{3+}$ magnetic moments stabilized by strongly-frustrated, Kitaev and other bond-dependent anisotropic interactions. Most studies so far have focused on relatively large single crystals that undergo a structural phase transition from monoclinic to rhombohedral upon cooling below 150 K and a magnetic transition to zigzag order around 7 K. Here we study the magnetism of high-quality, very small (sub 100 $μ$m diameter) single crystals, which remain monoclinic upon cooling to low temperatures and display a sharp magnetic transition at 14 K. Using highly-sensitive piezo-cantilever magnetic torque measurements in fields up to 16 T, we report a comprehensive study of the magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic field rotated in three orthogonal crystallographic planes. Our extensive torque data collected upon varying almost continuously the angular orientation of the field as well as the field magnitude, allows clear detection of phase transitions via anomalies in the raw torque data and its higher order derivatives with respect to both angle and field magnitude. The obtained magnetic phase diagrams show many differences compared to rhombohedral samples, displaying reduced rotational symmetry, substantially higher fields required to suppress the spontaneous magnetic order, and distinct field-induced phases. We provide a direct comparison of the torque data with mean-field calculations for magnetic Hamiltonians appropriate for $α$-RuCl$_3$ allowing for the monoclinic lattice symmetry and propose minimal magnetic structure models for all explored regions of the magnetic phase diagrams.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Self-Orthogonal $τ$-Tilting Modules and Tilting Modules III: Finitistic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00105)
*Xiaojin Zhang,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在 Artin 代数上研究 τ-tilting 模的自扩张消失条件，证明有限小 finitistic 维数加上 T 的自扩张消失范围有限可推出 T 是经典 1-tilting 模；并建立自正交 τ-tilting 猜想与 finitistic 维数猜想之间的蕴涵关系，进而验证 radical 立方为零的 Artin 代数情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文关注 τ-tilting 理论与经典 tilting 理论之间的联系，以及自正交 τ-tilting 猜想与 finitistic 维数猜想之间的相互关系，旨在用同调有限性条件刻画经典 1-tilting 模，并在特定代数类中验证该猜想。

Method: 设 A 为 Artin 代数，T 为右 A 模中的 τ-tilting 模，令 C=A/Ann_A(T)。通过考察 C^op 的有限小 finitistic 维数以及 T 的自扩张消失范围的有界性，利用 τ-tilting 理论与 tilting 模的经典刻画工具进行证明。

Result: 若 C^op 的小 finitistic 维数有限且 T 的自扩张消失范围有限，则 T 必为经典 1-tilting 模。此外，若 finitistic 维数猜想对所有 Artin 代数成立，则自正交 τ-tilting 猜想对所有 Artin 代数成立；反之，自正交 τ-tilting 猜想的反例将给出 finitistic 维数猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 作为应用，自正交 τ-tilting 猜想在 radical 立方为零的 Artin 代数上成立；该结果将两个重要猜想联系起来，并为特定代数类提供了验证。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an Artin algebra and let $T$ be a $τ$-tilting right $A$-module. Put $C=A/{\rm Ann}_A(T)$. We prove that finite little finitistic dimension of $C^{\rm op}$, together with a finite range of self-extension vanishing for $T$, forces $T$ to be a classical $1$-tilting module. We also show that if the finitistic conjecture holds for all Artin algebras then the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for all Artin algebras. Moreover, a counterexample to the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture will give a counterexample to the finitistic dimension conjecture. As an application, the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for Artin algebras with radical cube zero.

</details>


### [132] [A characterization of triangulated structures induced by premutation triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00135)
*Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明(MT4)兼容条件既是Iitsuka预三角结构成为三角结构的充分条件也是必要条件，并将其与Nakaoka同心孪生余挠对条件等价化，最后构造出非mutation triple的可约三元组，否定Iitsuka的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Iitsuka引入premutation triple统一各种mutation构造，并证明商范畴Z/[I]具有典范预三角结构；但该结构何时成为三角结构尚缺精确刻画，且其提出的reducible triple是否必为mutation triple的问题悬而未决。

Method: 在extriangulated范畴中考察premutation triple (S,Z,V)，令I=S∩Z=Z∩V；证明兼容条件(MT4)对Z/[I]的预三角结构升级为三角结构的必要性；对concentric twin cotorsion pairs，证明Nakaoka构造三角商所用的兼容条件与(MT4)等价；并显式构造一个可约但非mutation triple的三元组。

Result: (MT4)被证明为Iitsuka典范预三角结构成为三角结构的充要条件；Nakaoka的兼容条件与(MT4)等价；构造出一个reducible triple不是mutation triple的反例。

Conclusion: (MT4)精确刻画了Iitsuka预三角结构三角化的条件，统一了Nakaoka的构造；并否定性地回答了Iitsuka关于reducible triple与mutation triple关系的问题。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{V})$ be a premutation triple in an extriangulated category. Set $\mathcal{I}=\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{Z}=\mathcal{Z}\cap\mathcal{V}$. Iitsuka introduced premutation triples as a unified framework for various mutation constructions and showed that the quotient category $\mathcal{Z}/[\mathcal{I}]$ admits a canonical pretriangulated structure. In this paper, we prove that the compatibility condition $(\mathrm{MT4})$ is not only sufficient but also necessary for this structure to be triangulated. Therefore, $(\mathrm{MT4})$ precisely characterizes when Iitsuka's canonical pretriangulated structure becomes triangulated. Furthermore, we prove that, for concentric twin cotorsion pairs, the compatibility condition used by Nakaoka in the construction of triangulated quotients is equivalent to $(\mathrm{MT4})$. Finally, we construct an explicit reducible triple which is not a mutation triple. This gives a negative answer to a question posed by Iitsuka.

</details>


### [133] [Explicit Transgression Primitives on Flag Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00300)
*Greg Weiler*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了相对超渡原语 $\\alpha_j \\in (\\Lambda^{2j-1}\\mathfrak{m}^\\vee)^T \\cong {}^K\\Omega^{2j-1}(K/T)$，满足 $d_\\mathfrak{m}\\alpha_j = -p_j(F_\\mathfrak{t})$，给出对所有 $j\\ge 1$ 普适闭形式，揭示其结构转变并在 $A_2$ 格、Koszul DGA 分解上给出应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borel 定理以非构造方式给出旗流形上特征形式 $p_j(F_\\mathfrak{t})$ 的消没，而从 Weil 代数 $W(\\mathfrak{k})$ 投影得到的闭原语无法下降到 $K/T$。因此需要显式构造可下降的相对原语，以把消没变成可计算、可验证的机制。

Method: 引入相对超渡原语 $\\alpha_j \\in (\\Lambda^{2j-1}\\mathfrak{m}^\\vee)^T$；利用连接族 $\\theta_s = \\theta_\\mathfrak{t} + s\\theta_\\mathfrak{m}$，使曲率分解为 $F_s = (1-s)(F_\\mathfrak{t} - s\\theta_\\mathfrak{m}^2)$；通过非交换字和展开与 Euler Beta 积分推导出对一切 $j \\ge 1$ 的普适闭公式；并在 $A_2$ 根格上以 $T$-不变上链/闭回路、以及极小 Koszul DGA 分解 $K_\\bullet(p_2,\\dots,p_k)$ 进行分析。

Result: 得到显式原语并刻画其结构转变：$\\alpha_2 = -\\frac{1}{3}\\mathrm{tr}(\\theta_\\mathfrak{m}^3)$ 无曲率，$\\alpha_3$ 对 $F_\\mathfrak{t}$ 线性，而在 $j=4$ 时 $[F_\\mathfrak{t},\\theta_\\mathfrak{m}^2]\\neq 0$ 打破循环迹对称性，产生不可约不变量 $\\mathrm{tr}(\\theta_\\mathfrak{m} \\wedge F_\\mathfrak{t} \\wedge \\theta_\\mathfrak{m}^2 \\wedge F_\\mathfrak{t})$。在 $A_2$（$\\mathfrak{sl}_3(\\mathbb{C})$）上 $\\alpha_2 = \\Omega_- - \\Omega_+$ 为三角形有向差，$d_\\mathfrak{m}$ 作为边碎裂算子见证 $c_2(E)$ 的正合性。Koszul DGA 分解恢复了 Poincaré 多项式 $P_{K/T}(t)=\\prod_{j=2}^k \\frac{1-t^{2j}}{1-t^2}$ 与 Euler 特征 $\\chi(K/T)=k!$，避免了矩阵秩的模糊性。

Conclusion: 相对超渡原语给出了 Borel 消没的构造性证明：原语可下降至 $K/T$ 并显式满足 $d_\\mathfrak{m}\\alpha_j=-p_j(F_\\mathfrak{t})$；其随 $j$ 出现的对称性破缺（如 $j=4$ 的不可约不变量）反映了高阶非交换效应。Koszul DGA 分解进一步以无歧义方式恢复旗流形的 Poincaré 多项式与 Euler 特征，并克服了 Specht 分解中 $k=4$ 处特征理论的 multiplicity 障碍。

Abstract: Borel's theorem presents the flag manifold cohomology as the coinvariant algebra $H^*(K/T; \mathbb{C}) \cong S(\mathfrak{h}^\vee)/I_W$, establishing the vanishing of characteristic forms $p_j(F_\mathfrak{t}) = \mathrm{tr}(F_\mathfrak{t}^j)$ non-constructively; projection from the Weil algebra $W(\mathfrak{k})$ yields closed primitives on $K$ failing to descend.
  We construct relative transgression primitives $α_j \in (Λ^{2j-1}\mathfrak{m}^\vee)^T \cong {}^KΩ^{2j-1}(K/T)$ satisfying $d_\mathfrak{m} α_j = -p_j(F_\mathfrak{t})$ on $K/T$. Via connections $θ_s = θ_\mathfrak{t} + sθ_\mathfrak{m}$, the curvature factors as $F_s = (1-s)(F_\mathfrak{t} - sθ_\mathfrak{m}^2)$; expanding via non-commutative word sums and Euler's Beta integral yields a universal closed formula for all $j \ge 1$.
  These primitives exhibit a structural transition: $α_2 = -\frac{1}{3}\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m}^3)$ is curvature-free, $α_3$ is linear in $F_\mathfrak{t}$, while at $j = 4$, $[F_\mathfrak{t}, θ_\mathfrak{m}^2] \neq 0$ shatters cyclic trace symmetry into independent necklaces, producing the irreducible invariant $\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m} \wedge F_\mathfrak{t} \wedge θ_\mathfrak{m}^2 \wedge F_\mathfrak{t})$.
  On the $A_2$ root lattice ($\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$), $T$-invariant cochains correspond to closed loops, $α_2 = Ω_- - Ω_+$ is the oriented difference of triangles, and $d_\mathfrak{m}$ acts as an edge-fracturing operator witnessing the exactness of $c_2(E)$. While character theory encounters a multiplicity barrier at $k=4$ in Specht decompositions, a minimal Koszul DGA resolution $K_\bullet(p_2, \dots, p_k)$ resolves the coinvariant algebra, recovering the Poincaré polynomial $P_{K/T}(t) = \prod_{j=2}^k \frac{1-t^{2j}}{1-t^2}$ and Euler characteristic $χ(K/T) = k!$ without matrix-rank ambiguities.

</details>


### [134] [Magnitude of module categories and special biserial algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00413)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Børve、Horiatakis和Kalck关于表示有限代数模范畴magnitude的猜想：该magnitude至少等于单模个数，且等号成立当且仅当代数为special biserial。证明在代数闭域上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Børve等人定义了表示有限代数模范畴的magnitude，将其刻画为Auslander–Reiten箭图的Euler特征（顶点、箭与mesh的交替计数），并猜想它不小于单模个数，等号恰在special biserial代数时取得。该猜想此前未被证明，本文旨在解决这一问题，并借此揭示magnitude与代数结构之间的定量联系。

Method: 对表示有向（representation-directed）代数，建立模范畴、去掉给定单组成因子的子范畴及其理想商三者magnitude之间的关系；利用Ringel与Vossieck的hammock理论，将上述理想商等价为有限偏序集表示范畴，并计算其magnitude；一般情形则通过标准形的分次化所得代数约化到表示有向情形。

Result: 在代数闭域上证明了原猜想：表示有限代数的模范畴magnitude ≥ 单模个数，且等号当且仅当代数为special biserial。同时给出了表示有向情形中模范畴、剔除某单组成因子的子范畴与相应理想商三者magnitude的定量关系，并算出了由有限偏序集表示范畴实现的magnitude。

Conclusion: 该工作完全解决了Børve–Horiatakis–Kalck猜想，刻画了magnitude达到下界（单模个数）的代数类，并将magnitude计算归约到有限偏序集表示范畴的hammock框架，为表示有限代数的同调/组合不变量研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Børve, Horiatakis, and Kalck defined the magnitude of the module category of a representation-finite algebra as the sum of the entries of the inverse of the matrix of Hom dimensions between indecomposable modules. They showed it is an Euler characteristic, the alternating count of vertices, arrows, and meshes of the Auslander--Reiten quiver, and conjectured that it is at least the number of simple modules, with equality precisely for special biserial algebras. We prove this conjecture over an algebraically closed field. For a representation-directed algebra, we relate the magnitudes of the module category, the subcategory of modules without a given simple composition factor, and the ideal quotient by it. By the theory of hammocks of Ringel and Vossieck, this quotient is equivalent to a category of representations of a finite poset, whose magnitude we compute. The general case reduces to the representation-directed case through algebras obtained by grading a standard form.

</details>


### [135] [Big Finitistic Dimensions of Radical-Cube-Zero Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00433)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 构造了一个十维代数A，满足(rad A)^3=0、findim A=2、Findim A=∞、Findim(A^op)=0，从而否证大finitistic dimension猜想；并给出相关模的分类与若干Artin代数上的有限性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战/否证大finitistic dimension猜想（Findim），通过显式构造给出Findim=∞而单侧Findim(A^op)=0的反例，并刻画相关投射维数模的结构。

Method: 在任意域上构造十维代数A（rad^3=0）；用显式inverse-syzygy构造给出任意正有限投射维数的可数生成右模，逐步识别完整投射覆盖核，并用非零Ext检测解析长度；对嵌入投射模根中的投射维数≤1的模用可逆线性算子分类；对投射维数2的有限维模确定所有维数向量并分类达到尖锐维数界者（含全部十四维模）；对一般Artin代数给出通过radical-annihilated理想与合适corner控制有限投射分解的递归充分判据；对有限维elementary radical-cube-zero代数在两顶点各带一个loop情形证明大finitistic dimension有限，并给出界为1或2的乘法映射判据；最后用精确表示嵌入证明投射维数2类的wild性及每域上该类中的superdecomposable pure-injective模存在。

Result: 得到findim A=2、Findim A=∞、Findim(A^op)=0的十维代数反例，否证大finitistic dimension猜想；存在任意正有限投射维数的可数生成右模；完成投射维数≤1嵌入根中模的分类；确定投射维数2模的全部维数向量并分类达到尖锐界者及全部十四维模；给出Artin代数的递归充分判据；证明两顶点各一loop的elementary radical-cube-zero代数Findim有限并给出界1或2的乘法映射判据；证明投射维数2类wild且存在superdecomposable pure-injective模。

Conclusion: 大finitistic dimension猜想被一个rad^3=0的十维代数反例否证，说明Findim与单侧Findim可极端不对称；同时建立了投射维数≤1与2模的分类、Artin代数有限性判据，并揭示投射维数2类的wild性。

Abstract: We construct, over any field, a ten-dimensional algebra $A$ with $(\operatorname{rad}A)^3=0$ such that $\operatorname{findim}A=2$, $\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$, and $\operatorname{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})=0$. An explicit inverse-syzygy construction gives countably generated right modules of every positive finite projective dimension, disproving the big finitistic dimension conjecture. At each step we identify the full projective-cover kernel, and nonzero Ext detects the exact resolution length. For this algebra, we classify the modules of projective dimension at most one that embed in radicals of projective modules, with no generation restriction, in terms of invertible linear operators. We determine all dimension vectors of finite-dimensional modules of projective dimension two and classify those attaining the sharp dimension bounds, including all fourteen-dimensional modules of projective dimension two. For arbitrary Artin algebras, we give recursive sufficient criteria that control finite projective resolutions through radical-annihilated ideals and suitable corners. For finite-dimensional elementary radical-cube-zero algebras, we prove finiteness of the big finitistic dimension in the two-vertex case with one loop at each vertex and obtain multiplication-map criteria for bounds of one or two. Finally, an exact representation embedding yields wildness of the projective-dimension-two class and, over every field, a superdecomposable pure-injective module in that class.

</details>


### [136] [Endomorphism algebras of Gorenstein-projective modules over string algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00937)
*Yu-Zhe Liu,Zheng Xin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究弦代数的Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对，用极大好集合与有限相容图刻画，并在G条件下给出End_A(T)的带状曲面模型及表示有限性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦代数的τ-倾斜理论与曲面模型是表示论的核心工具，但Gorenstein-投射情形下的支撑τ-倾斜对的组合刻画及其自同态代数的曲面实现尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将弦代数A的Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对等同于一类特殊的极大好集合，并构造其有限相容图；在G条件下，对任意非零基本Gorenstein-投射模T，显式构造End_A(T)的带标号带状曲面模型。

Result: 建立了Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对与极大好集合、有限相容图的对应关系；在G条件下实现了End_A(T)的带状曲面模型，并证明A表示有限当且仅当对所有基本Gorenstein-投射模T，End_A(T)表示有限。

Conclusion: 该工作将支撑τ-倾斜的组合图论与曲面模型推广到Gorenstein-投射框架，给出了弦代数表示有限性的自同态代数判据。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a string algebra. We identify its Gorenstein-projective support $τ$-tilting pairs with a distinguished class of maximal good collections and give a finite compatibility graph for them. Under the G-condition, we construct an explicit labelled tiled-surface model for $\mathrm{End}_A(T)$ for every nonzero basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$. Under the same condition, we show that $A$ is representation-finite if and only if $\End_A(T)$ is representation-finite for every basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$.

</details>


### [137] [Homology and higher-order intermediate categories for proper abelian subcategories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01155)
*Zheng-Gang Wu,Xianhui Fu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在不依赖 t-结构的前提下，于有限扩张窗口上建立自然长正合同调序列，并证明该界的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长正合同调序列通常依赖 t-结构；本文旨在摆脱该依赖，在更一般的扩张封闭真阿贝尔子范畴框架下研究同调序列的存在性与界的最优性。

Method: 在含 ℓ+1 层扩张封闭真阿贝尔子范畴的有限扩张窗口上，假设 1 至 ℓ+1 次负自扩张消没，构造自然长正合同调序列；在 Krull-Schmidt 情形下利用相容序列对取纯同调层稳定的中间子范畴进行分类。

Result: 建立了有限扩张窗口上的自然长正合同调序列，证明了该界的统一最优性，并在 Krull-Schmidt 情形下完成了对纯同调层稳定中间子范畴的分类。

Conclusion: 无需 t-结构即可获得长正合同调序列，且所得界是紧的；中间子范畴可由相容序列刻画。

Abstract: Without needing a $t$-structure, we establish natural long exact homology sequences on finite extension windows with $\ell+1$ layers of extension-closed proper abelian subcategories, assuming vanishing of negative self-extensions in degrees $1,\ldots,\ell+1$, and prove uniform sharpness of this bound. In the Krull-Schmidt setting, we classify intermediate subcategories stable under taking pure homology layers by compatible sequences.

</details>


### [138] [Central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01159)
*Taeyeoup Kang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究零特征局部域和数域上Galois gerbe的中心代数上同调，系数取自连通约化群及代数中心子群（可为无限或不连通）。对可比于Kaletha刚性gerbe的情形给出abel化上同调的纤维积公式，并在数域上通过笛卡尔方块由abel化与实局部化恢复完整上同调集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galois gerbe的中心上同调在迹公式、Langlands对应及Kottwitz gerbe的局部-整体理论中具有核心作用，但既有工作多局限于有限中心子群，对无限或不连通的代数中心子群缺乏统一的上同调计算框架。本文旨在突破这一限制，以Kaletha的刚性gerbe为比较基准，建立一般性的结构定理。

Method: 以Kaletha刚性gerbe为参照构造比较态射；对存在此类比较的gerbe，将abel化上同调表示为有限中心商的代数基本群与从band到中心子群的同态之纤维积；在数域情形引入实局部化，构造从完整上同调集到其abel化与实局部化之笛卡尔方块；最后将公式特化到Kottwitz局部与整体gerbe。

Result: 对附带来自Kaletha刚性gerbe比较态射的gerbe，得到abel化上同调的纤维积公式，其因子为有限中心商的代数基本群和band到中心子群的同态；此类比较在band为有限型时恒存在。数域上不依赖比较假设，完整上同调集可由其abel化与实局部化经笛卡尔方块恢复。非阿基米德局部域与全虚数域上abel化映射为双射。

Conclusion: 该结果统一并推广了Galois gerbe中心上同调的计算，将band有限型作为比较存在的充分条件，并揭示了数域上"abel化+实局部化"的完备不变量结构；对Kottwitz局部与整体gerbe的特化给出其中心上同调的具体描述，为相关算术对偶与迹公式应用提供工具。

Abstract: We study central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes over local fields of characteristic zero and number fields, with coefficients in a connected reductive group and an algebraic central subgroup, not necessarily finite or connected. For gerbes admitting a comparison morphism from Kaletha's rigid gerbe, we obtain fiber product formulas for abelianized cohomology in terms of algebraic fundamental groups of finite central quotients and homomorphisms from the band to the central subgroup. Such comparisons exist whenever the band is of finite type. Over number fields, independently of the comparison hypothesis, the full cohomology set is recovered from its abelianization and its real localizations through a cartesian square. Over non-Archimedean local fields and totally imaginary number fields, abelianization is bijective. We also specialize the formulas to Kottwitz's local and global gerbes.

</details>


### [139] [Harmonic Norms on Quiver Representations over Non-Archimedean Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01694)
*Oren Ben-Bassat*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明：在任意完备非阿基米德域上，任何有限箭图的polystable表示都允许HKKP意义下的split调和范数，从而回答HKKP预印本中的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKKP在其《Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry》中提出问题：polystable表示是否具有split调和范数。该问题此前仅在受限情形下有答案，作者希望去掉球完备性、值群和箭图等限制。

Method: 利用Moret-Bailly闭像定理给出与不稳定子对象的一致分离；对范数权重作有限估计得到稳定表示的线性强制性界，从而在全体范数的完备CAT(0)空间上得到扩展HKKP势的最小值；通过一阶变分计算将split极小元等同于HKKP调和范数；用严格支撑铰链归约将split可达性化归为带任意实半径标签箭图的半稳定归约；对提升分裂作微小扰动使反向扩张非分裂，并用有限斜率—秩下降证明终止。

Result: 每个完备非阿基米德域上有限箭图的polystable表示都存在split调和范数；完备范数空间中的每个最小值都由具有相同箭头奇异剖面的split范数达到，且不依赖值群或箭图的限制。非split极小元例示了一次和二次半径修改下的split可达性，双参数Berkovich解析族区分了所选split范数的polystability、semistability与harmonicity。

Conclusion: 该工作完整回答了HKKP的问题，建立了非阿基米德域上polystable箭图表示、split调和范数与半稳定归约之间的对应，并为更一般的非阿基米德几何稳定性理论提供了工具。

Abstract: Let $K$ be any complete non-Archimedean field, not necessarily spherically complete. We prove that every polystable representation of a finite quiver over $K$ admits a split harmonic norm in the sense of Haiden--Katzarkov--Kontsevich--Pandit (HKKP), answering a question from their preprint \emph{Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry}. Moret-Bailly's closed-image theorem gives a uniform separation from representations with destabilizing subobjects. A finite estimate on norm weights then yields a linear coercivity bound for stable representations and hence a minimum of the extended HKKP potential on the complete CAT(0) space of all norms. A direct first-variation computation identifies split minimizers with HKKP harmonic norms. Strict supporting hinges reduce split attainment to semistable reduction for quivers with arbitrary real radius labels. Small changes of the lifted splitting make each reversed extension nonsplit; a finite slope--rank descent proves termination. Consequently, every minimum in the completed norm space is attained by a split norm with the same arrow singular profiles, without restrictions on the value group or the quiver. Nonsplit minimizers illustrate split attainment in one and two radius modifications, and a two-parameter Berkovich analytic family separates polystability, semistability, and harmonicity of a chosen split norm.

</details>


### [140] [Rotation fixed points of undecorated braid varieties and fusion rings of affine Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02034)
*Yuma Mizuno*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文证明：与Coxeter元素幂对应的无装饰辫子簇上由字循环旋转诱导的Zamolodchikov变换，其不动点栈的粗模空间同构于相应仿射李代数融合环的谱；结论可推广至扭曲Coxeter元素，并由此得到所有仿射李代数的融合环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立辫子簇上的有限阶自等价（Zamolodchikov变换）与仿射李代数融合环之间的几何联系，统一刻画各类仿射李代数的融合环。

Method: 设G为单连通半单代数群，考察Coxeter元素幂对应的无装饰辫子簇，利用字循环旋转诱导的Zamolodchikov变换这一有限阶自等价，计算其不动点栈的粗模空间，并与相应仿射李代数融合环的谱作同构比较；方法推广至扭曲Coxeter元素。

Result: 不动点栈的粗模空间同构于对应仿射李代数融合环的谱；该同构对扭曲Coxeter元素亦成立，从而可通过此构造得到所有仿射李代数的融合环。

Conclusion: Zamolodchikov变换的不动点几何为融合环提供了统一的几何实现，覆盖全部仿射李代数情形。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simply connected semisimple algebraic group. On the (undecorated) braid variety associated with a power of a Coxeter element, there is a finite-order autoequivalence, called the Zamolodchikov transformation, induced by cyclic rotation of words. In this paper, we show that the coarse moduli space of the fixed-point stack of this autoequivalence is isomorphic to the spectrum of the fusion ring of the corresponding affine Lie algebra. The above results also extend to twisted Coxeter elements, and the fusion rings of all affine Lie algebras are obtained in this way.

</details>
