### [141] [Three New Likely Spider Millisecond Pulsar Binaries and Optical Kinematic Tracers of Intrabinary Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00455)
*Jay Strader,Rebecca Kyer,Laura Chomiuk,Elias Aydi,Peter Craig,Thomas M. Do,Isabella Molina,Teresa Panurach,Subhroja Roy,Kirill V. Sokolovsky,Samuel J. Swihart,Ryan Urquhart*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过多波段跟踪观测未关联Fermi伽马源，发现三个蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星候选体，其中两个为强红背候选体，并首次证明H-alpha发射可来自环绕脉冲星的双星内激波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未关联的Fermi-LAT伽马射线源可能隐藏着蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星系统，需要通过光学和X射线多波段跟踪观测来确认其对应体并研究其物理性质。

Method: 对未关联Fermi-LAT伽马源进行多波段跟踪观测；使用SOAR 4.1米望远镜获取光学光谱，使用Swift获取X射线数据；通过光谱双星分析测定轨道周期和质函数，并分析H-alpha发射线的径向速度相位。

Result: 发现三个蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星候选体。两个强红背候选体分别对应4FGL J1020.4-5314和4FGL J1408.7-3747，位于伽马源定位椭圆中心附近，为光谱双星，轨道周期0.15-0.3天，质函数0.6-0.9太阳质量。4FGL J1020.4-5314在两年内所有光谱中显示明亮且极宽（FWHM 2100 km/s）的H-alpha发射，其径向速度相位与次星吸收线速度存在偏移，符合靠近主星的发射源预期。第三个候选体与4FGL J1429.8-0739关联较弱，具有30.5小时轨道周期、可能的亚巨星次星和高X射线光度。

Conclusion: 首次证明对于任何蜘蛛系统，H-alpha发射可关联于环绕脉冲星的双星内激波，为利用光学发射线作为部分红背中双星内激波的运动学示踪开辟了可能性。第三个候选体虽关联不确定，但其特殊性质值得进一步研究。

Abstract: We report the discovery of three candidate spider millisecond pulsars via multi-wavelength follow-up of unassociated Fermi Large Area Telescope gamma-ray sources. All have optical spectroscopy from the SOAR 4.1-m telescope and X-ray data from Swift. Two optical/X-ray sources are exceptionally strong redback candidates, sitting close to the centres of the localization ellipses of the gamma-ray sources 4FGL J1020.4-5314 and 4FGL J1408.7-3747. We show that both optical sources are spectroscopic binaries with orbital periods (0.15-0.3 d) and mass functions (0.6-0.9 Msun) typical for redbacks. 4FGL J1020.4-5314 shows bright, very broad (full width at half maximum 2100 km/s) H-alpha emission in all spectra obtained over two years. Remarkably, the H-alpha radial velocities are offset in phase from the absorption-line velocities of the secondary, and match the signal expected for an emission source near the primary. This demonstrates, for the first time for any spider, that the H-alpha emission can be associated with an intrabinary shock that wraps around the pulsar, opening up the possibility of using optical emission lines as kinematic tracers of the intrabinary shock in some redbacks. A third candidate spider is less secure in its association with the gamma-ray source 4FGL J1429.8-0739, but has unusual properties, including a 30.5-hour orbital period, a potential subgiant secondary, and a high X-ray luminosity, making it worthy of further investigation.

</details>


### [142] [XClass: An Automated Multiwavelength Machine-Learning Pipeline for Classification of Extragalactic X-ray Sources. I. Pipeline Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00459)
*Blagoy Rangelov,Nicholas Moore,Haley Dutton,Erika Marentes*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: XClass是一个端到端机器学习流水线，将河外X射线点源分为AGN、LMXB、HMXB、CV、前景恒星与超新星遗迹等七类，通过SED翻译解决光度异质性，采用两阶段随机森林，在11,374源上达到99.6%准确率和0.90平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chandra在近邻星系探测到的大多数X射线源缺乏天体物理分类，而训练源多为银河系内、测光来自宽视场巡天（PanSTARRS、2MASS），河外目标却需HST成像且滤光片系统不重叠，存在光度异质性这一核心难题。

Method: 构建XClass流水线：先对每个训练源拟合类别适配的光谱模型，并将最佳拟合模型卷积通过HST滤光片曲线，生成统一特征空间中的合成星等（SED翻译）；分类器采用非对称两阶段随机森林，Stage 1区分AGN、X射线双星、SNR、恒星四大类，Stage 2利用包含Stage 1概率的增强特征向量将X射线双星细分为LMXB与HMXB；训练集由十个银河系星表与河外SNR星表构建，并与Chandra Source Catalog v2.1交叉匹配，特征包括X射线硬度比、SED翻译得到的HST颜色及X射线与光学流量比，且限定训练源至少有一个光学星等以避免插补伪影。

Result: 在11,374源的光学基准集上达到99.6%准确率与0.90平衡准确率，校准极佳（ECE = 0.002）。

Conclusion: XClass具有模块化、可推广至任意HST滤光片配置的特点，并将在姊妹论文中应用于M31与M33。

Abstract: Most X-ray sources detected in nearby galaxies by the Chandra X-ray Observatory lack astrophysical classifications. We present XClass (X-ray Classifier for extragalactic sources), an end-to-end machine-learning pipeline that classifies extragalactic X-ray point sources into seven classes: AGN, LMXBs, HMXBs, CVs, low-mass and high-mass foreground stars, and supernova remnants. The key challenge is photometric heterogeneity: training sources are predominantly Galactic with photometry from wide-field surveys (PanSTARRS and 2MASS), while extragalactic targets require Hubble Space Telescope (HST) imaging in a disjoint filter system. We address this through a spectral energy distribution (SED) translation that fits class-appropriate spectral models to each training source and convolves the best-fit model through HST filter curves, producing synthetic magnitudes in a common feature space. The classifier uses an asymmetric two-stage Random Forest: Stage 1 separates broad categories (AGN, X-ray binaries, SNRs, stars) and Stage 2 resolves X-ray binaries into LMXBs and HMXBs using an augmented feature vector that includes Stage 1 probabilities. The training set is assembled from ten Galactic catalogs and extragalactic SNR catalogs, cross-matched with the Chandra Source Catalog v2.1. Features include X-ray hardness ratios, SED-translated HST colors, and X-ray-to-optical flux ratios. We restrict the training set to sources with at least one optical magnitude, avoiding imputation artifacts; the pipeline achieves 99.6% accuracy and balanced accuracy of 0.90 on the resulting 11,374-source optical baseline, with excellent calibration (ECE = 0.002). XClass is modular, generalizable to any HST filter configuration, and will be applied to M31 and M33 in a companion paper.

</details>


### [143] [A Multi-Wavelength Picture of the Surprisingly Short 2024/25 Outburst from EXO 0748-676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00495)
*Amy H. Knight,Lauren Rhodes,Noel Castro Segura,Douglas J. K. Buisson,Aru Beri,David M. Russell,Kevin Alabarta,Maria C. Baglio,Jakob van den Eijnden,Matthew J. Middleton,Payaswini Saikia,Chris Done,Rob Fender,Fraser Lewis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: EXO 0748-676 在沉寂16年后于2024年3月爆发，但仅持续约10个月便于2025年1月回归沉寂。九台设备的射电到X射线多波段观测揭示其光变曲线不符合典型瞬变低质量X射线双星特征，X射线和光学通量保持相对稳定，整体表现为一次"失败的态转变爆发"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EXO 0748-676 在1985–2008年经历了一次长达23年的爆发后沉寂16年，于2024年3月意外再次爆发。其2024/25爆发在持续时间和行为上与此前爆发形成鲜明对比，为首次对该爆发进行多波段联合研究提供了契机，以理解其爆发机制与态转变性质。

Method: 汇集来自九台观测设备、覆盖从射电到X射线波段的资料，对2024/25爆发开展首个多波段研究。利用X射线（XMM-Newton、NICER）及光学数据构建光变曲线和光学相位曲线，并进行X射线颜色分析和光谱建模。

Result: 与1985年爆发不同，X射线和光学波段的上升与衰减均被完整记录。光变曲线不具备典型瞬变低质量X射线双星的标志性特征，X射线与光学通量在爆发期间保持相对稳定。光学数据显示出偏斜的相位曲线和辐照主导的光变曲线，直至外盘氢复合（约11000K）引发爆发末期快速衰减。XMM-Newton和NICER发现早期存在严重遮蔽，软X射线贡献受抑制，可用含团块状电离吸收体的光谱模型描述，但遮蔽的确切成因仍不确定。X射线颜色分析表明其大部分时间处于暗弱硬态，末期亮度略有上升但从未进入软态。

Conclusion: EXO 0748-676 的2024/25爆发表现出失败态转变爆发的特征——亮度仅小幅上升且未进入软态，区别于典型瞬变低质量X射线双星爆发，为理解此类天体的爆发与态转变机制提供了重要依据。

Abstract: After sixteen years in quiescence, EXO 0748-676 unexpectedly returned to outburst in March 2024. However, in stark contrast to its previous outburst (1985 - 2008), EXO 0748-676 returned to quiescence in January 2025, after ~ 10 months in outburst. Here, we collate data from nine observing facilities, covering wavelengths from radio to X-ray, to provide the first multi-wavelength study of the 2024/25 outburst. Unlike the 1985 outburst, both the rise and decay are fully captured at X-ray and optical wavelengths. The light curves do not exhibit the hallmarks of a canonical transient low-mass X-ray binary outburst. Instead, the X-ray and optical fluxes remain reasonably stable during the 2024/25 outburst. The wealth of optical data of EXO 0748-676 reveals a skewed optical phase curve and an irradiation-dominated optical light curve which lasts until the onset of hydrogen recombination in the outer disc at ~11000K, triggering a rapid fade at the end of the outburst. X-ray observations by XMM-Newton and NICER revealed that EXO 0748-676 was heavily obscured early on, with spectra exhibiting a dampened soft X-ray contribution. We find that a spectral model including a clumpy, ionised absorber describes the obscuration, although the exact cause of the early-time obscuration remains uncertain. X-ray colour analysis indicates that EXO 0748-676 spent most of the ~10 month outburst in a faint, hard state. EXO 0748-676 increased marginally in brightness towards the end of the outburst, but never entered a soft spectral state. This behaviour is characteristic of a failed transition outburst.

</details>


### [144] [X-ray Through Radio Observations and Modeling of the Soft X-ray Flash GRB 250419A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00508)
*Genevieve Schroeder,Anna Y. Q. Ho,Gavin P. Lamb,Tanmoy Laskar,Daniel A. Perley,Ore Gottlieb,Tomas Ahumada,Igor Andreoni,Aleksandra Bochenek,Michael Bremer,Michael Camilo,Jonathan Carney,S. Bradley Cenko,Alessandra Corsi,Michael W. Coughlin,James Freeburn,Erica Hammerstein,Rahul Jayaraman,Mansi M. Kasliwal,Noel Klingler,Brendan O'Connor,Cassie Sevilla,Gokul P. Srinivasaragavan,Robert Stein,Jada L. Vail,Sylvain Veilleux,Lin Yan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对X射线闪GRB 250419A开展了X射线、光学与射电多波段观测，发现其余辉与长暴群体相似但偏暗，并可由低动能、高洛伦兹因子喷流（含能量注入）解释，表明至少部分XRF是经典伽马暴向低能端的延伸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线闪（XRF）可由多种假想机制产生，如"脏火球"（重子负载显著的相对论喷流）和偏轴观测的伽马暴。要区分不同前身星场景，需要详细的多波段观测，但目前具备此类数据的XRF样本极少，因此急需新的多波段案例来约束其物理本质。

Method: 利用Space-based multi-band astronomical Variable Objects Monitor发现的XRF GRB 250419A（峰值能量Ep<4 keV），对其开展X射线、光学及射电（毫米至厘米波段）多波段观测，并对光变曲线与能谱（包括消色差拐折、射电双谱成分）进行建模分析。

Result: 在X射线和光学波段观测到明显的消色差拐折，射电辐射可能存在两个谱成分。余辉整体与长伽马暴群体相似但略偏暗，光学余辉在≲2天呈现异常平缓的衰减阶段。

Conclusion: 该事件可由低动能（E~10^49–10^50 erg）、高洛伦兹因子喷流解释，其中的能量注入可能源于径向分层结构和/或喷流被略微偏轴观测。由此推断，至少部分X射线闪是经典伽马暴向更低能量端的延伸。

Abstract: Soft X-ray flashes (XRFs) are predicted from a variety of hypothesized phenomena, including "dirty fireballs" (relativistic jets with significant baryon loading), and gamma-ray bursts viewed marginally off-axis. For a given burst, detailed multiwavelength observations are necessary for distinguishing between possible progenitor scenarios, but the number of XRFs with such data remains small. Here we present X-ray, optical, and radio (mm to cm) data of GRB 250419A, an XRF (with peak energy $E_p<4\,$keV) discovered by the Space-based multi-band astronomical Variable Objects Monitor. We observe a clear achromatic break in the X-ray and optical bands, and likely two spectral components in the radio emission. The afterglow is similar to the long-duration gamma-ray burst population, but slightly underluminous, and the optical afterglow has an unusually shallow decay phase at $\lesssim2\,$d. We find that the event is well explained by a high-Lorentz factor jet with low kinetic energy ($E\sim10^{49}$-$10^{50}\,$erg), with energy injection that could arise from radial stratification and/or the jet being viewed marginally off-axis. We conclude that at least some X-ray flashes are an extension of classical gamma-ray bursts down to lower energies.

</details>


### [145] [The X-ray and optical emission of the intermediate polar Swift J0614.0+1709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00612)
*Nikita Rawat,Domitilla De Martino,David A. H. Buckley,Koji Mukai,Martina Veresvarska,Zackery A. Irving,Simone Scaringi,Nicola Masetti*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次对中间偏振体Swift J0614.0+1709开展X射线与光学联合研究，确认其白矮星自转周期约1411 s，并揭示其X射线自转脉冲的能量依赖性与光学调制的时间变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swift J0614.0+1709作为候选中间偏振体缺乏详细的X射线与光学表征，需确认其磁性本质、自转周期、辐射机制及长期光学变率特征。

Method: 基于XMM-Newton定点观测，辅以TESS长期光学测光；开展X射线时序分析、相位分辨光谱与宽波段光谱拟合。

Result: 探测到约1411 s的白矮星自转相干调制；X射线自转脉冲随能量升高而调制幅度下降，相位分辨光谱表明自转变化主要由部分覆盖吸收体覆盖因子变化驱动；宽波段X射线谱可由温度约12 keV的部分吸收热等离子体加约63 eV软黑体成分描述，辐射热光度约5×10^34 erg/s，属较亮中间偏振体；TESS显示轨道与自转调制相对强度存在明显扇区间变化，拍频调制变化显著且在一个扇区中完全消失，轨道及自转调制幅度在更亮光学态有增强趋势。

Conclusion: Swift J0614.0+1709是一个X射线较亮的中间偏振体，具有由脉冲形态变化和反复增亮事件构成的复杂光学变率，是未来同步光学与X射线观测的有价值目标。

Abstract: We present the first detailed X-ray and optical study of the intermediate polar Swift J0614.0+1709 based on a pointed XMM-Newton observation complemented by long-term optical photometry from TESS. A strong coherent modulation at the white dwarf spin period was detected in X-rays, with a period of $\sim$1411 s, confirming the magnetic nature of the system. The X-ray spin pulse exhibits a pronounced energy dependence, with the modulation amplitude decreasing towards higher energies, consistent with phase-dependent absorption in the accretion flow. Phase-resolved spectroscopy further shows that the spin variability is primarily driven by changes in the covering fraction of the partial-covering absorber. The broadband X-ray spectrum is well described by a partially absorbed thermal plasma model with a temperature of $\sim$12 keV and a soft blackbody component at $\sim$63 eV. The inferred bolometric luminosity of $\sim$5$\times$10$^{34}$ erg s$^{-1}$ places the source among the more luminous intermediate polars. The TESS observations reveal substantial sector-to-sector variability in the relative strengths of the orbital and spin modulations. While the spin signal remains persistently dominant, the beat modulation varies significantly and disappears entirely in one Sector, suggesting temporal changes in the contribution of the reprocessing region(s). The orbital and, to a lesser extent, spin modulation amplitudes show some tendency to be stronger during brighter optical states, suggesting that changes in the mass transfer rate may influence the optical emission and modulation amplitudes. Overall, Swift J0614.0+1709 is a comparatively X-ray luminous intermediate polar exhibiting complex optical variability characterised by changes in pulse morphology and recurrent brightening events, making it a valuable target for future simultaneous optical and X-ray studies.

</details>


### [146] [AT2018hyz: Predictions for a Sky Projection from a Delayed Off-Axis Jet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00632)
*Ranadeep Ghosh Dastidar,Paul C Duffell*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟延迟离轴喷流的余辉，解释AT2018hyz的晚期射电增亮，并指出不同观测角与喷流发射延迟存在简并，需借助未来VLBI天图观测打破简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AT2018hyz在光学发现后972天才被射电探测到，并在1370-2160天出现晚期射电增亮，其成因可能是离轴喷流余辉或延迟的轻度相对论外流，需明确何种条件可解释该现象。

Method: 探索延迟离轴喷流模型，计算不同延迟时间t_delay与观测角theta_obs组合下的射电余辉，并估算喷流的天空投影尺度以打破参数简并。

Result: 不同t_delay与theta_obs组合均可产生与AT2018hyz相符的射电余辉；观测者越偏离喷流核心，推断的延迟越短，二者存在简并。天空投影图像尺度从离轴30°时的0.6 mas到垂直喷流核心时的0.3 mas。

Conclusion: 未来VLBI观测有望分辨这些结构；联合光变曲线与天空图像观测可约束AT2018hyz及其他带喷流TDE的观测角与喷流发射延迟。

Abstract: Initially discovered optically, Tidal Disruption Event (TDE) AT2018hyz was first detected in radio 972 days later. The continued monitoring of the event revealed a late-time (1370-2160 days) radio brightening. The source for this radio brightening has been associated with an afterglow originating from an off-axis jet or a delayed mildly relativistic outflow. In this work, we explored the conditions required for a delayed off-axis jet to explain the observed radio brightening. We find that the combined effect of different delay times (t_delay) and observer angles (theta_obs) can produce radio afterglow, as observed for AT2018hyz. The further off-axis from the jet core an observer is, the shorter is the delay inferred between the optical disruption and launching of the jet. This presents a degeneracy between different theta_obs and tdelay. We estimate the sky projection of the jet for these different combinations to break this degeneracy. The spread in sky images ranges from 0.6 mas for a 30 deg off-axis observer to 0.3 mas for an observer perpendicular to the jet core. Future VLBI observations should be able resolve these structures. Thus a combined observation of the light curve and sky images for AT2018hyz (and other jetted TDEs) can be used to narrow down the observer angle and any delay in launching of the underlying jet.

</details>


### [147] [Early Emergence of SSS emission in RS Oph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00762)
*J. -U. Ness,J. P. Osborne,M. Orio,S. Starrfield,S. Mitrani,E. Behar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过XMM-Newton对RS Oph 2021年爆发第26天左右的观测，发现其软X射线谱与2006年同期高度相似，包含快速变化的软成分与多个未证认谱线特征，谱形介于SSS的SSe与SSa类别之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RS Oph在2006年爆发早期Swift探测到超软源（SSS）相开始，XMM-Newton第26.1天观测到的软X射线谱含有无法用已知跃迁识别的瞬变窄发射特征，不同于典型SSS谱。作者希望借助2021年爆发同一阶段的新的XMM-Newton观测，与其他历元及相似新星对比，厘清这类异常软X射线谱的本质与分类。

Method: 分析2021年爆发与2006年可比阶段获得的新的XMM-Newton观测，并与2006年EPIC/pn低分辨谱及RGS高分辨数据、其他历元和其他相似新星（V1494 Aql、V1716 Sco、持续SSS Cal 87）的谱进行对比；采用黑体模型拟合检验谱形，分析激波成分与软成分的时变特性，并搜索35秒周期。

Result: 黑体模型能再现2006年低分辨EPIC/pn谱，但无法描述同期高分辨RGS数据。2021年谱与2006年高度相似，由缓慢衰减的激波成分和高度变化的软成分组成，软成分含若干未识别特征。激波辐射以天为时标演化，软成分以小时为时标变化并主导观测到的变率，其性质类似新星V1494 Aql与V1716 Sco，而不同于持续SSS Cal 87；未探测到显著的35秒周期性。

Conclusion: 低分辨观测会遗漏高分辨光谱揭示的复杂、线主导的辐射。快速变化的软成分，连同辐射复合连续谱、Fe X发射及此前探测到的35秒周期，提示存在隐藏的光电离源。这类奇异谱与V1494 Aql、V1716 Sco共同构成Ness等（2013）SSS分类中SSe与SSa之间的中间类别。未来需通过建模确定光电离、电荷交换等过程在该类中间SSS谱中的相对重要性。

Abstract: Early in the 2006 outburst of the recurrent nova RS Oph, Swift detected the onset of the super-soft-source (SSS) phase. An XMM-Newton observation on day 26.1 revealed an unusual soft X-ray spectrum containing transient narrow emission features that could not be identified with known transitions and are atypical of SSS spectra. We analyse a new XMM-Newton observation obtained at a comparable stage of the 2021 outburst and compare it with spectra from other epochs and with similar novae.
  A blackbody model reproduces the low-resolution 2006 EPIC/pn spectrum but fails to describe the simultaneous high-resolution RGS data. The 2021 spectrum closely resembles that observed in 2006 and consists of a slowly fading shock component and a highly variable soft component containing several unidentified features. While the shock emission evolves on timescales of days, the soft component varies on hourly timescales and dominates the observed variability. Its properties resemble those of the novae V1494 Aql and V1716 Sco, but not those of the persistent SSS Cal 87. No significant 35-s periodicity is detected.
  The results demonstrate that low-resolution observations can miss the complex line-dominated emission revealed by high-resolution spectroscopy. The rapidly variable soft component, together with radiative recombination continua, Fe X emission, and the previously detected 35-s period, suggests the presence of a hidden photoionising source. We conclude that these enigmatic spectra form an intermediate class between the SSe and SSa categories of Ness et al. (2013), together with V1494 Aql and V1716 Sco. Future modelling will be required to determine the relative importance of photoionisation, charge exchange, and other processes in shaping this class of intermediate SSS spectra.

</details>


### [148] [Optimal Pulsar Timing Array Strategies with the Deep Synoptic Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00836)
*Tyler Cohen,Paul B. Demorest,Jeremy G. Baier,Ryan S. Lynch,Scott M. Ransom,Michael T. Lam,Gabriella Agazie,Akash Anumarlapudi,Anne M. Archibald,Zaven Arzoumanian,Paul T. Baker,Ava L. Battaglia,Paul R. Brook,Anabelle L. Brueggemann,H. Thankful Cromartie,Kathryn Crowter,Megan E. DeCesar,Timothy Dolch,Elizabeth C. Ferrara,William Fiore,Emmanuel Fonseca,Gabriel E. Freedman,Nate Garver-Daniels,Peter A. Gentile,Joseph Glaser,Deborah C. Good,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,Ross J. Jennings,Megan L. Jones,David L. Kaplan,Matthew Kerr,Duncan R. Lorimer,Jing Luo,Hugo E. Marquis,Alexander McEwen,Maura A. McLaughlin,Natasha McMann,Bradley W. Meyers,Cherry Ng,David J. Nice,Timothy T. Pennucci,Benetge B. P. Perera,Nihan S. Pol,Henri A. Radovan,Paul S. Ray,Ann Schmiedekamp,Carl Schmiedekamp,Brent J. Shapiro-Albert,Ingrid H. Stairs,Kevin Stovall,Abhimanyu Susobhanan,Joseph K. Swiggum,Haley M. Wahl*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模拟脉冲星计时观测的框架，用于预测每颗脉冲星的最优仪器、观测频率和积分时间，并将其应用于 NANOGrav 的85颗脉冲星源列表及 GBT、VLA、CHIME、DSA 等望远镜，评估噪声来源并优化 GWB 探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA 实验已发现纳赫兹引力波背景的证据，未来观测将提升其显著性并揭示其前身天体。为充分利用下一代射电望远镜，需要在固定时间预算下优化脉冲星计时观测策略，以最大化 GWB 信噪比。

Method: 构建模拟脉冲星计时观测的框架，针对给定脉冲星集合与望远镜集合，预测单脉冲星最优仪器、观测频率与积分时间；将其应用于 NANOGrav 85颗脉冲星源列表（GBT、VLA、CHIME、DSA），分析主要噪声贡献；确定哪些脉冲星应使用 DSA 与 CHIME 或仅用 DSA 计时；在固定时间预算下求解最大化 GWB 信噪比的最优积分时间；并通过 PTA 的 Monte Carlo 实现进行评估。

Result: 多数脉冲星的计时精度并非由内禀脉冲相位抖动噪声主导；基于 DSA 的 PTA 比使用现有仪器的 PTA 更灵敏；对 DSA 观测的这些脉冲星而言，优化积分时间收益甚微；相比等时间分配方案，时间优化观测使 GWB 灵敏度提升约5%。

Conclusion: 所提框架可有效指导 PTA 观测策略制定，在早期科学固定源列表下，积分时间优化对 GWB 灵敏度的提升有限（约5%），表明等时间分配方案已接近最优。

Abstract: Pulsar timing array (PTA) experiments have seen evidence for a nanohertz-frequency gravitational wave background (GWB) through coordinated radio timing observations of millisecond pulsars. The significance of this evidence is expected to grow and the GWB's progenitor(s) uncovered with continued observations, especially as the next generation of radio telescopes comes online. We present a framework for simulating timing observations of a set of pulsars with a set of telescopes to predict the per-pulsar optimal instrument, observing frequency, and integration time. We apply this methodology to the NANOGrav source list of 85 pulsars observed with the Green Bank Telescope, Very Large Array, Canadian Hydrogen Intensity Mapping Experiment (CHIME), and the Deep Synoptic Array (DSA), a radio dish array under construction in Nevada. We discuss the dominant noise contributions to the timing precision of these pulsars and show that most are not dominated by intrinsic pulse phase jitter noise. We also determine which pulsars should be timed with DSA and CHIME or only DSA and show that an ab initio DSA PTA is more sensitive than one which uses current instruments. Finally, we solve for the optimal integration time per pulsar, subject to a fixed time budget, that maximizes the GWB signal-to-noise. We show that there is little benefit to optimizing over integration time for these pulsars observed with DSA. Through Monte Carlo realizations of the PTA, we show that the GWB sensitivity improves by $\sim 5\%$ for time-optimized observations compared to the equal-time-per-source scenario with an early-science, fixed source list.

</details>


### [149] [Towards Retrieval Augmented Generation in High-Energy and Astroparticle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00891)
*Jacky Kumar,Sajan Kumar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套开源的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，基于arXiv高能物理与天体粒子物理文献库，生成带引用的简明综述报告，助力研究者快速把握领域现状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理与天体粒子物理文献体量庞大且持续增长，研究者难以全面掌握已有工作与开放问题；尤其在狭窄主题上开展聚焦式文献调研十分困难，传统关键词检索工具（如arXiv、Inspire-HEP）已难以满足需求。

Method: 构建开源RAG流程：嵌入约23万篇hep-ph与astro-ph.HE论文，采用混合检索融合关键词匹配与语义相似度；用交叉编码器重排序精炼候选集；随后将论文传入大语言模型（Google DeepMind开源Gemma-4-E4B），由其先作为评判者评估相关性，再综合生成有依据、带引用的连贯报告。

Result: 该流水线能够针对任意细分方向生成简洁、聚焦、带引用的综述报告，使研究者、编辑与审稿人可快速定位并了解该领域的文献概况。

Conclusion: 这一AI驱动的方法在文献调研能力上超越了arXiv或Inspire-HEP上的传统关键词搜索，为高能物理与天体粒子物理领域的文献综述提供了新的可行途径。

Abstract: The high-energy and astroparticle physics literature has grown into an enormous body of knowledge. Gaining a comprehensive understanding of this literature is an important step in research, but is a challenging task. Before making meaningful advances, it is important to establish what has already been done and identify open questions. However, this process is becoming increasingly difficult given the sheer volume of publications. Conducting a focused literature survey on a narrow topic is particularly challenging. We develop an open-source Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline to produce concise, targeted summary reports with citations, grounded in the database of arXiv papers, enabling researchers, editors, and referees to rapidly orient themselves within any corner of the field. Specifically, we embed approximately 230K hep-ph and astro-ph.HE papers to enable a hybrid retrieval that captures both keyword matches and semantic similarity. The fused candidate set is then refined using a cross-encoder reranker. Following retrieval, the papers are passed to a Large Language Model (LLM), for which we use Google DeepMind's open-source Gemma-4-E4B model. The LLM first acts as a judge to evaluate the relevance and then synthesizes a coherent and grounded report with references. This modern AI-driven approach allows us to go beyond the capabilities of classical keyword search on arXiv or Inspire-HEP.

</details>


### [150] [High-energy neutrinos from shocked circumnuclear material around optically-bright and infrared-only tidal disruption events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00965)
*Mainak Mukhopadhyay,Shigeo S. Kimura,Tatsuya Matsumoto*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文半解析建模潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）驱动的亚相对论外流激波加速宇宙线并通过强子核作用产生中微子，发现致密尘埃遮蔽的红外TDE因辐射压缩使pp效率接近量热计极限，可贡献100 TeV弥漫中微子通量的约10%，并预测未来30 km³中微子天文台可通过联合堆叠搜索检验该情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDE外流激波注入核周介质（CNM）被视为宇宙线加速与高能中微子产生的潜在场所；近期发现的弱/无光学对应体的明亮红外暂现源暗示存在嵌于尘埃遮蔽核环境中的TDE，促使需要区分光学明亮与红外（IR-only）两类TDE的中微子产生效率，以解释弥漫中微子通量并指导搜索策略。

Method: 对光学明亮与IR-only TDE分别建模：半解析计算激波动力学、宇宙线加速与输运、以及中微子产生，纳入致密环境中的辐射冷却与压缩效应，从而评估强子核（pp）作用的效率与中微子产额。

Result: 光学TDE为低效pp中微子源、中微子产额较小；IR-only TDE中辐射压缩提高靶物质密度，使pp效率接近量热计区（calorimetric regime），在100 TeV处、考虑速率不确定度下可贡献高达约10%的观测弥漫中微子通量。中微子辐射持续时间长、单源产额低，因此适合电磁信息引导的联合堆叠搜索。对IceCube、IceCube-Gen2、KM3NeT与HUNT的搜索预测表明，未来约30 km³的中微子天文台对近邻（z ≲ 0.4）IR-only TDE、采用多年搜索窗口可达3σ灵敏度。

Conclusion: 不断增长的光学与红外TDE样本可支撑群体分辨的搜索，从而检验致密、遮蔽的核环境是否为高效高能中微子源。

Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) can launch sub-relativistic outflows that drive shocks into the surrounding circumnuclear material (CNM), providing a natural site for cosmic ray (CR) acceleration and high-energy neutrino production through hadronuclear ($pp$) interactions. We model this emission for optically bright and infrared (IR)-only TDEs, the latter motivated by a recently identified population of luminous IR transients with weak or absent optical counterparts, consistent with TDEs embedded in dusty, obscured nuclear environments. We semi-analytically compute the shock dynamics, CR acceleration and transport, and neutrino production, including the radiative cooling and compression in dense environments. While optical TDEs remain inefficient $pp$ neutrino sources with comparatively small neutrino yields, radiative compression in dense IR-only TDEs enhances the target density and allows the pp efficiency to approach the calorimetric regime, yielding up to $\sim 10\%$ of the observed diffuse neutrino flux at $100$ TeV, given the rate uncertainties. The long-lasting neutrino emission and low individual source yield motivate joint electromagnetically informed stacking searches. We forecast such searches for IceCube, IceCube-Gen2, KM3NeT, and HUNT, and conclude that a future $\sim 30\ {\rm km^3}$ neutrino observatory can reach $3σ$ sensitivity for nearby ($z \lesssim 0.4$) IR-only TDEs with multi-year search windows. Growing optical and IR TDE samples can therefore enable population-resolved searches that can test whether dense, obscured nuclear environments are efficient high-energy neutrino sources.

</details>


### [151] [Eight Gamma-Ray Bursts Observed with the Fermi Gamma-ray Burst Monitor: Significant Quasi-Thermal Components and Conditional Jet Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01061)
*Ze-lian Du,Zhao-Yang Peng,Jia-Ming Chen,Yue Yin,Ting Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对8个有红移的Fermi GBM伽马暴进行光谱分解与光球反演，发现喷流成分受发射半径、效率与分解模型强烈影响，无普适喷流类别或唯一非热机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽马射线暴喷流的物质成分（热与非热成分比例）仍不确定，且非耗散光球模型的参数反演结果对假设半径和光谱分解方式是否稳健缺乏系统检验。

Method: 从126个事件中按预设流量与谱判据筛选8个有红移的GBM伽马暴；对时间分辨谱比较加性黑体（BB）模型与CPL/Band拟合，用DIC及后验稳定性检验筛选；再用多色黑体加幂律（mBB+PL）分解估计mBB通量占比；最后应用H2013与G2015非耗散光球反演，在R0=21、100、1000 km下求解并剔除边界或物理不可行解。

Result: 稳定BB对比在215个合格时间bin中的203个给出ΔDIC>10（每个暴60–100%）；mBB+PL分解在215个bin中的214个给出F_mBB/F_tot>0.5。CPL–mBB相关性（r=0.83、0.78）强于Band–mBB（r=0.41、0.48），表明结果依赖模型。时间积分BB占比为0.005–0.311，8个中6个低于0.12。H2013在ε_Th>0.5下的有效解为1/2、1/4、2/8（R0=21、100、1000 km）；G2015在σ0<1下为0/1、1/4、1/8。小R0处大量无效解（21 km时分别6和7个）。在多半径均有效的暴中，R0增大一般降低ε_Th并提高σ0。

Conclusion: 喷流约束主要由发射半径、辐射效率和光谱分解方式决定，条件性强；未发现普适的喷流类别或唯一的非热辐射机制，光球反演结论不宜在缺乏半径与分解稳健性时作统一推断。

Abstract: The composition of gamma-ray burst (GRB) jets remains uncertain. We analyze eight Fermi Gamma-ray Burst Monitor (GBM) GRBs with reported redshift information, selected from 126 events by prespecified fluence and spectral criteria. After excluding compound-model DIC evaluations classified as unstable by the posterior-stability tests, a stable added-blackbody (BB) comparison gives $Δ\mathrm{DIC}>10$ in 203 of 215 accepted bins (60--100\% per burst); independently, a multicolor-blackbody plus power-law (mBB+PL) decomposition gives $F_{\rm mBB}/F_{\rm tot}>0.5$ in 214 of 215 bins. The cutoff-power-law (CPL)--mBB correlations ($r=0.83$ and 0.78) exceed the Band--mBB correlations ($r=0.41$ and 0.48), demonstrating model dependence. Time-integrated BB fractions span 0.005--0.311, with six of eight below 0.12. We then apply the non-dissipative photosphere prescriptions of \citet{2013A&A...551A.124H} (H2013) and \citet{2015ApJ...801..103G} (G2015). After excluding boundary or physically inadmissible inversions, the H2013 numbers with $ε_{\rm Th}>0.5$ are 1/2, 1/4, and 2/8 among valid solutions at $R_0=21$, 100, and 1000~km, respectively; the corresponding G2015 numbers with $σ_0<1$ are 0/1, 1/4, and 1/8. The large number of invalid solutions at small $R_0$ (six and seven at 21~km for H2013 and G2015) is itself evidence that those assumed radii are incompatible with the adopted inversion for most events. Among bursts having valid solutions at more than one radius, increasing $R_0$ generally lowers $ε_{\rm Th}$ and raises $σ_0$. Thus launch radius, efficiency, and spectral decomposition dominate the conditional jet constraints; no universal jet class or unique non-thermal mechanism is inferred.

</details>


### [152] [Superorbital Phase Evolution and Identification of New Anomalous Low State Candidates for Her X-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01123)
*Yi Chou,Jun-Lei Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于35年多仪器X射线观测，用Rank-Searching方法系统研究Her X-1的35天超轨道调制，识别异常低态并刻画长期超轨道相位演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Her X-1的35天超轨道调制长期缺乏客观、可复现的异常低态（ALS）判定标准，且其超轨道相位的长期演化及对应吸积盘物理机制尚不清晰，需要系统性的长期观测分析。

Method: 利用35年多仪器X射线观测基线，开发以主高态峰值强度为依据的Rank-Searching方法识别ALS；进而刻画长期超轨道相位演化并用一阶倾斜盘框架解释。

Result: 方法复现已知ALS，识别两个此前未确认的候选事件ALS 1991与ALS 2015，并独立复现新近报道的ALS 2025；发现七个交替平滑与可变行为的时期，平滑成分符合三次趋势，可变时期特征时标约3400天；相位长期演化对应累积盘质量增加约1.8%、外盘半径扩张约0.9%，短期相位偏移对应盘质量变化≲2.4%；粘性扩散时标约168天；主高态与短高态相位关系稳定、轮廓高度相似。

Conclusion: 弯曲盘在整个观测基线上保持 remarkably 稳定的全局几何结构，一阶倾斜盘框架可自洽解释Her X-1的超轨道相位长期演化与短期变化。

Abstract: The 35-day superorbital modulation of the X-ray binary Her X-1 was systematically investigated using a 35-year multi-instrument X-ray observational baseline. We developed a Rank-Searching method using the peak intensity of the main high state to identify anomalous low states (ALSs) with an objective and reproducible criterion. The method recovers the known ALSs, identifies two previously unrecognized candidate episodes, ALS 1991 and ALS 2015, and independently recovers the recently reported ALS 2025. We then characterized the long-term superorbital phase evolution, identifying seven epochs that alternate between smooth and variable behavior. The smooth component is empirically described by a cubic trend, while the recurrent variable epochs have a characteristic timescale of approximately 3400 days. Within a first-order tilted-disk framework, the secular phase evolution corresponds to an inferred cumulative disk-mass increase of $\sim1.8\%$ and an outer-disk-radius expansion of $\sim0.9\%$, while the short-term phase excursions can be reproduced by disk-mass variations of $\lesssim2.4\%$. A viscous diffusion timescale of approximately 168 days provides a characteristic timescale for the short-term disk response. Finally, the stable phase relation and high profile similarity between the main and short high states indicate that the warped disk maintains a remarkably stable global geometry over the observational baseline.

</details>


### [153] [25 Years of Mrk 421 with XMM-Newton: Unveiling Structured Jets and Energy-Dependent Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01214)
*Tek P. Adhikari,Gopal Bhatta,Sangeetha Kizhakkekalam,Navaneeth P K,Alex Markowitz,Zhicheng He,Santanu Mondal*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于XMM-Newton/pn近25年76个观测时段，对TeV耀变体Mrk 421进行长期X射线能谱与时序分析，揭示其光变、谱态转换及喷流结构约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过长期多历元X射线观测刻画Mrk 421的流强变化与能谱演化规律，理解高同步峰耀变体相对论喷流的长期行为特征。

Method: 使用2000—2025年间XMM-Newton/pn在IMAGING与TIMING模式下获取的76个历元数据，开展能谱拟合（幂律、折断幂律、对数抛物线）、硬度比（HR）、分数变异性及流量分布分析。

Result: 流量变化约一个量级，呈现明显"越亮越硬"趋势但HR存在离散；硬软波段分数变异性紧密相关且硬段更陡；流量分布呈对数正态型非高斯分布；59个历元由BPL拟合最佳、17个由LP拟合最佳；BPL折断能量集中于约2 keV，折点上方平均谱陡化ΔΓ≈0.19。

Conclusion: BPL与LP的交替指示辐射区在加速激波前沿与下游随机湍流之间转换；轻微谱陡化不支持标准单区冷却模型，支持结构化喷流或能量依赖电子逃逸情景。

Abstract: We present a long-term X-ray spectral and timing analysis of the TeV blazar Mrk~421 based on nearly a quarter century of archival \textit{XMM-Newton}/pn observations obtained between 2000 and 2025. Our sample comprises 76 epochs obtained with the pn instrument in both IMAGING and TIMING modes, allowing us to trace the source's variability and spectral evolution. Mrk~421 exhibits flux variations by approximately an order of magnitude, with prominent flaring periods interspersed with low-activity states. A clear harder-when-brighter trend is observed, accompanied by scatter in the hardness ratio (HR), suggesting flux alone does not uniquely determine the spectral state. Fractional variability in the hard band ($2-10$ keV) correlates tightly with that in the soft band ($0.3-2$ keV), with a steeper slope indicating pronounced variability at higher energies. The flux distribution is non-Gaussian and lognormal-like, dominated by moderate flux levels with a tail of bright flares. Spectral analysis shows that power-law model is not sufficient; 59 epochs are best described by a broken power-law (BPL) and 17 by a log-parabola (LP). This fluctuation between BPL and LP models suggests the emission region transitions between a distinct acceleration shockfront and downstream stochastic turbulence. The spectral break energy of BPL remains predominantly clustered around $2$ keV across all flux states, while the average spectral steepening above the break is mild ($ΔΓ\approx 0.19$), inconsistent with standard one-zone cooling models. Our results support structured jet scenarios or energy-dependent electron escape, providing key constraints on the long-term behavior of relativistic jets in high-synchrotron-peaked blazars.

</details>


### [154] [Detection of kilosecond hard lags in the new pulsating ULX candidate NGC 7456 ULX-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01444)
*W. Leone,F. Pintore,C. Pinto,N. O. Pinciroli Vago,A. Sanna,A. Wolter,T. Di Salvo,R. Iaria,A. D'Aì,A. Anitra,R. Soria,P. Esposito,F. Barra,E. Ambrosi,S. Caserta,C. Salvaggio,R. Salvaterra,G. L. Israel,S. Banerjee,M. Marelli,G. Rodriguez-Castillo,L. Burderi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析NGC 7456 ULX-1的两期XMM-Newton观测，首次在该源中探测到显著的硬X射线延迟（约10^3 s），并将其解释为超爱丁顿吸积流中吸积率扰动内传与致密外流中光子扩散的共同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超亮X射线源（ULX）通常由致密天体上的超爱丁顿吸积驱动。虽已在若干ULX中探测到软X射线延迟，但硬延迟罕见且物理起源不清。作者希望通过对NGC 7456 ULX-1的时变与能量依赖性研究，约束X射线延迟的物理机制并探测超爱丁顿吸积流。

Method: 分析2018年与2023年两次最深的XMM-Newton观测。对硬（1-10 keV）与软（0.3-1 keV）光变曲线采用针对泊松低计数数据优化的自适应分bin技术进行互相关分析；在连续10 ks区间内测量延迟并考察其能量依赖性；同时用热辐射与Comptonization模型拟合能谱。

Result: 两次观测均探测到显著的硬X射线延迟，在快速光变阶段硬发射延迟约10^3 s，延迟主要由最低能量光子驱动。能谱拟合表明存在Comptonization主导的吸积流，包含较冷的延展外区与嵌入光学厚风中的更热致密内流。推断的内发射区半径较小，不利于中等质量黑洞吸积体解释。

Conclusion: 延迟的符号、幅度与能量依赖性均不支持标准混响（reverberation）机制。传播驱动的光变与光学厚风中的辐射转移共同作用，似乎在塑造超爱丁顿吸积流的时间特性中起主要作用。

Abstract: Context. Ultraluminous X-ray sources (ULXs) are thought to be powered, in many cases, by super-Eddington accretion onto compact objects. While soft X-ray lags have been detected in several ULXs, hard lags remain rare and poorly understood. Aims. We investigate the temporal and energy dependence of X-ray lags in NGC 7456 ULX-1 to constrain their physical origin and probe the super-Eddington accretion flow. Methods. We analyzed the two deepest XMM-Newton observations, taken in 2018 and 2023. Hard (1-10 keV) and soft (0.3-1 keV) light curves were cross-correlated using adaptive-binning techniques optimized for Poissonian low-count data. We measured lags over consecutive 10 ks intervals and investigated their energy dependence. Spectra were modeled using thermal and Comptonization models. Results. We detect significant hard X-ray lags in both observations, with the hard emission delayed by $\sim 10^3$ s during phases of rapid flux variability. The delays are primarily driven by the lowest-energy photons. Spectral modeling indicates a Comptonization-dominated flow comprising a cooler, extended outer region and a hotter, compact inner flow embedded in an optically thick wind. We interpret the delays as the combined effect of inward propagation of accretion-rate fluctuations and photon diffusion within the dense outflow. Fluctuations first enhance the soft-emitting outer regions and then propagate toward the hotter inner flow, where photons undergo stronger Comptonization before escaping with a kilosecond delay. The small inferred inner emitting radius disfavors an intermediate-mass black hole accretor. Conclusions. The sign, amplitude, and energy dependence of the delays disfavor standard reverberation. Propagation-driven variability coupled with radiative transfer in optically thick winds appears to play a major role in shaping the timing properties of super-Eddington accretion flows.

</details>


### [155] [Disk-Like Matter Outflow from a Kerr-Type Wormhole: Possible Observational Manifestations and Magnetic Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01457)
*Mikhail Piotrovich,Stanislava Buliga,Tinatin Natsvlishvili*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对一种假想的致密天体进行现象学研究：该天体具有可穿越的2型Kerr虫洞几何，连接两个渐近不同的区域。一端位于有吸积活动的活动星系核(AGN)环境，另一端位于无吸积的宁静星系核。被吸积物质可穿过虫洞喉部并从另一端喷出，由于携带角动量，外流主要呈赤道向，形成与黑洞周围常规吸积盘根本不同的盘状结构。文章讨论了多种动力学区域、可能的观测表现、热学性质、复合特征与磁场效应，并特别关注在无常规吸积盘情况下喷流形成的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨可穿越2型Kerr虫洞作为假想致密天体的现象学后果，特别是其两端处于不对称环境（一端活动吸积、一端宁静）时，物质穿越虫洞所产生的独特外流与盘状结构，为区分虫洞与黑洞的观测特征提供理论依据。

Method: 采用现象学方法，设定虫洞一端位于活动星系核(AGN)吸积环境、另一端位于宁静星系核；分析被吸积物质穿越虫洞喉部并在另一端喷出的过程。考虑基于外流速度相对于局部逃逸速度和轨道速度的多种动力学区域，区分弱场大半径的牛顿力学描述与喉部附近需用广义相对论和磁流体力学处理的强场区域，并讨论从吸积流内边缘继承的比能量与角动量、磁应力与能量提取过程对其的修正，以及虫洞内禀偶极磁场的影响。

Result: 由于流入物质携带角动量，从对侧虫洞喷出的外流主要呈赤道向，形成与黑洞常规吸积盘根本不同的盘状结构。文中讨论了该结构的可能观测表现、热学性质、复合特征与磁场效应，并特别分析了在无常规吸积盘情况下喷流形成的可能性。

Conclusion: 该假想虫洞系统可产生区别于黑洞吸积盘的独特赤道盘状外流，其观测特征、热学与磁场效应以及无吸积盘喷流形成机制值得进一步研究；喉部附近动力学须在广义相对论磁流体力学框架下处理，而外流则可用弱场大半径牛顿近似分类。

Abstract: We present a phenomenological study of a hypothetical compact object with a traversable 2 Kerr-type wormhole geometry connecting two asymptotically distinct regions. One mouth of the wormhole is assumed to reside in an active galactic nucleus (AGN) environment with ongoing accretion, while the second mouth is located in a comparatively quiescent galactic nucleus without accretion activity. In such a configuration, matter accreted near the AGN-side mouth may traverse the wormhole throat and emerge from the opposite mouth. Because the inflowing matter possesses angular momentum, the resulting outflow is expected to be predominantly equatorial, producing a disk-like structure fundamentally different from a conventional accretion disk around a black hole. Several dynamical regimes are considered depending on the outflow velocity relative to the local escape and orbital velocities. Possible observational manifestations, thermal properties, recombination signatures, and magnetic field effects are discussed. Special attention is given to the possibility of jet formation in the absence of a conventional accretion disk. We explicitly distinguish the weak-field, large-radius Newtonian classification used for the outer flow from the strong-field region near the throat, where the dynamics should be treated in a general-relativistic and magnetohydrodynamic framework. We further discuss the specific energy and angular momentum inherited from the inner edge of the accretion flow, the possible modification of these quantities by magnetic stresses and energy-extraction processes, and the effects of an intrinsic dipolar magnetic field of the wormhole.

</details>


### [156] [Slingshot on compact binaries in the nuclear region of galaxies as the origin of large offset gamma-ray bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01850)
*Juan C. Rodríguez-Ramírez,Viviane Alfradique,Clécio R. Bom,Davi C. Rodrigues*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出引力散射机制：核区致密双星被中等质量黑洞或次级超大质量黑洞通过引力弹弓效应抛射，从而解释极端偏移的伽马射线暴/千新星事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到越来越多伽马射线暴距离其宿主星系中心投影偏移达数十千秒差距。若伴随千新星，通常归因于中子星诞生反冲（natal kick），但该机制可能不足以解释如此极端的偏移，故需探索替代通道。

Method: 考虑NS-NS、NS-BH、NS-WD三类双星族。解析推导双星在核区与IMBH/次级SMBH引力散射后的存活条件，并参数化碰撞后速度。随后数值积分双星质心在含主SMBH、恒星成分和暗物质晕的宿主星系势（椭圆型和螺旋型剖面）中的后弹弓轨道。

Result: 参数抽样显示：NS-NS和NS-WD双星通常比NS-BH系统在更远处并合；约30-45%的成功抛射事件在三维距离超过100 kpc处并合，这些极端偏移并合发生在抛射后约0.1-10 Gyr。

Conclusion: 该引力弹弓抛射机制可作为观测到的极端偏移暂现源（伽马射线暴/千新星）的候选形成通道。

Abstract: A growing number of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) have been observed at large projected offsets from the centres of their putative host galaxies, sometimes extending to several tens of kiloparsecs. When the GRB is associated with a kilonova, a compact binary with at least one neutron star is expected to be involved, and the cause of the offset can be attributed to the neutron star natal kick. In this work, we explore an alternative mechanism: the gravitational scattering of compact binaries populating the nuclear region of the host galaxy, by an intermediate-mass black hole or secondary supermassive black hole. We specifically consider populations of neutron star-neutron star (NS-NS), neutron star-black hole (NS-BH), and neutron star-white dwarf (NS-WD) binaries, and analyse the ejection of bound systems from the nuclear region through a gravitational-slingshot effect. We proceed analytically to derive the conditions for binary survival and to parametrise the post-encounter velocity. To quantify the associated merger offsets, we numerically integrate the post-slingshot trajectory of the binary centre-of-mass in host-galaxy potentials that include the primary SMBH, the stellar component, and the dark-matter halo, considering both elliptical-like and spiral-like galaxy profiles. Our parameter sampling shows that NS-NS and NS-WD binaries typically merge farther from the galactic centre than NS-BH systems, with approximately 30-45% of successful ejections merging at three-dimensional distances exceeding 100 kpc; these extreme-offset mergers occur within $\sim$0.1-10 Gyr after ejection. These results motivate the analysed mechanism as a candidate channel for observed extreme-offset transients.

</details>


### [157] [Recycling serendipitous observations of XRISM for dark matter searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01877)
*Lidiia Zadorozhna,Denys Malyshev,Oleg Ruchayskiy*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出XRISM/Resolve低计数率观测策略，在3.48-3.56 keV搜寻银河系晕keV质量暗物质辐射衰变线，给出比星系团观测更强的限制，并发现E=3.5311 keV约3σ残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: keV质量暗物质辐射衰变的搜寻长期受限于能量分辨率；需借助高分辨率谱仪提升探测灵敏度。

Method: 针对XRISM/Resolve设计低计数率观测策略，在3.48-3.56 keV能段搜寻银河系晕暗物质衰变线。

Result: 尽管曝光量更低，所得限制仍强于XRISM对星系团的观测；在E=3.5311 keV发现约3σ局部显著性残差，与既往报道的未识别特征吻合，且无合理原子或仪器对应物。

Conclusion: 该策略可给出更严格的暗物质衰变限制，且随更多指向观测的积累，限制将持续收紧。

Abstract: Searches for radiative decays of keV-mass dark matter have been limited by energy resolution. We propose a strategy for the XRISM/Resolve low count rate observations, and search the 3.48-3.56 keV band for a decay line from the Milky Way halo. Despite a lower exposure, our bounds are stronger than those from XRISM observations of galaxy clusters. We also identify a residual at E = 3.5311 keV with ~3sigma local significance, coincident with the previously reported unidentified feature, and having no plausible atomic or instrumental counterpart. These bounds will continue to tighten as additional pointings become available.

</details>


### [158] [Stationarity and angular momentum conservation in pulsar spin noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01929)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于双组分自旋模型分析脉冲星自旋噪声，发展可扩展高斯过程方法，用模拟数据证明两模型在全态观测下可检验区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星自旋噪声（自旋游走）的物理机制尚不明确，需要区分不同角动量动力学模型（非稳态扩散型 vs 稳态守恒型），但缺乏可扩展的检验方法。

Method: 提出物理驱动的双组分自旋游走模型：一是总角动量扩散的奇异非稳态模型，二是总角动量守恒的最小稳态模型；发展可扩展的高斯过程方法，并用模拟数据检验。

Result: 在全态观测下（同时独立获得壳层与超流体转动状态数据），两模型完全可检验且可区分；但仅当单组分可观测时，稳健推断与预测更难，且依赖先验和数据质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为检验脉冲星自旋噪声的稳态性开辟了路径，有望通过连续引力波与射电计时的联合观测实现。

Abstract: We present an analysis of pulsar spin noise based on physically-motivated two-component models of spin wandering. We focus on two models, distinguished by their total angular momentum dynamics: a singular, nonstationary model with a diffusive total angular momentum, and a minimal, stationary model anchored on a conserved total angular momentum. We develop scalable Gaussian process methods and, using mock data, show that the two models are fully testable and distinguishable with full-state observations, i.e., simultaneous independent data on the crust and the superfluid rotational states. This paves a path to testing stationarity in pulsar spin noise, potentially achievable with joint continuous gravitational wave observations and radio timing of pulsars. However, robust inferences and predictions are harder to achieve, and depend on the priors and data quality, when only one component is observationally accessible.

</details>


### [159] [Intrinsic versus observation induced spin nonstationarity in pulsar timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01978)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种高斯过程方法，利用脉冲星计时相位数据直接检验中子星自旋噪声的平稳性，比较双组分模型中角动量内部守恒（非平稳）与外部交换（平稳）两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双组分模型中，中子星自旋噪声是否平稳，取决于恒星角动量是在内部守恒，还是通过作用在壳层与超流体上的独立随机力矩与外界交换。该问题以两组分角速度来表述，但射电脉冲星计时只能通过拟合确定性计时模型后观测壳层的自转相位，因此需要一种能直接利用相位数据检验平稳性的方法。

Method: 从奇异非平稳模型与最小平稳模型的精确解析相位均值与协方差出发，证明壳层相位协方差具有半可分结构，构建随观测数线性标度的似然函数，并解析边缘化计时模型。对两模型的模拟相位数据进行分析，使用全状态观测与仅壳层观测，并做微分得到局域自旋频率。

Result: 积分、去除计时模型与微分既不产生也不破坏平稳与非平稳自旋噪声之间的区别，只决定在有限含噪数据集中有多少区别得以保留。当两组分均被观测时，两模型可被决定性地区分，且生成参数被恢复。仅观测壳层时，两模型在大部分参数空间对观测相位的描述同样好，贝叶斯因子量级为一且受先验限制。

Conclusion: 最小模型仍通过由超流体惯性分数设定的单一因子，将隐藏的超流体相位与观测壳层相位关联起来；因此，对该惯性分数的独立估计（如来自glitch）可使其成为可证伪预言，并可由对内部的连续引力波观测加以检验。

Abstract: Whether spin noise of a neutron star is stationary is, in two-component models, a question of whether stellar angular momentum is conserved internally or exchanged externally through independent stochastic torques on the crust and superfluid. The question is posed in the two components' angular velocities, but radio pulsar timing observes only the crust, through its rotational phase after a deterministic timing model is fitted. We develop a Gaussian process method for testing spin noise stationarity directly with such phase data. Starting from the exact analytical phase means and covariances of a singular, nonstationary and a minimal, stationary two-component model, we show that the crust phase covariances are semiseparable and construct a likelihood that scales linearly with the number of observations, with the timing model marginalized analytically. We analyze simulated phase data from both models using full-state and crust-only observations, and after differentiation into local spin frequencies. Integration, timing model removal and differentiation neither create nor destroy the distinction between stationary and nonstationary spin noise, but determine how much survives in a finite, noisy data set. With both components observed, the models are distinguished decisively and the generating parameters are recovered. With the crust alone, both models describe the observed phase equally well over much of parameter space, and the Bayes factors are of order unity and prior-limited. The minimal model nonetheless ties the hidden superfluid phase to the observed crust phase through a single factor set by the superfluid inertia fraction, so an independent estimate of this fraction, as from glitches, turns it into a falsifiable prediction that a continuous gravitational wave observation of the interior could test.

</details>


### [160] [The Impact of a Clumpy Ambient Medium on the Dynamics and Synchrotron Emission of AGN Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01983)
*Ivan Almeida,Christian Fendt,Bhargav Vaidya*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过3D相对论磁流体模拟，研究了pc尺度团块状星际介质对年轻AGN喷流早期演化和辐射的影响，发现团块介质显著增强质量加载并产生更亮、更不规则的同步辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGN相对论喷流预期穿过非均匀星际介质，但小尺度气体不均匀性在其早期演化中的作用尚不明确。

Method: 使用PLUTO进行3D特殊相对论磁流体模拟，追踪洛伦兹因子为10的喷流穿过pc尺度介质；对比均匀环境与两种团块环境模型（云占据体积填充因子0.1%和1%），并利用经扩散激波加速的拉格朗日宏粒子计算合成同步辐射图与光谱。

Result: 喷流-云相互作用使流动偏转、增强激波形成，并产生比均匀情况更不对称的茧；夹带质量从均匀模拟的0.43 M⊙增至团块模拟的5.3和8.8 M⊙；团块介质改变湍流与茧内物质混合，并产生更亮、更不规则的同步辐射；相同注入喷流下，团块模型产生更强的频率积分同步辐射，SED差异在亚毫米到红外/光学波段最大。

Conclusion: 纳入小尺度星际介质结构能更完整地描述年轻AGN喷流的早期动力学演化及其辐射特性。

Abstract: Relativistic jets from AGN are expected to propagate through an inhomogeneous ISM, but the role of small-scale gas inhomogeneities in shaping their early evolution remains uncertain. We investigate this problem using 3D special-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations with PLUTO, following a jet with a Lorentz factor of 10 propagating through a pc-scale medium. We compare a homogeneous ambient medium with two clumpy ambient-medium models, motivated by dense gas in the nuclear ISM, in which clouds occupy volume filling factors of $0.1$ and $1$ per cent. We compute synthetic synchrotron maps and spectra from Lagrangian macro-particles that have been accelerated by diffusive shock acceleration. Jet-cloud interactions deflect the flow, enhance shock formation, and produce a more asymmetric cocoon than in the homogeneous ISM case. Altogether, this results in a substantially stronger mass loading: the entrained mass increases from $0.43$ M$_{\odot}$ in the homogeneous run to $5.3$ and $8.8$ M$_{\odot}$ in the clumpy runs. The clumpy ISM also modifies the turbulence and the material mixing within the jet cocoon. The dynamical differences we find translate into brighter and more irregular synchrotron emission. For the same injected jet, the models with a clumpy medium produce stronger frequency-integrated synchrotron emission than the homogeneous run, with the largest differences in the SED occurring from the sub-mm to the infrared/optical bands. Our results demonstrate that including a small-scale ISM structure provides a more complete description of the early dynamical evolution of young AGN jets and their radiative properties.

</details>

### [161] [Integrating a Novel Kumaraswamy-Teissier Distribution with VARMA: A Hybrid Framework for Rainfall Modeling and Forecasting in the Northwest Himalayas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00060)
*Kamana Mishra,Neeraj Poonia,Tanmay Kayal,Sarita Azad*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出KTD-VARMA混合框架:先构建三参数Kumaraswamy-Teissier分布(KTD)刻画极端降雨,再以其作为正态化变换,结合VARMA模型捕获多站点时空依赖,实现山区降雨极值分析与更精确预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山区降雨建模需要能同时刻画强偏态与极端值的灵活统计工具;现有模型在极值拟合与多站点时空预报上存在不足,缺乏兼顾极值分析与预测的集成方法。

Method: 通过Kumaraswamy-G生成元构造三参数KTD分布,推导其统计性质,并采用极大似然、最大乘积间距及贝叶斯(MCMC)方法估计参数,给出可信区间与HPD区间;开展重现期分析;将KTD作为偏态降雨序列的正态化变换,对变换后数据建立VARMA模型以捕捉时空依赖,并进行季节调整后预测对比。

Result: KTD相对于其子模型拟合更优;重现期分析显示明显空间差异:Dehradun极端事件最频繁,Mandi与Kangra呈中等极值特征,Shimla与Nainital极端月总量重现期较长;经季节调整后,KTD-VARMA预测精度优于原始数据模型和单变量ARIMA,VARMA的RMSE最低。

Conclusion: KTD-VARMA框架首次提出,兼具极值分析与改进的多站点预报能力,为山区水文风险评估提供稳健方法。

Abstract: Rainfall modeling in mountainous regions requires flexible statistical tools capable of capturing strong skewness and extremes. This study proposes a novel hybrid KTD-VARMA framework to address this challenge. The framework first introduces the Kumaraswamy-Teissier Distribution (KTD), a new three-parameter model derived via the Kumaraswamy-G generator, to statistically characterize extreme rainfall. Its properties are derived, and parameters are estimated via maximum likelihood, maximum product spacing, and a Bayesian (MCMC) approach along with credible and HPD intervals. The KTD provides a superior fit compared to its sub-models. The return-period analysis highlights clear spatial contrasts: Dehradun experiences the most frequent extremes, Mandi and Kangra exhibit moderate extremal behavior, while Shimla and Nainital display long return periods for extreme monthly totals. Further, the KTD serves as a normalizing transformation for the skewed rainfall series and KTD-transformed data are then modeled using a Vector Autoregressive Moving Average (VARMA) model to capture spatio-temporal dependencies. This integrated methodology, presented here for the first time, yields more accurate forecasts after seasonal adjustment than models using raw data or univariate ARIMA, with VARMA achieving the lowest RMSE. The work establishes the KTD-VARMA framework as a comprehensive tool for both extreme value analysis and improved multi-site forecasting, providing a robust approach for hydrological risk assessment in mountainous regions.

</details>


### [162] [Counting the Uncounted: Population-Level Surveillance of Documented Pregnancy and Fetal Harm in Police Crash Narratives with a System One Model (Jev)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00213)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究首次在人口尺度上读取德州2017–2025年全部5,018,079条警方碰撞叙事，用正则筛选结合校准的AI决策模型（Jev）识别涉孕碰撞，估计出5,467起有记录的妊娠相关碰撞（95%区间5,306–5,577），并揭示漏报严重、记录与否主要取决于驾驶者自身受伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机动车碰撞是美国创伤性胎儿死亡的首要原因，但无碰撞数据库记录妊娠。既有估计依赖区间很宽的国家调查样本、仅覆盖伤者的创伤登记，或少数几个州的记录链接，缺乏人口尺度证据。警方叙事在事发现场只要相关就会记录妊娠，但尚无研究在人口尺度上阅读这些文本。

Method: 读取德州2017–2025年全部5,018,079条警方碰撞叙事；以正则表达式筛查出6,840条候选；由经校准的System One决策模型Jev按八问题schema读取，仅返回概率、不返回文本，确认5,333条记录妊娠当事人（含角色、孕周与碰撞后状况）；对剩余文本抽取499,306条做"是否存在"判读以估计漏检。以概率分层样本进行盲式人工裁决，得到加权灵敏度0.999、特异度1.000，再用Rogan-Gladen估计量与bootstrap构建置信区间。

Result: 确认5,467起碰撞有妊娠记录，95%区间为5,306–5,577，其中58起记录胎儿伤害；个体层面比率为每1,000名15–49岁女性驾驶者1.13–1.45起。与生命统计的活产期望相比，叙事仅记录到预期妊娠驾驶者的2.56%–3.09%；有记录状态与驾驶者自身有记录受伤关联最强，比值比达21.7。

Conclusion: 警方碰撞叙事可在人口尺度上用于识别涉孕碰撞，但记录的妊娠案例远低于应然水平（约3%），且记录与否高度依赖驾驶者本人是否受伤，提示现有数据对妊娠相关碰撞与胎儿伤害存在系统性漏报，需改进现场记录与数据结构。

Abstract: Motor-vehicle crashes are a leading cause of traumatic fetal death in the United States, yet no crash database records pregnancy. Existing estimates come from national investigation samples with wide intervals, trauma registries seeing only the injured, or record linkage in a few states. Police narratives record pregnancy whenever it matters at the scene, and no study has read them at population scale. We read every police crash narrative in Texas from 2017 through 2025, 5,018,079 in total. A regular-expression screen flags 6,840 candidates, which Jev, a calibrated System One decision model, reads under an eight-question schema, returning probabilities and no text. It confirms 5,333 as documenting a pregnant person involved in the crash, with role, stage and post-crash condition. The same model reads 499,306 of the remainder under the presence question, to estimate what the screen missed. Blind human adjudication of a probability-stratified sample gives weighted sensitivity 0.999 and specificity 1.000, from which the Rogan-Gladen estimator and a bootstrap give 5,467 crashes with a documented pregnancy, with a 95% interval of 5,306 to 5,577, including 58 crashes documenting fetal harm. Person-level records give a rate of 1.13 to 1.45 per 1,000 female drivers aged 15 to 49. Measured against a live-birth expectation from vital statistics, narratives document 2.56% to 3.09% of expected pregnant drivers, and documented-case status is most strongly associated with the driver's own recorded injury, at an odds ratio of 21.7.

</details>


### [163] [Efficient Convex Optimization Methods for State-Space Heartbeat Dynamics Models with Gamma Generalized Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00884)
*Sabrina Liu,Andrew S. Perley,Todd P. Coleman*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种心跳动力学的动态状态空间统计模型，其最优状态估计可用凸优化高效求解，并用于评估自主神经与心血管健康。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部平均方法难以捕捉心率模式中的急剧瞬变，需要一种能刻画心跳动力学并具有生理可解释性的高效统计建模框架。

Method: 构建状态空间模型：以高斯-马尔可夫过程作为先验，结合对数链接函数下的伽马发射；用静态模型估计参数，采用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）高效求解最大后验潜在状态估计；并利用偏自相关函数在模型规模与拟合优度之间权衡进行模型选择。

Result: 在合成心跳数据上，模型能较准确恢复潜在权重；在倾斜床数据集上，相比传统局部平均方法能更好捕捉心率模式的急剧瞬变；结合呼吸相位等其他器官信息，可提取信息论指标（如呼吸性窦性心律不齐）以量化器官间耦合。

Conclusion: 该心跳动力学统计框架高效且能提供具有生理意义的洞见，拓展了评估心跳动力学的潜在工具。

Abstract: Objective: This work proposes a dynamic state-space statistical model of heartbeat dynamics whose optimal state estimate can be solved efficiently with convex optimization and used to assess autonomic and cardiovascular health. Methods: This state-space model combines a Gauss-Markov process prior with gamma emissions using a log-link function. Parameters are estimated using a static version of the model, and alternating direction method of multipliers is used to efficiently find the optimal maximum a priori latent state estimate. Model selection is performed by finding the tradeoff between model size and model fit using the partial autocorrelation function. Results: We demonstrate in synthetically generated heartbeats that this model is able to closely recover the underlying latent weights. Then, we show using a tilt table dataset that this statistical model is able to better capture sharp transients in heart rate patterns than traditional local averaging methods. In addition, combining heart beat timings with information from other organs such as the respiratory phase, we show that our statistical modeling framework enables the extraction of information-theoretic measures to quantify inter-organ coupling such as respiratory sinus arrythmia. Conclusion: This statistical framework for modeling heartbeat dynamics provides an efficient framework that can provide physiologically meaningful insights. Significance: This work broadens potential tools that can be used to assess heartbeat dynamics.

</details>


### [164] [On relations between the Cochran--Mantel--Haenszel test and Augmented Inverse Probability Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01362)
*Ekkehard Glimm*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该文从因果推断视角重新诠释Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel (CMH)检验，证明其统计量可视为因果平均处理效应AIPW估计的标准化形式，并讨论了条件/无条件方差及检验含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验中因果推断方法日益普及，而经典CMH检验通常仅被解释为分层logistic回归中共优势比的检验，缺少因果推断框架下的理解，因此有必要重新审视CMH检验与因果估计量之间的关系。

Method: 针对具有二分类结局且各层处理分配比例相同的分层随机试验，作者将CMH统计量的分子表示为总体平均因果风险差的增广逆概率加权（AIPW）估计的成比例形式，并比较该估计量的条件方差与无条件方差概念，讨论相应的方差估计及其对检验的影响。

Result: 结果表明CMH统计量可解释为AIPW因果平均处理效应估计的标准化版本，不依赖于共优势比模型；在等处理分配下，CMH检验对零平均处理效应的弱原假设渐近保守，并厘清了经典条件CMH检验与Wald型AIPW检验之间的关系。

Conclusion: CMH检验在因果推断框架下具有不依赖共优势比模型的新解释，其与Wald型AIPW检验的关系得以明确；在等分配情形下CMH检验对弱原假设渐近保守，这为临床试验中检验方法的选择与解释提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This note revisits the Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel (CMH) test in light of the increasing use of causal inference methods in clinical trials. It shows that the CMH test statistic can be interpreted as a standardized version of the augmented inverse probability weighted (AIPW) estimate of the causal average treatment effect. Consequently, it is not restricted to an interpretation as a test of the common-odds ratio in a stratified logistic regression model. The note then discusses conditional and unconditional variances for this estimator as well as variance estimation and its implications for testing. The results clarify the relationship between the classical conditional CMH test and Wald-type AIPW tests, and show that, under equal treatment allocation, the CMH test is asymptotically conservative for the weak null hypothesis of zero average treatment effect. %This note revisits the Cochran--Mantel--Haenszel (CMH) test in light of the increasing use of causal inference methods in clinical trials. For stratified randomized trials with binary endpoints and a common treatment allocation fraction across strata, we show that the numerator of the CMH statistic is proportional to an augmented inverse probability weighted estimator of the population-average causal risk difference. This provides an interpretation of the CMH test that does not depend on the common-odds-ratio model often used to motivate it. We then compare conditional and unconditional variance concepts for this estimator and discuss their implications for testing. The results clarify the relationship between the classical conditional CMH test and Wald-type AIPW tests, and show that, under equal treatment allocation, the CMH test is asymptotically conservative for the weak null hypothesis of zero average treatment effect.

</details>

### [165] [Inverse Optimal Control with Convex Features and Box Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00033)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究凸特征下层目标中非负权重辨识的逆最优控制问题，建立乐观双层最优控制模型并给出良态性、可辨识性与稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向含非负权重的凸特征下层目标，需要通过逆最优控制反演权重；关键在于缺少活跃集可微性时仍能刻画解映射与稳定性。

Method: 将问题建模为乐观双层最优控制；下层为L^2中带箱约束的强凸线性约束控制问题。证明约化响应映射全局单值及显式Lipschitz估计；利用下层泛函对x的仿射依赖，证明值函数凹且Lipschitz，构造渐近精确最优值松弛；通过二阶增长的无梯度论证建立局部可辨识性与O(δ)噪声稳定性；KKT重构得到函数空间MPCC，Scholtes松弛在乘子一致有界下给出C-平稳性；并用解耦标量例给出严格互补证书。

Result: 得到约化响应映射的全局单值性与显式Lipschitz估计；值函数凹且Lipschitz，并给出渐近精确最优值松弛；证明局部可辨识性与O(δ)噪声稳定性；在乘子一致有界下得到C-平稳性；标量例给出严格互补性证书。

Conclusion: 该工作为逆最优控制中权重辨识提供非光滑活跃集情形下的良态性与稳定性理论，并借助MPCC与Scholtes松弛建立平稳性刻画。

Abstract: We study an inverse optimal-control problem for identifying nonnegative weights in a convex-feature lower-level objective. The resulting model is an optimistic bilevel optimal-control problem whose lower level is a strongly convex, linearly constrained control problem with box constraints in \(L^2\). For the reduced response map \(x\mapsto (y_x,u_x)\), we prove global single-valuedness and an explicit Lipschitz estimate, without assuming differentiability of active sets. Exploiting the affine dependence of the lower-level functional on \(x\), we show that the value function is concave and Lipschitz and derive an asymptotically exact optimal-value relaxation. We further establish local identifiability and \(O(δ)\) noise stability by a gradient-free argument based on second-order growth. Finally, a KKT reformulation yields a function-space MPCC; Scholtes relaxation gives C-stationarity under uniform multiplier boundedness. A decoupled scalar example provides an explicit strict-complementarity certificate via finiteness of threshold contacts.

</details>


### [166] [Improved upper bounds for five IHTC-2024 benchmark instances via exact reoptimization of the nurse-to-room layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00044)
*Alfonso Gippini Requeijo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对IHTC-2024基准，固定入院日、房间与手术室安排，仅对护士—房间—班次层重新优化至最优，报告了5个改进上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IHTC-2024的公开最优上界表可持续提交，作者希望检验已发布最先进解在护士排班层的质量，并改善已知上界。

Method: 固定已发布解中的入院日、房间分配与手术室分配，仅对护士—房间—班次分配子问题建模并精确求解至最优；所有解用官方IHTP验证器检验。

Result: 5个实例的上界改进：i02 1263（原1264）、i05 12744（原12760）、m01 3380（原3384）、m03 6692（原6697）、m04 3315（原3318）；改进幅度0.075%–0.125%，无硬约束违反。

Conclusion: 护士层可精确求解的子问题多数在数秒内闭合最优；部分已发布最先进解在该层未达最优，说明该层仍存在可挖掘的优化空间。

Abstract: The Integrated Healthcare Timetabling Competition 2024 (IHTC-2024) defined a benchmark that integrates surgical case planning, patient admission scheduling and nurse-to-room assignment. Its instance set remains live: the organizers keep a public table of best-known upper bounds that is open to new submissions. We report five improved upper bounds on that table, for instances i02 (1263, previously 1264), i05 (12744, previously 12760), m01 (3380, previously 3384), m03 (6692, previously 6697) and m04 (3315, previously 3318). All five solutions were checked with the official IHTP validator and carry no hard-constraint violations. The solutions are not built from scratch: each is derived from the corresponding solution published on the benchmark page, holding its admission days, room assignments and operating-theater assignments fixed, and reoptimizing only the nurse-to-room-and-shift layer to proven optimality. The improvements are small in magnitude, between 0.075% and 0.125%, but they carry a structural observation: on the instances where the nurse layer can be closed exactly, it closes within seconds on all but one of them, and several of the published state-of-the-art solutions fall short of optimality in that layer.

</details>


### [167] [Predictor-Based Exponential Tracking of Turbulent Solutions for the Kuramoto--Sivashinsky Equation with Input Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00048)
*Amadou Cissé*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对带常数输入时滞的非线性Kuramoto–Sivashinsky方程，研究非平稳参考轨迹的一致指数跟踪问题；通过预测器–反步变换补偿时滞，证明全局适定性与一致指数稳定性，并给出数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考轨迹取自全局吸引子中的完整轨迹族，不要求其平稳、周期、慢变或由有限维外系统生成；在输入时滞存在时，未补偿反馈会放大跟踪误差，因此需要一种能在无初始时刻、无参考轨迹先验知识下实现一致指数跟踪的方法。

Method: 将时滞输入用一阶输运方程表示，并与四阶跟踪误差动力学耦合；采用预测器–反步变换，把增广闭环系统映射为无时滞的名义误差动力学，误差由齐次输运子系统的出射迹驱动，该子系统在一个时滞区间后消失；利用一致吸引子界，使反馈参数与稳定性常数可独立于初始时刻和参考轨迹选取。

Result: 在增广状态空间中建立了全局适定性与一致指数稳定性；数值结果表明，在所考虑配置下未补偿的时滞反馈会放大跟踪误差，而预测器补偿可恢复持续衰减；预测器一致性、有限时间消逝和离散化细化诊断支持了数值实现。

Conclusion: 预测器–反步补偿能够有效补偿命令生成与执行之间的时间失配，实现对非平稳轨迹的一致指数跟踪，且稳定性保证不依赖于初始时刻与特定参考轨迹。

Abstract: Uniform exponential tracking is addressed for nonstationary trajectories of the nonlinear Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equation subject to a constant input delay. The reference belongs to a family of complete trajectories contained in the global attractor and need not be stationary, periodic, slowly varying, or generated by a finite-dimensional exosystem. The delayed input is represented by a first-order transport equation coupled with the fourth-order tracking-error dynamics. A predictor--backstepping transformation compensates for the temporal mismatch between command generation and actuation by mapping the augmented closed-loop system into the nominal delay-free error dynamics driven by the outgoing trace of a homogeneous transport subsystem. This subsystem vanishes after one delay interval. Uniform attractor bounds permit the feedback parameters and stability constants to be selected independently of the initial time and the reference trajectory. Global well-posedness and uniform exponential stability are established in the augmented state space. Numerical results show that, for the considered configuration, uncompensated delayed feedback amplifies the tracking error, whereas predictor compensation restores sustained decay. Predictor-consistency, finite-time-extinction, and discretization-refinement diagnostics support the numerical implementation.

</details>


### [168] [New Pareto Z-eigenvalue localization theorems for tensors with applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00113)
*Licai Shao,Xingong Shao,Yisheng Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为张量特征值互补问题中的Pareto Z-特征值建立了新的定位集，厘清了已有定位集之间的关系，并将其应用于TCP与TGEiCP解的存在性判定及一般标量势真空稳定性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量特征值互补问题的Pareto Z-特征值定位集已有若干结果，但缺乏系统性比较，且需要更有效的定位工具来推导互补问题的可解性条件并拓展到物理稳定性判定等应用场景。

Method: 构造Pareto Z-特征值的新型定位集（localization sets），分析其与现有定位集之间的包含或等价关系；在此基础上推导张量互补问题（TCP）与张量广义特征值互补问题（TGEiCP）解存在性的充分条件，并将该条件用于检验一般标量势的真空稳定性。

Result: 给出了若干新的Pareto Z-特征值定位集，明确了它们与已有定位集的关系；获得TCP与TGEiCP解存在性的充分条件；该条件可成功用于一般标量势真空稳定性的判定。

Conclusion: 新定位集推广并统一了已有结果，为张量互补问题的可解性提供了新的充分条件，并展示了该理论工具在真空稳定性分析中的可用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish some new localization sets for Pareto Z-eigenvalues of tensor eigenvalue complementarity problems and discuss the relationships among existing ones. As applications, a sufficient condition for the solution existence of the tensor complementarity problem (TCP) and the tensor generalized eigenvalue complementarity problem (TGEiCP) are presented. In addition, this condition is also used to test the vacuum stability of a general scalar potential

</details>


### [169] [Control of 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems: a brief survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00139)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Jean-Michel Coron七十华诞的综述文章，回顾一维一阶线性双曲系统控制理论的相关文献，梳理其在该领域的代表性贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借Jean-Michel Coron七十寿辰之际，对其作出重大贡献的一维一阶线性双曲系统控制方向进行系统性文献回顾，梳理该领域的发展脉络与核心成果。

Method: 综述性文献回顾方法，围绕一维一阶线性双曲系统控制的理论进展进行归纳与评述。

Result: 整合并呈现了该方向的研究成果，突出Coron在该领域的开创性与代表性工作。

Conclusion: 该综述为研究者提供了该领域的整体图景，并凸显了Coron在一维一阶线性双曲系统控制中的重要学术地位。

Abstract: At the occasion of the 70th birthday anniversary of Jean-Michel Coron, we review the literature on the control of 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems, a topic where he has made significant contributions.

</details>


### [170] [Minimal control time of some 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems with row rank deficient boundary coupling matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00140)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive,Zengzhi Xin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类边界耦合矩阵行秩亏缺的一维一阶线性双曲系统的最小控制时间问题，刻画了其最小控制时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献已充分刻画边界耦合矩阵满行秩情形下一维一阶线性双曲系统的最小控制时间，但行秩亏缺情形尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对边界耦合矩阵行秩亏缺的一类系统，分析其边界条件与特征线传播特性，从而刻画最小控制时间。

Result: 给出了该类行秩亏缺边界耦合矩阵系统的最小控制时间的明确刻画。

Conclusion: 将最小控制时间的经典结论由满行秩情形推广到一类行秩亏缺情形。

Abstract: The minimal control time of one-dimensional first-order linear hyperbolic systems is by now well-known when the boundary coupling matrix is full row rank. The goal of this article is to characterize the minimal control time for a class of systems with row rank deficient boundary coupling matrices.

</details>


### [171] [Sharpness and Rigidity of the Two-Factor Recht-Re Lower Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00155)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Lai 与 Lim 在 ICML 2020 提出的半正定矩阵乘积和不等式常数的紧致性猜想，给出达到该下界的显式构造，并完整刻画了所有等号成立的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机优化方法中，连续迭代使用不同数据点会产生半正定矩阵的乘积；即使每个矩阵均半正定，其平均仍可能出现负特征值。Lai 与 Lim（ICML 2020）证明了 $\sum_{i\ne j}A_iA_j\succeq-\frac{1}{4}n(n-1)I$（当 $A_i\succeq0$ 且 $\sum_i A_i\preceq nI$），并猜想该常数对所有 $n\ge2$ 都是最优的，但该紧致性猜想此前未被证明。

Method: 作者构造了一个显式的 $n$ 维空间中 $n$ 个秩一矩阵族：将某组标准正交基的一个方向压缩因子 $1/\sqrt{2}$，从而得到达到下界的例子。此外，给出了该已知不等式的一个简短自洽证明，并对所有等号成立情形进行分类：每个极值矩阵都包含一个与上述构造等价的 $n$ 维公共不变块。

Result: 所构造的秩一矩阵族对每个 $n\ge2$ 均达到下界 $-\frac{1}{4}n(n-1)I$，证明了猜想中的紧致性；分类结果表明等号成立要求存在等价的 $n$ 维公共不变块，因此维度 $n$ 是等号成立的必要条件，而正定矩阵只能逼近、永远无法达到该下端点。最后，强凸二次目标可将该极端负特征值实现为两步期望梯度下降更新。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了 Lai–Lim 常数紧致性猜想，明确了不等式的最优性及其等号成立的结构条件，并建立了该代数现象与随机优化中梯度下降动态之间的直接联系。

Abstract: Products of positive semidefinite matrices arise when successive steps of a randomized optimization method use different data points. Their average can have a negative eigenvalue even though every individual matrix is positive semidefinite. Lai and Lim proved at ICML 2020 that $\sum_{i\ne j}A_iA_j\succeq-\frac{1}{4}n(n-1)I$ whenever $A_i\succeq0$ and $\sum_i A_i\preceq nI$, and conjectured that the constant is sharp for every $n\ge2$. We give an explicit family of $n$ rank-one matrices in dimension $n$ that attains this bound for every $n$, proving that sharpness statement. The construction compresses one direction of an orthonormal basis by a factor $1/\sqrt{2}$. We also give a short, self-contained proof of the known inequality and classify all equality cases: every extremizer contains a common invariant $n$-dimensional block equivalent to the construction. Consequently, dimension $n$ is necessary for equality, and positive definite matrices approach but never attain the lower endpoint. Finally, strongly convex quadratic objectives realize the extremal negative eigenvalue as a two-step expected gradient-descent update.

</details>


### [172] [A Two-Dimensional Extremizer for Relatively Inexact Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00156)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文给出了梯度下降单步在相对梯度误差下的显式极值函数（一坐标为二次函数、另一坐标为Huber函数之和），证明了Vernimmen与Glineur于2025年6月提出的Conjecture 2.6，并证明了中间区间内二维构造的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究梯度误差相对于真实梯度有界时，梯度下降单步的最坏情况性能，旨在验证Vernimmen与Glineur提出的猜想（Conjecture 2.6），并给出显式极值构造。

Method: 构造显式极值函数：一个坐标为二次函数、另一坐标为Huber函数之和；通过变量替换将参数描述化简为具有严格指定根的标量三次方程；证明全部参数不等式，给出上界的非负平方证书，并直接验证匹配构造。

Result: 该函数对每个可容许相对误差水平和中间区间内的每个步长，同时达到最终平方梯度除以初始目标间隙与除以目标下降量两个最坏情况常数；并刻画了一维精确最坏值，证明在开放中间区间内二维构造是必要的。

Conclusion: 证实了Conjecture 2.6，给出了梯度误差下梯度下降单步的显式极值函数与匹配的上界证书，并明确了中间区间中二维性的必要性。

Abstract: We give an explicit extremal function for one step of gradient descent when the gradient error is bounded relative to the true gradient. The function is the sum of a quadratic in one coordinate and a Huber function in the other. It attains, for every admissible relative error level and every stepsize in the intermediate regime, the proposed worst-case constants for both the final squared gradient divided by the initial objective gap and the final squared gradient divided by the objective decrease. This proves Conjecture 2.6 of Vernimmen and Glineur, first stated in June 2025. A change of variable reduces the parameter description to a scalar cubic with a rigorously specified root. We prove all parameter inequalities, derive a nonnegative-square certificate for the upper bound, and verify the matching construction directly. We also characterize the exact one-dimensional worst-case value and prove that two dimensions are necessary in the open intermediate regime.

</details>


### [173] [Global Output Lifting and Surjectivity in Invertible Nonlinear MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00160)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在全局正则修正逆、固定行选择及满输入秩有限终止条件下，任一固定齐次线性输出赋值都给出满射的输出导数映射，并具有光滑全局截面；所需状态界条件严格弱于弱一致零状态可检测性，从而将全局逆途径中单独要求的满射性化为可检测性的推论，并可构造光滑动态控制器实现任意光滑输出的精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局逆问题中，一个目标输出导数取值是否能由某个系统状态实现（即输出导数映射的满射性）通常被单独要求。本文旨在证明该满射性并非独立假设，而可由更弱的可检测性类条件推出，从而简化全局逆用于镇定的理论框架。

Method: 在全局正则修正逆（固定行选择、满输入秩下有限终止）假设下，引入每个有限区间上的状态界条件，即状态可由初始状态与有限多个输出导数一致估计（对输入一致成立）。基于相容光滑参考的完全提升方法，构造输出导数映射的光滑全局截面；对已知系统，用全状态反馈与当前参考导数构造光滑动态控制器，并以系统状态初始化。

Result: 证明在所述条件下：1）任一固定齐次线性输出赋值对应的输出导数映射是满射的，且存在光滑全局截面；2）所需状态界条件严格弱于弱一致零状态可检测性；3）截面可为每个导数目标光滑地选取实现状态，即使存在内部动态；4）已知系统情形下，提升方法直接给出光滑动态控制器，可从任意初始状态出发，在任意指定正时间后实现任意光滑期望输出的精确跟踪。

Conclusion: 全局逆途径中对满射性的额外要求，在给定的逆假设下可由（更弱的）可检测性条件导出；所提出的完全提升构造同时给出光滑全局截面与光滑动态跟踪控制器。

Abstract: Global inversion of nonlinear multivariable systems raises the question of whether every target value of the selected output derivatives can be realized by a plant state. We prove that, under globally regular modified inversion with fixed row selection and finite termination at full input rank, every fixed homogeneous linear output assignment yields a surjective output-derivative map with a smooth global section. The result requires a state bound on each finite interval in terms of the initial state and finitely many output derivatives, uniformly over inputs. This condition is strictly weaker than weak uniform zero-state detectability, making the separate surjectivity requirement in a global inversion approach to stabilization a consequence of detectability under the stated inversion hypotheses. The section selects a realizing state smoothly for every derivative target, including in the presence of internal dynamics, and is constructed through complete lifting of compatible smooth references. For known plants, the same lifting method yields a smooth dynamic controller using full state feedback and current reference derivatives, with initialization from the plant state. From any initial state, it achieves exact tracking of any smooth desired output after any prescribed positive time.

</details>


### [174] [Reachability and exact controllability of linear Stieltjes differential systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00161)
*Francisco J. Fernández*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究由非减左连续Stieltjes导子驱动系统的可达性与精确可控性，导子测度可含连续、奇异连续与原子部分，平坦区间表示不活动。构造演化族与跳后变常数公式，以输入方向的本质张成刻画可达子空间；常系数下Kalman条件对任意导子必要、在回归性下对子区间上连续且非常值的导子充分；纯原子测度含N个正原子时可达子空间为B,…,A^{N-1}B生成的截断Krylov空间，Kalman可控对的最小原子数等于可控性指标；阈值处Gramian退化与能量爆炸，阈值及以上紧正质量范围给出一致矫顽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性系统可达性/精确可控性理论依赖常微分方程与标准积分，而含有连续、奇异连续及原子混合测度并伴平坦（不活动）区间的Stieltjes导子驱动系统缺乏统一的演化族、变常数公式与可达子空间刻画，尤其是原子数、可控性指标与能量代价之间的定量关系不明确。

Method: 构造演化族与跳后变常数公式（post-jump variation-of-constants）；将可达子空间刻画为输入方向的本质张成（essential span）；对常系数情形给出Kalman条件的必要性与在回归性（regressivity）及导子于子区间连续非常值下的充分性；针对纯原子测度（N个正原子）推导可达子空间为截断Krylov空间span{B,AB,…,A^{N-1}B}；建立最小原子数与可控性指标的等价关系；通过Gramian与紧正质量范围分析阈值处的退化与能量行为，并以混合双积分器算例验证。

Result: 可达子空间等于输入方向的本质张成；常系数下Kalman条件必要，回归性且导子连续非常值时充分；纯原子可微导子（N个正原子）的可达子空间为截断Krylov空间，Kalman可控对的最小原子数等于可控性指标；阈值原子数时一个质量消失、其余收敛到正有限极限会导致Gramian退化与最坏情形能量爆炸；在阈值及以上，紧正质量范围提供一致矫顽性；混合双积分器例子验证结论。

Conclusion: 该工作将可达性与精确可控性理论推广到含一般Stieltjes测度（连续、奇异连续、原子）与平坦区间的系统，揭示了本质张成、Krylov截断、原子数与可控性指标及能量矫顽性/爆炸之间的精确联系，为具有不活动区间的广义线性系统可控性分析提供框架。

Abstract: We study reachability and exact controllability for systems driven by a nondecreasing left-continuous Stieltjes derivator whose measure may have continuous, singular-continuous and atomic parts, while flat intervals represent inactivity. We construct an evolution family and post-jump variation-of-constants formula, and characterize the reachable subspace as the essential span of input directions. For constant coefficients, the Kalman condition is necessary for every derivator and, under regressivity, sufficient when the derivator is continuous and nonconstant on a subinterval. If the measure is purely atomic with exactly $N$ positive atoms, the reachable subspace is the truncated Krylov space generated by $B,AB,\ldots,A^{N-1}B$; for a Kalman-controllable pair, the minimum atom count among purely atomic derivators equals the controllability index. At the threshold count, one vanishing mass with the others converging to positive finite limits causes Gramian degeneration and worst-case energy blow-up; at or above threshold, compact positive mass ranges yield uniform coercivity. A mixed double-integrator example illustrates the results.

</details>


### [175] [Certificate-Coupled DCA for the Trust-Region Subproblem: Early Structural Escape and Progressive Global Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00172)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种证书耦合的DC算法求解非凸信赖域子问题，利用持久化随机Krylov子空间在达到一阶稳定前引入谱信息，证明全局收敛与复杂度界，实验显示Krylov复用使谱矩阵-向量乘积中位数减少约2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸信赖域子问题的硬情形（近正交、负曲率）中，传统方法需先达到精确一阶稳定点才做谱校正，效率低；希望在不依赖精确稳定性的前提下提前利用谱信息实现负曲率修正与全局最优保证。

Method: 证书耦合的差分凸（DCA）算法：维护持久化随机Krylov子空间与DCA迭代同步；近稳定时用充分负的移位Rayleigh商构造显式可行修正并保证目标值定量下降；否则完成预定Lanczos深度以高概率给出近似半正定证书；同一子空间给出最大特征值上界用于选择DC曲率参数；采用渐进精度调度，Krylov子空间跨修正与精度阶段复用。

Result: 证明信赖域上一致的负曲率修正结果（覆盖近正交硬情形），给出固定容差下的有限终止与工作量界，以及定量目标间隙证书；渐进精度下证书目标值收敛到全局最优、迭代点距离收敛到全局解集；数值实验验证负曲率修正可先于后收敛谱修正发生、所有近正交实例满足定量下降界、Krylov复用使谱矩阵-向量乘积中位数减少约2.6倍。

Conclusion: 谱信息与DCA迭代的证书耦合及Krylov子空间持久化，可在非凸信赖域子问题上兼顾全局收敛性、复杂度保证与计算效率。

Abstract: We study the nonconvex trust-region subproblem by a certificate-coupled difference-of-convex algorithm that incorporates spectral information before exact first-order stationarity is attained. The method maintains a persistent randomized Krylov subspace together with the DCA iterates. Near stationarity, a sufficiently negative shifted Rayleigh quotient yields an explicit feasible correction with a quantitative decrease in the objective value; otherwise, completion of a prescribed Lanczos depth provides, with high probability, an approximate positive-semidefiniteness certificate. The same Krylov subspace also provides a certified upper bound on the largest eigenvalue for selecting the DC curvature parameter at subsequent accuracy stages. We prove a uniform negative-curvature correction result over the trust region, covering the near-orthogonal hard-case regime, together with finite-termination and work bounds at fixed tolerances and a quantitative objective-gap certificate. Under a progressive accuracy schedule, the certified objective values converge to the global optimum and the iterates converge in distance to the global solution set, while the Krylov subspace is retained across corrections and accuracy stages. Numerical experiments illustrate the phenomena established by the analysis: negative-curvature corrections can occur before the corresponding post-convergence spectral corrections, the quantitative decrease bound is satisfied in all tested near-orthogonal instances, and retaining the Krylov subspace reduces the median number of spectral matrix-vector products by approximately a factor of $2.6$ relative to restarting the spectral process in the controlled comparison.

</details>
